/* ================= FINAL PRODUCTION PATCH ================= */
let selectedMessageIds=new Set();
let profileUnsubs={};
let previousPageBeforeCall='';
let callPageLocked=false;

function photoMarkup(url,initial,cls='avatar'){
  return url?`<div class="${cls}"><img src="${escapeHtml(url)}" alt=""></div>`:`<div class="${cls} green">${escapeHtml(initial||'U')}</div>`;
}
function refreshChatProfile(c,data){
  if(!c||c.group)return;
  c.name=data.displayName||data.firstName||data.username||'User';
  c.username=data.username||c.username||'';
  c.initial=(c.name[0]||'U').toUpperCase();
  c.photoURL=data.photoURL||'';
  c.peerId=data.peerId||'';
  c.recipientPeerId=data.peerId||'';
  c.online=c.online||false;
  if(current===c){
    const hn=document.getElementById('headName'); if(hn)hn.textContent=c.name;
    const ha=document.getElementById('headAvatar'); if(ha){ha.innerHTML=c.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(c.photoURL)}" alt="">${c.online?'<span class="dot"></span>':''}`:`${escapeHtml(c.initial)}${c.online?'<span class="dot"></span>':''}`;}
  }
}
function attachProfileListener(c){
  if(!c||c.group||!c.otherUid||profileUnsubs[c.otherUid]||!fbDb)return;
  profileUnsubs[c.otherUid]=fbDb.collection('users').doc(c.otherUid).onSnapshot(s=>{if(!s.exists)return;refreshChatProfile(c,s.data()||{});renderChats();if(current===c)renderMessages();},e=>console.warn('profile listener',e));
}

async function loadFirebaseChats(){
  if(!fbUser||!fbDb)return;
  try{
    const snap=await fbDb.collection('chats').where('memberIds','array-contains',fbUser.uid).get();
    const remote=[];
    for(const d of snap.docs){
      const x=d.data()||{};
      const memberIds=Array.isArray(x.memberIds)?x.memberIds:[];
      if(x.type==='group'){
        const name=x.name||'Group';
        remote.push({id:d.id,firestoreId:d.id,name,initial:(name[0]||'G').toUpperCase(),preview:x.lastMessage||'No messages yet',time:kpTime(x.lastMessageAt),sortTime:kpMillis(x.lastMessageAt)||kpMillis(x.createdAt)||0,online:false,unread:Number(x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0),messageCount:Number(x.messageCount||0),color:'green',group:true,groupType:x.groupType||'private',avatar:x.avatar||'',avatarType:x.avatarType||'initial',about:x.about||'',adminIds:x.adminIds||[],memberIds,messages:[],startedAt:kpTime(x.createdAt)||'Today'});
      }else{
        const other=memberIds.find(id=>id!==fbUser.uid); if(!other)continue;
        const us=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(other).get();
        const u=us.exists?(us.data()||{}):{};
        let online=false; try{const ps=await fbRtdb.ref('presence/'+other).once('value');online=ps.val()?.state==='online';}catch(e){}
        const name=u.displayName||u.firstName||u.username||'User';
        const c={id:d.id,firestoreId:d.id,name,username:u.username||'',initial:(name[0]||'U').toUpperCase(),preview:x.lastMessage||'No messages yet',time:kpTime(x.lastMessageAt),sortTime:kpMillis(x.lastMessageAt)||kpMillis(x.createdAt)||0,online,unread:Number(x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0),color:'green',group:false,messages:[],peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:other,photoURL:u.photoURL||'',startedAt:kpTime(x.createdAt)||'Today'};
        remote.push(c); attachProfileListener(c);
      }
    }
    remote.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));
    chats.splice(0,chats.length,...remote);
    renderChats();
  }catch(e){console.error('loadFirebaseChats',e);toast('Could not load chats: '+(e.code||e.message||'Firestore error'));}
}

function renderChats(){
  const list=document.getElementById('chatList'); if(!list)return;
  const q=(document.getElementById('search')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
  const arr=chats.filter(c=>{
    const tab=activeTab==='all'||(activeTab==='unread'&&c.unread)||(activeTab==='groups'&&c.group);
    const hay=((c.name||'')+' '+(c.username||'')).toLowerCase();
    return tab&&hay.includes(q);
  }).sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));
  list.innerHTML=arr.map(c=>{
    const av=c.avatar||c.photoURL?`<div class="avatar green"><img src="${escapeHtml(c.avatar||c.photoURL)}" alt=""></div>`:`<div class="avatar green">${escapeHtml(c.initial||'U')}</div>`;
    const badge=c.unread?`<span class="unread">${c.unread>99?'99+':c.unread}</span>`:'';
    const count=c.group&&c.messageCount?`<span class="unread">${c.messageCount>99?'99+':c.messageCount}</span>`:'';
    return `<div class="chatRow ${current?.id===c.id?'selected':''}" data-chat-id="${escapeHtml(String(c.id))}" onclick="openChat('${String(c.id).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">${av}<div class="chatMeta"><div class="chatName"><b>${escapeHtml(c.name||'User')}</b>${c.username&&!c.group?`<small>@${escapeHtml(c.username)}</small>`:''}</div><div class="chatPreview">${escapeHtml(c.preview||'No messages yet')}</div></div><div class="chatSide"><small>${escapeHtml(c.time||'')}</small>${badge}${count}</div></div>`;
  }).join('')||'<div class="empty"><div><strong>No chats found</strong><br>Tap + to start a new chat.</div></div>';
}

async function startChatWithUser(uid){
  if(!fbUser||!uid||uid===fbUser.uid)return;
  try{
    const us=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid).get();
    if(!us.exists)throw new Error('User not found');
    const u=us.data()||{};
    const chatId=await ensurePrivateChat(uid,u.displayName||u.username||'User',u.peerId||'');
    let c=chats.find(x=>x.firestoreId===chatId||x.id===chatId);
    if(!c){c={id:chatId,firestoreId:chatId,name:u.displayName||u.username||'User',username:u.username||'',initial:(u.displayName||u.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase(),preview:'No messages yet',time:'',sortTime:Date.now(),online:false,unread:0,color:'green',group:false,messages:[],peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:uid,photoURL:u.photoURL||'',startedAt:'Today'};chats.unshift(c);}else refreshChatProfile(c,u);
    attachProfileListener(c); current=c; closePage(); closeModal(); document.getElementById('app').classList.add('chat-open');
    await loadFirebaseMessages(c); startFirebaseMessageListener(c); await markChatRead(c); renderChats(); renderMessages();
  }catch(e){console.error('startChatWithUser',e);toast('Could not open this chat: '+(e.code||e.message||'Firestore error'));}
}

async function openChat(id){
  const c=chats.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id)); if(!c)return;
  current=c; c.unread=0; document.getElementById('app').classList.add('chat-open'); renderChats();
  try{await loadFirebaseMessages(c);startFirebaseMessageListener(c);await markChatRead(c);renderMessages();}
  catch(e){console.error('openChat',e);toast('Could not open this chat: '+(e.code||e.message||'Firestore error'));}
}

function updateHeadAvatar(){
  const ha=document.getElementById('headAvatar');if(!ha||!current)return;
  if(current.avatar||current.photoURL){ha.innerHTML=`<img src="${escapeHtml(current.avatar||current.photoURL)}" alt="">${current.online&&!current.group?'<span class="dot"></span>':''}`;}
  else {ha.innerHTML=`${escapeHtml(current.initial||'U')}${current.online&&!current.group?'<span class="dot"></span>':''}`;}
}

function renderMessages(){
  const list=current?.messages||[], box=document.getElementById('messages'); if(!box)return;
  const html=list.map((m,idx)=>{
    const kind=m[0],isMe=['me','me-gif','me-media'].includes(kind),state=m[4]||(isMe?'read':null),docId=m[5]?.id||'',raw=m[5]?.raw||{},selected=selectedMessageIds.has(docId);
    if(kind==='system')return `<div class="systemMessage"><span>${escapeHtml(m[1]||'')}</span></div>`;
    let payload={},content='',caption='',reply='';
    if(kind==='gif'||kind==='me-gif'){try{payload=typeof m[1]==='string'&&m[1].startsWith('{')?JSON.parse(m[1]):{url:m[1]};}catch(e){payload={url:m[1]};}caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||'';content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" loading="lazy" alt="GIF"></div>`;}
    else if(kind==='media'||kind==='me-media'){try{payload=JSON.parse(m[1]);}catch(e){payload={};}caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||'';if((payload.type||'').startsWith('image/'))content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" loading="lazy" alt="${escapeHtml(payload.name||'image')}"></div>`;else if((payload.type||'').startsWith('audio/'))content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><audio controls src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}"></audio></div>`;else content=`<div class="fileCard"><span>📎</span><a href="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${escapeHtml(payload.name||'File')}</a></div>`;}
    else{content=`<div class="txt">${escapeHtml(m[1]||'')}</div>`;reply=m[3]||'';}
    const quote=reply?`<div class="replyQuote">↩ ${escapeHtml(reply)}</div>`:'';const cap=caption?`<div class="msgMediaCaption">${escapeHtml(caption)}</div>`:'';const status=isMe?deliveryIcon(state):'';
    return `<div class="msg ${isMe?'me':'in'} ${kind.includes('media')||kind.includes('gif')?'fileMsg':''} ${selected?'msgLongSelected':''}" data-message-id="${escapeHtml(docId)}" data-message-index="${idx}" oncontextmenu="event.preventDefault();selectMessage('${String(docId).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}',${idx})">${quote}${content}${cap}<div class="meta">${escapeHtml(m[2]||'')} ${status}</div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  box.innerHTML='<div class="love-fx" id="loveFx"></div><div class="chatStarted">Chat started · '+escapeHtml(current?.startedAt||'Today')+'</div><div class="day">TODAY</div>'+html;
  updateHeadAvatar();const hn=document.getElementById('headName'),hs=document.getElementById('headStatus');if(hn)hn.textContent=current?.name||'';if(hs)hs.textContent=current?.group?`${(current.memberIds||[]).length} members`:(current?.online?'online':'last seen recently');
  bindLongPressMessages(); box.scrollTop=box.scrollHeight;
}
function bindLongPressMessages(){
  const box=document.getElementById('messages');if(!box)return;
  box.querySelectorAll('.msg[data-message-id]').forEach(el=>{
    let timer=null,moved=false;
    const start=()=>{moved=false;timer=setTimeout(()=>{const id=el.dataset.messageId;const idx=Number(el.dataset.messageIndex);selectMessage(id,idx);},520)};
    const cancel=()=>{if(timer){clearTimeout(timer);timer=null;}};
    el.addEventListener('touchstart',start,{passive:true});el.addEventListener('touchmove',()=>{moved=true;cancel()},{passive:true});el.addEventListener('touchend',cancel);el.addEventListener('touchcancel',cancel);el.addEventListener('mousedown',start);el.addEventListener('mouseup',cancel);el.addEventListener('mouseleave',cancel);
  });
}
function selectMessage(id,idx){if(!id)return;selectedMessageIds.add(id);renderMessages();const bar=document.getElementById('messageSelectBar');bar?.classList.add('show');const count=document.getElementById('messageSelectCount');if(count)count.textContent=`${selectedMessageIds.size} selected`;}
function cancelMessageSelection(){selectedMessageIds.clear();document.getElementById('messageSelectBar')?.classList.remove('show');renderMessages();}
async function deleteSelectedMessages(){
  if(!current?.firestoreId||!fbUser||!selectedMessageIds.size)return;
  const ids=[...selectedMessageIds]; if(!confirm(ids.length===1?'Delete this message?':`Delete ${ids.length} messages?`))return;
  try{const batch=fbDb.batch();ids.forEach(id=>batch.delete(fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId).collection('messages').doc(id)));await batch.commit();cancelMessageSelection();toast('Message deleted');}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not delete message');}
}
async function deleteMessage(messageId,idx){selectMessage(messageId,idx);}

/* Keep media/GIF compact and prevent local fake message simulation. */
async function sendPendingGif(){if(!pendingGifUrl||!current)return;const url=pendingGifUrl,caption=document.getElementById('gifCaption')?.value.trim()||'',reply=document.getElementById('gifReply')?.value.trim()||'';try{await persistFirestoreMessage('gif',{url},'',caption,reply);pendingGifUrl=null;closeModal();toast('GIF sent');}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('GIF could not be sent');}}

/* Firebase-backed groups using real users only. */
async function openGroupMembersStep(){
  const name=document.getElementById('groupName')?.value.trim();if(!name){toast('Enter a group name');return}
  groupDraft={name,about:document.getElementById('groupAbout')?.value.trim()||'',type:groupType||'public',avatar:groupAvatarUrl||''};selectedGroupMembers=[];
  if(!realUsers?.length)await loadRealUsers();
  const s=document.getElementById('sheet');const rows=(realUsers||[]).map(u=>`<label class="memberRow"><input type="checkbox" value="${escapeHtml(u.uid)}" onchange="toggleGroupMember('${String(u.uid).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}',this.checked)">${u.photoURL?`<div class="miniAvatar"><img src="${escapeHtml(u.photoURL)}" alt=""></div>`:`<div class="miniAvatar">${escapeHtml((u.displayName||u.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase())}</div>`}<div style="flex:1"><b>${escapeHtml(u.displayName||u.username||'User')}</b><small>@${escapeHtml(u.username||'')}</small></div></label>`).join('');
  s.innerHTML=`<div class="handle"></div><h3>Select members</h3><div class="gifHint">Only real Firebase users are shown.</div><div class="memberList">${rows||'<div style="padding:15px;color:#777">No other users found.</div>'}</div><button class="primary" style="margin-top:10px" onclick="finishCreateGroup()">Create group</button><button class="secondary" onclick="openModal('group')">Back</button>`;
}
function toggleGroupMember(id,on){if(on&&!selectedGroupMembers.includes(id))selectedGroupMembers.push(id);if(!on)selectedGroupMembers=selectedGroupMembers.filter(x=>x!==id)}
async function finishCreateGroup(){
  if(!fbUser||!fbDb)return toast('Please sign in first');
  const name=(groupDraft.name||'New Group').trim();const about=(groupDraft.about||'').trim();const type=groupDraft.type||'private';let avatar=groupDraft.avatar||groupAvatarUrl||'';
  try{
    if(avatar.startsWith('data:')){const blob=await (await fetch(avatar)).blob();const f=new File([blob],'group-avatar.jpg',{type:blob.type||'image/jpeg'});avatar=(await cloudinaryUpload(f)).secure_url;}
    const members=[...new Set([fbUser.uid,...selectedGroupMembers.filter(x=>x&&x!==fbUser.uid)])];
    const ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(),ts=KPServerTS(),unread={};members.forEach(id=>unread[id]=0);const welcome=`Welcome to ${name}! This group was created successfully.`;
    await ref.set({type:'group',name,about,groupType:type,avatar,avatarType:avatar?'gif':'initial',memberIds:members,adminIds:[fbUser.uid],createdBy:fbUser.uid,createdAt:ts,lastMessage:welcome,lastMessageAt:ts,lastMessageSenderId:fbUser.uid,messageCount:1,unreadCount:unread});
    const mref=ref.collection('messages').doc();await mref.set({senderId:fbUser.uid,type:'system',text:welcome,createdAt:ts,edited:false,deleted:false,readBy:[fbUser.uid],deliveredTo:members});
    const c={id:ref.id,firestoreId:ref.id,name,initial:(name[0]||'G').toUpperCase(),preview:welcome,time:new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}),sortTime:Date.now(),online:false,unread:0,messageCount:1,color:'green',group:true,groupType:type,avatar,avatarType:avatar?'gif':'initial',about,adminIds:[fbUser.uid],memberIds:members,messages:[['system',welcome,new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}),'','',{id:mref.id,raw:{senderId:fbUser.uid,type:'system'}}]],startedAt:'Today'};
    chats.unshift(c);current=c;selectedGroupMembers=[];groupAvatarUrl='';groupDraft={name:'',about:'',type:'private',avatar:''};closeModal();renderChats();document.getElementById('app').classList.add('chat-open');startFirebaseMessageListener(c);renderMessages();toast('Group created successfully');
  }catch(e){console.error('group create',e);toast('Group creation failed: '+(e.code||e.message||'Firestore error'));}
}

/* Incoming/outgoing call is a full-screen page; while active, navigation is locked. */
function showCallModal(type,name,initial){
  const o=document.getElementById('callOverlay');if(!o)return;previousPageBeforeCall=document.getElementById('pageView')?.classList.contains('show')?'page':'chat';callPageLocked=true;o.classList.add('show');o.classList.toggle('incoming',type==='incoming');
  const card=o.querySelector('.callCard');if(card&&!card.querySelector('.callOrb'))card.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin','<div class="callOrb"><div class="callOrbCore"></div></div>');
  const av=document.getElementById('callAvatar');if(av){av.textContent=initial||'A';av.style.display='grid';}
  document.getElementById('callTitle').textContent=name||'Voice call';document.getElementById('callPeer').textContent='';document.getElementById('callTimer').textContent='';
  const sub=document.getElementById('callSub'),a=document.getElementById('callActions');
  if(type==='incoming'){sub.textContent='Incoming voice call';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction accept" onclick="acceptIncomingCall()" aria-label="Answer"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 4.5 9 4l2 4-2 2a13 13 0 0 0 5 5l2-2 4 2-.5 3a2 2 0 0 1-2.2 1.5C9.5 18.2 5.8 14.5 4.5 6.7A2 2 0 0 1 6 4.5Z"/></svg></button><button class="callAction end" onclick="rejectIncomingCall()" aria-label="Reject"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}
  else if(type==='outgoing'){sub.textContent='Calling…';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction end" onclick="endVoiceCall()" aria-label="End call"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}
  else{sub.textContent='Connected';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction" id="muteCallBtn" onclick="toggleMute()" aria-label="Mute"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 3a3 3 0 0 0-3 3v6a3 3 0 0 0 6 0V6a3 3 0 0 0-3-3Z"/><path d="M19 10v2a7 7 0 0 1-14 0v-2M12 19v3M8 22h8"/></svg></button><button class="callAction" onclick="appendCallNote()" aria-label="Notes"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 4h12v16H6zM9 8h6M9 12h6M9 16h4"/></svg></button><button class="callAction end" onclick="endVoiceCall()" aria-label="End call"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}
}
function hideCallModal(){document.getElementById('callOverlay')?.classList.remove('show');callPageLocked=false;}
function startVoiceCall(){
  if(!current||current.group)return toast('Voice calls are 1-on-1 only');const rid=current.peerId||current.recipientPeerId;if(!rid)return toast('This user is not ready for voice calling');if(!peer||!peer.open)return toast('Voice calling is still connecting…');
  showCallModal('outgoing',current.name,current.initial||'A');ensureLocalAudio().then(stream=>{localStream=stream;activeCall=peer.call(rid,stream,{metadata:{uid:fbUser.uid,name:fbUserProfile?.displayName||'KP User',username:fbUserProfile?.username||'',photoURL:fbUserProfile?.photoURL||'',peerId}});if(!activeCall)throw new Error('Call failed');bindCall(activeCall,current.name,current.initial||'A');}).catch(e=>{console.error(e);stopLocalStream();hideCallModal();toast('Could not start voice call')});
}
function handleIncomingCall(call){if(activeCall||incomingCall){try{call.close()}catch(e){}return;}incomingCall=call;const meta=call.metadata||{};const callerName=meta.name||meta.username||'Incoming caller';showCallModal('incoming',callerName,(callerName[0]||'U').toUpperCase());playRingtone();}
function acceptIncomingCall(){if(!incomingCall)return;stopRingtone();ensureLocalAudio().then(stream=>{localStream=stream;activeCall=incomingCall;incomingCall=null;bindCall(activeCall,activeCall.metadata?.name||'Voice call',(activeCall.metadata?.name||'A')[0].toUpperCase());activeCall.answer(stream);}).catch(e=>{console.error(e);rejectIncomingCall();toast('Microphone permission is required')});}
function endVoiceCall(sendClose=true){stopRingtone();if(activeCall&&sendClose)try{activeCall.close()}catch(e){}activeCall=null;incomingCall=null;stopLocalStream();const audio=document.getElementById('remoteAudio');if(audio)audio.srcObject=null;stopCallTimer();hideCallModal();}

/* Block navigation while a call is active; restore chat after end. */
const _navOriginal=window.nav;window.nav=function(btn,type){if(callPageLocked){toast('End the call before leaving the call screen');return}return _navOriginal? _navOriginal(btn,type):null;};
const _openPageOriginal=window.openPage;window.openPage=function(type,extra){if(callPageLocked){toast('End the call before opening another page');return}return _openPageOriginal? _openPageOriginal(type,extra):null;};

/* Live profile page uses the same DP everywhere. */
async function saveProfileEdits(){
  if(!fbUser||!fbDb)return;const f=document.getElementById('editProfileFirst')?.value.trim()||'',l=document.getElementById('editProfileLast')?.value.trim()||'',bio=document.getElementById('editProfileBio')?.value.trim()||'';const display=[f,l].filter(Boolean).join(' ')||fbUserProfile?.username||'KP User';
  try{await fbDb.collection('users').doc(fbUser.uid).set({firstName:f,lastName:l,displayName:display,bio,updatedAt:KPServerTS()},{merge:true});fbUserProfile={...(fbUserProfile||{}),firstName:f,lastName:l,displayName:display,bio};await loadFirebaseChats();openProfilePage();toast('Profile saved');}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not save profile');}
}
async function uploadProfilePhoto(file){if(!file||!fbUser)return;try{toast('Uploading photo…');const u=await cloudinaryUpload(file);await fbDb.collection('users').doc(fbUser.uid).set({photoURL:u.secure_url,avatarType:'photo',updatedAt:KPServerTS()},{merge:true});fbUserProfile={...(fbUserProfile||{}),photoURL:u.secure_url,avatarType:'photo'};await loadFirebaseChats();openProfileEdit();toast('Profile photo updated');}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Photo upload failed');}}
async function removeProfilePhoto(){if(!fbUser)return;try{await fbDb.collection('users').doc(fbUser.uid).set({photoURL:'',avatarType:'initial',updatedAt:KPServerTS()},{merge:true});fbUserProfile={...(fbUserProfile||{}),photoURL:'',avatarType:'initial'};await loadFirebaseChats();openProfileEdit();}catch(e){toast('Could not remove photo');}}

/* Username-only calls page. */
function openCallsPage(){
  const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');if(!pv||!pi)return;
  pi.innerHTML=`<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Calls</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Voice calls</div></div></div><div class="pageCard"><div class="callUserSearch"><input id="callUsernameInput" class="field" placeholder="Search username or name" oninput="searchCallUser(this.value)"></div><div id="callResult" class="callResult"></div></div><div class="pageCard"><b>Recent calls</b><div id="callHistory"><div style="color:#777;padding-top:10px">Loading…</div></div></div>`;pv.classList.add('show');loadCallHistory();}
let callSearchTimer=null;async function searchCallUser(q){clearTimeout(callSearchTimer);callSearchTimer=setTimeout(async()=>{const box=document.getElementById('callResult');q=(q||'').trim().toLowerCase();if(!box)return;if(!q){box.innerHTML='';return}try{const snap=await fbDb.collection('users').where('username','>=',q).where('username','<=',q+'\\uf8ff').limit(8).get();const users=snap.docs.map(d=>({uid:d.id,...d.data()})).filter(u=>u.uid!==fbUser.uid);box.innerHTML=users.map(u=>`<button class="userRow" style="width:100%;text-align:left" onclick="callUserByUid('${String(u.uid).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">${u.photoURL?`<div class="avatar"><img src="${escapeHtml(u.photoURL)}" alt=""></div>`:`<div class="avatar green">${escapeHtml((u.displayName||u.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase())}</div>`}<div class="rowmain"><b>${escapeHtml(u.displayName||u.username||'User')}</b><small style="display:block;color:#777">@${escapeHtml(u.username||'')}</small></div></button>`).join('')||'<div style="color:#777;padding:10px">No user found.</div>';}catch(e){console.error(e);box.innerHTML='<div style="color:#777;padding:10px">Search unavailable.</div>'; }},160);}
async function callUserByUid(uid){try{const us=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid).get();if(!us.exists)return;const u=us.data()||{};const id=await ensurePrivateChat(uid,u.displayName||u.username||'User',u.peerId||'');let c=chats.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!c){c={id,firestoreId:id,name:u.displayName||u.username||'User',username:u.username||'',initial:(u.displayName||u.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase(),otherUid:uid,peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',photoURL:u.photoURL||'',group:false,messages:[]};chats.unshift(c);}current=c;closePage();document.getElementById('app').classList.add('chat-open');await loadFirebaseMessages(c);startFirebaseMessageListener(c);startVoiceCall();}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not start call');}}

<script>

/* ================= PEERJS VOICE CALLS ================= */
let peer=null, peerId='', activeCall=null, localStream=null, incomingCall=null, callTimerInt=null, callStartedAt=0;
const PEER_PREFIX='kpwa_';
function getMyPeerId(){let id=localStorage.getItem('kpwa_peer_id'); if(!id){id=PEER_PREFIX+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,10);localStorage.setItem('kpwa_peer_id',id)} return id}
function initPeer(){
 if(typeof Peer==='undefined'){toast('PeerJS could not load');return}
 try{
   peer=new Peer(getMyPeerId(),{debug:1});
   peer.on('open',id=>{peerId=id; window.KP_PEER_ID=id;});
   peer.on('call',call=>handleIncomingCall(call));
   peer.on('error',err=>{console.warn('PeerJS',err); if(err&&err.type==='unavailable-id'){localStorage.removeItem('kpwa_peer_id');setTimeout(initPeer,500)}});
   peer.on('disconnected',()=>{try{peer.reconnect()}catch(e){}});
 }catch(e){console.error(e);}
}
function ensureLocalAudio(){return navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({audio:true,video:false})}
function getRecipientPeerId(){return current && (current.peerId || current.recipientPeerId || current.peerID) || ''}
function startVoiceCall(){
 if(!current || current.group){toast('Voice calls are 1-on-1 only');return}
 let rid=getRecipientPeerId();
 if(!rid){rid=(window.prompt('Enter the recipient\'s Peer ID to start the voice call:', '')||'').trim();if(!rid){toast('Recipient Peer ID is required');return}current.peerId=rid}
 if(!peer || !peer.open){toast('Voice calling is still connecting…');return}
 showCallModal('outgoing',current.name,current.initial||'A');
 ensureLocalAudio().then(stream=>{
   localStream=stream;
   activeCall=peer.call(rid,stream,{metadata:{name:'KP User',peerId}});
   if(!activeCall){throw new Error('Call could not be started')}
   bindCall(activeCall,current.name,current.initial||'A');
 }).catch(err=>{console.error(err);stopLocalStream();hideCallModal();toast(err.name==='NotAllowedError'?'Microphone permission was denied':'Could not start voice call')});
}
function handleIncomingCall(call){
 if(activeCall||incomingCall){try{call.close()}catch(e){}return}
 incomingCall=call;
 const meta=call.metadata||{};
 const callerName=meta.name||'Incoming caller';
 const chat=chats.find(c=>c.peerId===call.peer || c.recipientPeerId===call.peer);
 showCallModal('incoming',callerName,chat?.initial||callerName.charAt(0).toUpperCase());
 playRingtone();
}
function acceptIncomingCall(){
 if(!incomingCall)return;
 stopRingtone();
 ensureLocalAudio().then(stream=>{
   localStream=stream;
   activeCall=incomingCall;
   incomingCall=null;
   bindCall(activeCall,activeCall.metadata?.name||'Voice call','A');
   activeCall.answer(stream);
 }).catch(err=>{console.error(err);rejectIncomingCall();toast('Microphone permission is required')});
}
function rejectIncomingCall(){stopRingtone();if(incomingCall){try{incomingCall.close()}catch(e){}}incomingCall=null;hideCallModal()}
function bindCall(call,name,initial){
 call.on('stream',remoteStream=>{
   const audio=document.getElementById('remoteAudio');audio.srcObject=remoteStream;audio.play().catch(()=>{});
   showCallModal('active',name,initial);startCallTimer();
 });
 call.on('close',()=>endVoiceCall(false));
 call.on('error',err=>{console.warn('Call error',err);endVoiceCall(false);toast('Voice call ended')});
}
function toggleMute(){if(!localStream)return;const track=localStream.getAudioTracks()[0];if(!track)return;track.enabled=!track.enabled;const b=document.getElementById('muteCallBtn');if(b)b.innerHTML=track.enabled?'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 3a3 3 0 0 0-3 3v6a3 3 0 0 0 6 0V6a3 3 0 0 0-3-3Z"/><path d="M19 10v2a7 7 0 0 1-14 0v-2M12 19v3M8 22h8"/></svg>':'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 4 16 16M9 9v3a3 3 0 0 0 5.1 2.1M15 9V6a3 3 0 0 0-5.2-2M19 10v2a7 7 0 0 1-11.9 5M12 19v3M8 22h8"/></svg>'}
function endVoiceCall(sendClose=true){stopRingtone();if(activeCall&&sendClose){try{activeCall.close()}catch(e){}}activeCall=null;incomingCall=null;stopLocalStream();const audio=document.getElementById('remoteAudio');audio.srcObject=null;stopCallTimer();hideCallModal()}
function stopLocalStream(){if(localStream){localStream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());localStream=null}}
function showCallModal(type,name,initial){const o=document.getElementById('callOverlay');document.getElementById('callAvatar').textContent=initial||'A';document.getElementById('callTitle').textContent=name||'Voice call';document.getElementById('callPeer').textContent=peerId?'Your Peer ID: '+peerId:'';const sub=document.getElementById('callSub'),a=document.getElementById('callActions'),timer=document.getElementById('callTimer');timer.textContent='';if(type==='incoming'){sub.textContent='Incoming voice call…';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction accept" onclick="acceptIncomingCall()" title="Accept"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 4.5 9 4l2 4-2 2a13 13 0 0 0 5 5l2-2 4 2-.5 3a2 2 0 0 1-2.2 1.5C9.5 18.2 5.8 14.5 4.5 6.7A2 2 0 0 1 6 4.5Z"/></svg></button><button class="callAction end" onclick="rejectIncomingCall()" title="Reject"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}else if(type==='outgoing'){sub.textContent='Calling…';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction end" onclick="endVoiceCall()" title="End call"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}else{sub.textContent='Connected';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction" id="muteCallBtn" onclick="toggleMute()" title="Mute"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 3a3 3 0 0 0-3 3v6a3 3 0 0 0 6 0V6a3 3 0 0 0-3-3Z"/><path d="M19 10v2a7 7 0 0 1-14 0v-2M12 19v3M8 22h8"/></svg></button><button class="callAction end" onclick="endVoiceCall()" title="End call"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}o.classList.add('show')}
function hideCallModal(){document.getElementById('callOverlay')?.classList.remove('show')}
function startCallTimer(){stopCallTimer();callStartedAt=Date.now();callTimerInt=setInterval(()=>{const s=Math.floor((Date.now()-callStartedAt)/1000),m=String(Math.floor(s/60)).padStart(2,'0'),sec=String(s%60).padStart(2,'0');const el=document.getElementById('callTimer');if(el)el.textContent=m+':'+sec},500)}
function stopCallTimer(){if(callTimerInt){clearInterval(callTimerInt);callTimerInt=null}}
function playRingtone(){const a=document.getElementById('ringtoneAudio');try{const ctx=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();ctx.resume?.();let on=true;function beep(){if(!on)return;const o=ctx.createOscillator(),g=ctx.createGain();o.frequency.value=660;g.gain.value=.045;o.connect(g);g.connect(ctx.destination);o.start();setTimeout(()=>{o.stop();},220);setTimeout(beep,800)}a._stop=()=>{on=false;try{ctx.close()}catch(e){}};beep()}catch(e){}}
function stopRingtone(){const a=document.getElementById('ringtoneAudio');if(a&&a._stop){a._stop();a._stop=null}}

const chats=[];
let current=null, activeTab="all";function setRecipientPeerId(chatId,id){const c=chats.find(x=>x.id===chatId);if(!c)return toast('Chat not found');c.peerId=String(id||'').trim();if(c.name==='Arjun')localStorage.setItem('kpwa_peer_arjun',c.peerId);if(c.name==='Meera')localStorage.setItem('kpwa_peer_meera',c.peerId);if(c.name==='Fahad')localStorage.setItem('kpwa_peer_fahad',c.peerId);toast(c.peerId?'Recipient Peer ID saved':'Recipient Peer ID cleared')}
function renderChats(){
 const q=document.getElementById('search').value.toLowerCase();
 const list=chats.filter(c=>(activeTab==='all'||(activeTab==='unread'&&c.unread)|| (activeTab==='groups'&&c.group))&&c.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
 document.getElementById('chatList').innerHTML=list.map(c=>`<div class="row" onclick="openChat(${c.id})"><div class="avatar ${c.color}">${c.initial}${c.online?'<span class="dot"></span>':''}</div><div class="rowmain"><div class="rline"><span class="name">${c.name}</span><span class="time">${c.time}</span></div><div class="rline"><span class="preview">${c.preview}</span>${c.unread?`<span class="unread">${c.unread}</span>`:''}</div></div></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty"><div><strong>No chats found</strong>Try another search.</div></div>';
}
function deliveryIcon(state){
 if(state==='pending') return '<span class="delivery pending" title="Sending"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8.5"/><path d="M12 7v5l3 2"/></svg></span>';
 if(state==='sent') return '<span class="delivery sent" title="Sent"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m5 12 4 4L19 6"/></svg></span>';
 if(state==='delivered') return '<span class="delivery delivered" title="Delivered"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m3.5 12 4 4L17 6"/><path d="m9 15 2 2L21 7"/></svg></span>';
 return '<span class="delivery read" title="Read"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m3.5 12 4 4L17 6"/><path d="m9 15 2 2L21 7"/></svg></span>';
}
function renderMessages(){
 const html=current.messages.map((m,idx)=>{
   const kind=m[0], time=m[2]||'', isMe=kind==='me'||kind==='me-gif'||kind==='me-media', state=m[4]|| (isMe?'read':null);
   let payload={},content='',caption='',reply='';
   if(kind==='gif'||kind==='me-gif'){try{payload=typeof m[1]==='string'&&m[1].startsWith('{')?JSON.parse(m[1]):{url:m[1]};}catch(e){payload={url:m[1]}} caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||'';content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" loading="lazy" alt="GIF"></div>`;}
   else if(kind==='media'||kind==='me-media'){try{payload=JSON.parse(m[1]);}catch(e){payload={url:'',name:'Media',type:''}} caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||''; if((payload.type||'').startsWith('image/')) content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url)}" loading="lazy" alt="${escapeHtml(payload.name||'image')}"></div>`; else if((payload.type||'').startsWith('audio/')) content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><audio controls src="${escapeHtml(payload.url)}"></audio></div>`; else content=`<div class="fileCard"><span>📎</span><a href="${escapeHtml(payload.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#b8ff22;text-decoration:none;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${escapeHtml(payload.name||'Download file')}</a></div>`;}
   else {content=`<div class="txt">${escapeHtml(m[1]||'')}</div>`; reply=m[3]||'';}
   const quote=reply?`<div class="replyQuote">↩ ${escapeHtml(reply)}</div>`:'';
   const cap=caption?`<div class="msgMediaCaption">${escapeHtml(caption)}</div>`:'';
   const status=isMe?deliveryIcon(state):'';
   const changed=isMe&&m._lastState!==state;
   if(isMe)m._lastState=state;
   return `<div class="msg ${isMe?'me':'in'} ${kind.includes('media')||kind.includes('gif')?'fileMsg':''} ${changed?'state-change':''}">${quote}${content}${cap}<div class="meta">${escapeHtml(time)} ${status}</div></div>`;
 }).join('');
 document.getElementById('messages').innerHTML='<div class="love-fx" id="loveFx"></div><div class="chatStarted">Chat started · '+escapeHtml(current.startedAt||'Today')+'</div><div class="day">TODAY</div>'+html;
 const el=document.getElementById('messages');el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight;
 document.getElementById('headName').textContent=current.name; const oc=current.group?((current.memberIds||[]).map(id=>id==='me'?{online:true}:chats.find(c=>c.id===id)).filter(Boolean).filter(x=>x.online).length):0; document.getElementById('headStatus').textContent=current.group?(oc?`${oc} online`:'offline'):(current.online?'online':'last seen recently'); const ha=document.getElementById('headAvatar');if(ha){ha.firstChild.nodeValue=current.initial;const d=ha.querySelector('.dot');if(d)d.style.display=(!current.group&&current.online)?'block':'none';}
 if(current.group){document.getElementById('chatHead').onclick=null;}
}
async function openChat(id){current=chats.find(c=>c.id===id)||chats[0];current.unread=0;renderChats();renderMessages();document.getElementById('app').classList.add('chat-open');if(current.firestoreId)startFirebaseMessageListener(current);else if(fbUser&&current.otherUid){current.firestoreId=await ensurePrivateChat(current.otherUid,current.name,current.peerId);startFirebaseMessageListener(current)}}
function closeChat(){document.getElementById('app').classList.remove('chat-open')}
async function sendMessage(){const input=document.getElementById('messageInput'),t=input.value.trim();if(!t)return;const tm=new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});const m=['me',t,tm,'','pending'];current.messages.push(m);current.preview=t;current.time=tm;input.value='';renderMessages();renderChats();try{const ok=await persistTextMessage(t);if(ok){m[4]='sent';renderMessages();}else{m[4]='delivered';renderMessages();}}catch(e){console.error(e);m[4]='sent';renderMessages();toast('Message saved locally; Firebase write failed')}}
function simulateIncoming(){setTimeout(()=>{current.messages.push(['in','Got it 👍',new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}),'']);renderMessages();renderChats();},700)}
function triggerLove(){const box=document.getElementById('loveFx');if(!box)return;for(let i=0;i<9;i++){const s=document.createElement('span');s.textContent='❤';s.style.left=(8+Math.random()*84)+'%';s.style.setProperty('--dx',((Math.random()-.5)*100)+'px');s.style.setProperty('--rot',((Math.random()-.5)*50)+'deg');s.style.animationDelay=(Math.random()*.45)+'s';box.appendChild(s);setTimeout(()=>s.remove(),2800)}}

function openEmojiPicker(){
 const s=document.getElementById('sheet');const groups={Faces:'😀 😃 😄 😁 😆 😅 😂 🤣 😊 😍 🥰 😘 😎 🤔 😐 😑 😶 🙄 😏 😢 😭 😡 🤬 😱 😴 🤗 🫡',Reactions:'👍 👎 👌 ✌️ 🤌 🤝 🙏 👏 🙌 💪 🫶 ❤️ 🧡 💛 💚 💙 💜 🖤 🤍 💔 💯 🔥 ✨',Kerala:'🥥 🌴 ☕ 🍛 🌧️ 🛶 🐘 🎉 😄 😂 🤣 ❤️ 🙏',Fun:'🎮 ⚽ 🏆 🎯 🚀 😎 🤩 🤯 👀 💀 😂 🤝'};let html='<div class="handle"></div><h3>Emoji</h3><div class="emojiPanel">';Object.values(groups).join(' ').split(/\s+/).forEach(e=>{if(e)html+=`<button onclick="insertEmoji('${e}')">${e}</button>`});html+='</div><button class="secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Close</button>';s.innerHTML=html;document.getElementById('modalBack').classList.add('show')}
function insertEmoji(e){const input=document.getElementById('messageInput');if(input){input.value+=e;input.focus()}closeModal()}

function openAttachMenu(){
 const s=document.getElementById('sheet');
 s.innerHTML='<div class="handle"></div><h3>Attach</h3><div class="attachMenu"><button class="attachOption" onclick="pickMedia(\'image/*\')"><strong>▧</strong>Photo</button><button class="attachOption" onclick="pickMedia(\'image/*\')"><strong>◉</strong>Camera</button><button class="attachOption" onclick="pickMedia(\'audio/*,.pdf,.zip,.doc,.docx,.xls,.xlsx,.txt\')"><strong>⌁</strong>File</button><button class="attachOption" onclick="closeModal();openGifPicker(false)"><strong>GIF</strong>GIF</button></div><button class="secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>';
 document.getElementById('modalBack').classList.add('show');
}
function pickMedia(accept){const input=document.getElementById('mediaInput');input.accept=accept;closeModal();setTimeout(()=>input.click(),80)}
async function prepareChatMedia(file){if(!file)return;pendingMediaFile=file;pendingMediaObjectUrl=URL.createObjectURL(file);openMediaPreview();}
let pendingMediaFile=null,pendingMediaObjectUrl=null,pendingGifUrl=null;
function openMediaPreview(){
 const s=document.getElementById('sheet'); const f=pendingMediaFile; const isImage=(f.type||'').startsWith('image/'); const isAudio=(f.type||'').startsWith('audio/');
 let media=isImage?`<img class="previewMedia" src="${pendingMediaObjectUrl}" alt="Preview">`:isAudio?`<audio class="previewMedia" controls src="${pendingMediaObjectUrl}"></audio>`:`<div class="selectedFile"><div class="mini">📎</div><div style="min-width:0"><b>${escapeHtml(f.name)}</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:11px">${Math.ceil(f.size/1024)} KB</div></div></div>`;
 s.innerHTML=`<div class="handle"></div><h3>Preview before sending</h3>${media}<div class="sendPreviewMeta">${escapeHtml(f.name)} · ${escapeHtml(f.type||'file')}</div><input class="field captionInput" id="mediaCaption" placeholder="Add a caption…" maxlength="1000"><input class="field replyInput" id="mediaReply" placeholder="Reply to (optional)…" maxlength="300"><button class="primary" onclick="sendPendingMedia()">Send</button><button class="secondary" onclick="cancelPendingMedia()">Cancel</button>`;

}

async function cloudinaryUpload(file){
 if(!file) return null; const fd=new FormData(); fd.append('file',file); fd.append('upload_preset',CLOUDINARY_PRESET);
 const r=await fetch(`https://api.cloudinary.com/v1_1/${CLOUDINARY_CLOUD}/auto/upload`,{method:'POST',body:fd}); if(!r.ok) throw new Error('Cloudinary upload failed'); return await r.json();
}

 }catch(e){grid.innerHTML='<div style="color:#f66;padding:12px">Could not load GIFs.</div>';console.error(e)}

function editContact(c){const person=c==='current'?{id:'me',name:'KP User',phone:'+91'}:c;const p=(person.name||'').split(' '),f=p.shift()||'',l=p.join(' ');const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');pi.innerHTML=`<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="openContactInfo(${c==='current'?'current':person.id})"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Edit contact</h2></div></div><div class="pageCard editContact"><div class="fieldIcon"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="8" r="3.4"/><path d="M5 20a7 7 0 0 1 14 0"/></svg></div><label>First name<input class="field" id="editFirst" value="${escapeHtml(f)}"></label><label>Last name<input class="field" id="editLast" value="${escapeHtml(l)}"></label><label>Phone number<div class="phoneFixed"><span>KP</span><b>+91</b><input id="editPhone" value="${escapeHtml((person.phone||'').replace(/^\+91/,'').trim())}"></div></label><button class="primary" onclick="saveContact(${c==='current'?'current':person.id})">Save</button></div>`;pv.classList.add('show')}

function clearChatFor(id){const c=id==='current'?current:chats.find(x=>x.id===id);if(c){c.messages=[];c.preview='No messages yet';renderMessages();renderChats()}toast('Chat cleared')}
function blockContact(id){const c=id==='current'?current:chats.find(x=>x.id===id);if(c)c.blocked=true;toast('Contact blocked');closePage();renderChats()}
function escapeHtml(s){s=String(s??'');return s.replace(/[&<>"']/g,m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#039;'}[m]))}

 const s=document.getElementById('sheet');
 s.innerHTML='<div class="handle"></div><h3>New chat</h3><div class="gifHint">Only real Firebase users are shown. Demo/bot accounts are never added.</div><input class="field" id="userSearch" placeholder="Search saved name or 6-digit number" oninput="filterRealUsers()"><div id="realUserList" style="max-height:55vh;overflow:auto"><div style="padding:15px;color:#777">Loading users…</div></div><button class="secondary" onclick="closeModal()">Cancel</button>';
 document.getElementById('modalBack').classList.add('show'); await loadRealUsers();
}
async function loadRealUsers(){
 if(!fbUser)return;try{const snap=await fbDb.collection('users').get();realUsers=[];snap.forEach(d=>{if(d.id===fbUser.uid)return;const u=d.data();realUsers.push({...u,uid:d.id})});renderRealUsers(realUsers)}catch(e){console.error(e);document.getElementById('realUserList').innerHTML='<div style="padding:15px;color:#ff6677">Could not load users.</div>'}}
function renderRealUsers(list){const box=document.getElementById('realUserList');if(!box)return;box.innerHTML=list.map(u=>`<div class="userRow" onclick="startChatWithUser('${u.uid}')"><div class="userAvatar">${u.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(u.photoURL)}">`:escapeHtml((u.displayName||u.firstName||'U')[0].toUpperCase())}</div><div class="userMeta"><b>${escapeHtml(u.displayName||u.firstName||'Unknown user')}</b><small>+91 ${escapeHtml(u.phoneNumber||'')} · ${u.online?'Online':'KP user'}</small></div><span>›</span></div>`).join('')||'<div style="padding:15px;color:#777">No other registered users found.</div>'}
function filterRealUsers(){const q=(document.getElementById('userSearch')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();renderRealUsers(realUsers.filter(u=>(u.displayName||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(u.firstName||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(u.phoneNumber||'').includes(q)))}
async function startChatWithUser(uid){const u=realUsers.find(x=>x.uid===uid);if(!u)return;closeModal();const chatId=await ensurePrivateChat(uid,u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber,u.peerId||'');let c=chats.find(x=>x.firestoreId===chatId);if(!c){c={id:chatId,firestoreId:chatId,name:u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber,initial:(u.displayName||u.firstName||'U')[0].toUpperCase(),preview:'No messages yet',time:'',sortTime:Date.now(),online:false,unread:0,color:'green',group:false,messages:[],peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:uid,phone:'+91 '+(u.phoneNumber||''),photoURL:u.photoURL||'',startedAt:'Today'};chats.unshift(c)}else{c.name=u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber;c.peerId=u.peerId||'';c.recipientPeerId=u.peerId||'';c.phone='+91 '+(u.phoneNumber||'');c.photoURL=u.photoURL||''}renderChats();await loadFirebaseMessages(c);openChat(c.id)}
function renderChats(){const q=(document.getElementById('search')?.value||'').toLowerCase();const list=chats.filter(c=>(activeTab==='all'||(activeTab==='unread'&&c.unread)||(activeTab==='groups'&&c.group))&&((c.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(c.phone||'').includes(q)));list.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));document.getElementById('chatList').innerHTML=list.map(c=>`<div class="row" onclick="openChat('${String(c.id).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')"><div class="avatar ${c.color||'green'}">${c.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(c.photoURL)}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:50%">`:escapeHtml(c.initial)}${c.online?'<span class="dot"></span>':''}</div><div class="rowmain"><div class="rline"><span class="name">${escapeHtml(c.name)}</span><span class="time">${escapeHtml(c.time||'')}</span></div><div class="rline"><span class="preview">${escapeHtml(c.preview||'')}</span>${c.unread?`<span class="unread">${c.unread}</span>`:''}</div></div></div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty"><div><strong>No chats yet</strong>Tap + to find people who are registered in KP WhatsApp.</div></div>'}
async function openChat(id){current=chats.find(c=>String(c.id)===String(id));if(!current)return;current.unread=0;renderChats();renderMessages();document.getElementById('app').classList.add('chat-open');if(current.firestoreId)startFirebaseMessageListener(current)}
async function persistChatOrder(){if(!current?.firestoreId||!fbUser)return;await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId).set({lastMessage:current.preview||'',lastMessageAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),lastMessageSenderId:fbUser.uid},{merge:true})}
async function persistTextMessage(text){if(!fbUser||!current||current.group)return false;let chatId=current.firestoreId;if(!chatId&&current.otherUid){chatId=await ensurePrivateChat(current.otherUid,current.name,current.peerId);current.firestoreId=chatId}if(!chatId)return false;const ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(chatId);const msg={senderId:fbUser.uid,type:'text',text,createdAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),edited:false,deleted:false,replyTo:null,media:null,reactions:{},deliveredTo:[],readBy:[]};await ref.collection('messages').add(msg);await ref.set({lastMessage:text,lastMessageAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),lastMessageSenderId:fbUser.uid},{merge:true});current.sortTime=Date.now();current.time=new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});renderChats();return true}

function kpAuthEmail(number){return `${number}@kpwa-auth.invalid`;}
function togglePassword(inputId,buttonId){const input=document.getElementById(inputId),btn=document.getElementById(buttonId);if(!input||!btn)return;const open=input.type==='password';input.type=open?'text':'password';btn.classList.toggle('is-open',open);btn.setAttribute('aria-label',open?'Hide password':'Show password');}
async function firebaseGoogleLogin(){setAuthError('');try{const p=new firebase.auth.GoogleAuthProvider();p.setCustomParameters({prompt:'select_account'});await fbAuth.signInWithPopup(p);}catch(e){console.error(e);setAuthError(firebaseAuthMessage(e));}}
async function firebasePhoneLogin(){setAuthError('');const phone=(document.getElementById('loginPhone')?.value||'').replace(/\D/g,'');const pass=document.getElementById('passwordAuth')?.value||'';if(!/^\d{6}$/.test(phone)){setAuthError('Enter your 6-digit KP number.');return}if(!pass){setAuthError('Enter your password.');return}try{const snap=await fbDb.collection('phoneNumbers').doc(phone).get();if(!snap.exists){setAuthError('No account exists for this KP number.');return}await fbAuth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(snap.data().email||kpAuthEmail(phone),pass);}catch(e){console.error(e);setAuthError(e?.code==='auth/wrong-password'||e?.code==='auth/invalid-credential'?'Incorrect KP number or password.':firebaseAuthMessage(e));}}
function firebaseAuthMessage(e){const m={'auth/popup-blocked':'Allow popups for this site and try again.','auth/popup-closed-by-user':'Google sign-in was cancelled.','auth/unauthorized-domain':'Add this website domain in Firebase Authentication → Settings → Authorized domains.','auth/invalid-credential':'The login details are invalid.','auth/wrong-password':'The password is incorrect.','auth/user-not-found':'No account exists for this login.','auth/email-already-in-use':'This account already exists.','auth/weak-password':'Use at least 6 characters for the password.','auth/too-many-requests':'Too many attempts. Try again later.'};return m[e?.code]||e?.message||'Authentication failed.'}
async function completeFirebaseProfile(){setProfileError('');if(!fbUser&&document.getElementById('profileSetupTitle').textContent!=='Create your account')return;const name=document.getElementById('profileName').value.trim();const phone=document.getElementById('profilePhone').value.replace(/\D/g,'');const mobile=(document.getElementById('profileMobile')?.value||'').replace(/\D/g,'');const age=Number(document.getElementById('profileAge').value);const pass=document.getElementById('profilePassword').value;if(!name){setProfileError('Enter your name.');return}if(!/^\d{6}$/.test(phone)){setProfileError('Enter a unique 6-digit KP number.');return}if(mobile && !/^\d{10}$/.test(mobile)){setProfileError('Enter a valid 10-digit mobile number.');return}if(!Number.isInteger(age)||age<13||age>120){setProfileError('Enter a valid age.');return}if(pass.length<6){setProfileError('Password must be at least 6 characters.');return}try{
  const numberRef=fbDb.collection('phoneNumbers').doc(phone);
  // Firebase Authentication owns the password and makes the hidden KP email unique.
  const methods=await fbAuth.fetchSignInMethodsForEmail(kpAuthEmail(phone)).catch(()=>[]);
  if(methods.length && (!fbUser || !methods.includes('google.com'))){
    const existingMap=await numberRef.get().catch(()=>null);
    if(!fbUser || !existingMap?.exists || existingMap.data()?.uid!==fbUser.uid){setProfileError('This 6-digit number is already in use. Choose another number.');return}
  }
  // Google users get a password credential linked to the same Firebase account, so both Google and KP-number login work.
  if(!fbUser){const cred=await fbAuth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(kpAuthEmail(phone),pass);fbUser=cred.user;}
  else if(!fbUser.providerData.some(p=>p.providerId==='password')){const credential=firebase.auth.EmailAuthProvider.credential(kpAuthEmail(phone),pass);await fbUser.linkWithCredential(credential);}
  else {await fbUser.updatePassword(pass);}
  const ref=fbDb.collection('users').doc(fbUser.uid);const snap=await ref.get();const old=snap.exists?snap.data():{};
  if(old.phoneNumber && old.phoneNumber!==phone){await fbDb.collection('phoneNumbers').doc(old.phoneNumber).delete().catch(()=>{});}
  try{await numberRef.set({uid:fbUser.uid,email:kpAuthEmail(phone),updatedAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true});}catch(e){console.warn('phone mapping write failed',e)}
  const data={uid:fbUser.uid,firstName:name.split(/\s+/)[0],lastName:name.split(/\s+/).slice(1).join(' '),displayName:name,phoneCountryCode:'+91',phoneNumber:phone,mobileNumber:mobile,age,email:fbUser.email||old.email||kpAuthEmail(phone),photoURL:fbUser.photoURL||old.photoURL||'',avatarType:fbUser.photoURL?'google':'svg',bio:old.bio||'',peerId:peerId||old.peerId||'',createdAt:old.createdAt||firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),updatedAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),lastSeen:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),privacy:old.privacy||{lastSeen:'everyone',profilePhoto:'everyone',about:'everyone',readReceipts:true},settings:old.settings||{notifications:true,sounds:true,darkMode:true},blockedUsers:old.blockedUsers||[]};
  await ref.set(data,{merge:true});fbUserProfile=data;await startFirebasePresence();document.getElementById('authGate').classList.add('hidden');document.getElementById('app').classList.remove('authLocked');renderChats();renderMessages();initPeer();await loadFirebaseChats();
}catch(e){console.error(e);if(e?.message==='KP_NUMBER_TAKEN'||e?.code==='KP_NUMBER_TAKEN')setProfileError('This 6-digit number is already in use. Choose another number.');else setProfileError('Could not create/save your account: '+(firebaseAuthMessage(e)||e.message||'Unknown error'));}}
let fbUserProfile=null;
async function loadFirebaseChats(){if(!fbUser||!fbDb)return;try{const snap=await fbDb.collection('chats').where('memberIds','array-contains',fbUser.uid).get();const remote=[];for(const d of snap.docs){const x=d.data();const other=(x.memberIds||[]).find(id=>id!==fbUser.uid);let u={};if(other){const us=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(other).get();if(us.exists)u=us.data();}if(x.type==='group'){remote.push({id:d.id,firestoreId:d.id,name:x.name||'Group',initial:(x.name||'G')[0].toUpperCase(),preview:x.lastMessage||'No messages yet',time:x.lastMessageAt?.toDate?x.lastMessageAt.toDate().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'',online:false,unread:x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0,color:'green',group:true,memberIds:x.memberIds||[],messages:[],startedAt:x.createdAt?.toDate?x.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleDateString():'Today'});}else{let online=false;if(other){try{const ps=await fbRtdb.ref('presence/'+other).once('value');online=ps.val()?.state==='online'}catch(e){}}remote.push({id:d.id,firestoreId:d.id,name:u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber||'Unknown user',initial:(u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber||'U')[0].toUpperCase(),preview:x.lastMessage||'No messages yet',time:x.lastMessageAt?.toDate?x.lastMessageAt.toDate().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'',sortTime:x.lastMessageAt?.toMillis?x.lastMessageAt.toMillis():0,online,unread:x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0,color:'green',group:false,messages:[],peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:other,phone:'+91 '+(u.phoneNumber||''),photoURL:u.photoURL||'',startedAt:x.createdAt?.toDate?x.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleDateString():'Today'});}}remote.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));chats.splice(0,chats.length,...remote);renderChats();if(current&&!chats.some(c=>c.id===current.id))current=null;if(current)await loadFirebaseMessages(current);}catch(e){console.warn('load chats',e);renderChats()}}
async function loadFirebaseMessages(c){try{const snap=await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId||c.id).collection('messages').orderBy('createdAt','asc').limit(200).get();c.messages=snap.docs.map(d=>{const m=d.data();const me=m.senderId===fbUser.uid;const time=m.createdAt?.toDate?m.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'';let kind=me?'me':'in';if(m.type==='gif')kind=me?'me-gif':'gif';if(['image','audio','file'].includes(m.type))kind=me?'me-media':'media';const payload=m.media?JSON.stringify({...m.media,caption:m.caption||'',reply:m.replyTo?.text||''}):m.text||'';return [kind,payload,time,m.replyTo?.text||'',m.readBy?.includes(fbUser.uid)?'read':m.deliveredTo?.includes(fbUser.uid)?'delivered':me?'sent':null,{id:d.id}];});if(current===c)renderMessages();}catch(e){console.warn('load messages',e)}}
function startFirebaseMessageListener(c){if(fbUnsubMessages)fbUnsubMessages();if(!fbUser||!c?.firestoreId)return;fbUnsubMessages=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId).collection('messages').orderBy('createdAt','asc').limit(200).onSnapshot(s=>{c.messages=s.docs.map(d=>{const m=d.data(),me=m.senderId===fbUser.uid,time=m.createdAt?.toDate?m.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'';let kind=me?'me':'in';if(m.type==='gif')kind=me?'me-gif':'gif';if(['image','audio','file'].includes(m.type))kind=me?'me-media':'media';return [kind,m.media?JSON.stringify({...m.media,caption:m.caption||'',reply:m.replyTo?.text||''}):m.text||'',time,m.replyTo?.text||'',m.readBy?.includes(fbUser.uid)?'read':m.deliveredTo?.includes(fbUser.uid)?'delivered':me?'sent':null,{id:d.id}]}).map(x=>x);renderMessages();renderChats();});}
async function ensurePrivateChat(otherUid,otherName,otherPeerId=''){if(!fbUser||!otherUid)return null;const ids=[fbUser.uid,otherUid].sort();const id=ids.join('_');const ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(id);const snap=await ref.get();if(!snap.exists)await ref.set({type:'private',memberIds:ids,createdAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),lastMessage:'',lastMessageAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),lastMessageSenderId:'',unreadCount:{[fbUser.uid]:0,[otherUid]:0},peerIds:{[fbUser.uid]:peerId||'', [otherUid]:otherPeerId||''} });return id}
async function persistTextMessage(text){if(!fbUser||!current||current.group)return false;let chatId=current.firestoreId;if(!chatId&&current.otherUid){chatId=await ensurePrivateChat(current.otherUid,current.name,current.peerId);current.firestoreId=chatId;}if(!chatId)return false;const ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(chatId);const msg={senderId:fbUser.uid,type:'text',text,createdAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),edited:false,deleted:false,replyTo:null,media:null,reactions:{},deliveredTo:[],readBy:[]};await ref.collection('messages').add(msg);await ref.set({lastMessage:text,lastMessageAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),lastMessageSenderId:fbUser.uid},{merge:true});return true}
async function startFirebasePresence(){if(!fbUser||!fbRtdb)return;fbPresenceRef=fbRtdb.ref('presence/'+fbUser.uid);await fbPresenceRef.onDisconnect().set({state:'offline',lastChanged:firebase.database.ServerValue.TIMESTAMP,peerId:peerId||''});await fbPresenceRef.set({state:'online',lastChanged:firebase.database.ServerValue.TIMESTAMP,peerId:peerId||''});}
async function firebaseLogout(){try{if(fbPresenceRef)await fbPresenceRef.set({state:'offline',lastChanged:firebase.database.ServerValue.TIMESTAMP,peerId:peerId||''});await fbAuth.signOut()}catch(e){console.error(e)}}

    <div class="newChatSearch"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"/><path d="m16 16 5 5"/></svg><input class="field" id="userSearch" placeholder="Search saved name or number" oninput="filterRealUsers()"></div>
