    <div id="numberAvailability" class="availability">Enter a 10-digit number to check availability.</div>
    <div class="realUsersFull" id="realUserList"><div style="padding:22px;color:#777;text-align:center">Loading registered users…</div></div>
  </div>`;
  pv.classList.add('show');
  loadRealUsers();
}
async function loadRealUsers(){
  if(!fbUser||!fbDb)return;
  try{
    const snap=await fbDb.collection('users').get(); realUsers=[];
    snap.forEach(d=>{if(d.id===fbUser.uid)return;const u=d.data();realUsers.push({...u,uid:d.id});});
    for(const u of realUsers){try{const ps=await fbRtdb.ref('presence/'+u.uid).once('value');u.online=ps.val()?.state==='online';}catch(e){u.online=false;}}
    renderRealUsers(realUsers);
  }catch(e){console.error(e);const b=document.getElementById('realUserList');if(b)b.innerHTML='<div style="padding:22px;color:#ff6878;text-align:center">Could not load registered users.</div>';}
}
function renderRealUsers(list){
  const box=document.getElementById('realUserList');if(!box)return;
  box.innerHTML=list.map(u=>`<div class="userRow" onclick="startChatWithUser('${u.uid}')"><div class="userAvatar">${u.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(u.photoURL)}">`:escapeHtml((u.displayName||u.firstName||'U')[0].toUpperCase())}</div><div class="userMeta"><b>${escapeHtml(u.displayName||u.firstName||'Unknown user')}</b><small>${u.mobileNumber?'+'+escapeHtml(u.phoneCountryCode||'91')+' '+escapeHtml(u.mobileNumber):'KP '+escapeHtml(u.phoneNumber||'')} · ${u.online?'Online':'Offline'}</small></div><span>›</span></div>`).join('')||'<div style="padding:22px;color:#777;text-align:center">No other registered users found.</div>';
}
function filterRealUsers(){const q=(document.getElementById('userSearch')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();renderRealUsers(realUsers.filter(u=>(u.displayName||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(u.firstName||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(u.lastName||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(u.phoneNumber||'').includes(q)||(u.mobileNumber||'').includes(q)));}
async function checkNewChatNumber(){
  const input=document.getElementById('newChatNumber'),out=document.getElementById('numberAvailability'); if(!input||!out)return;
  const n=input.value.replace(/\D/g,'');
  if(!/^\d{10}$/.test(n)){out.className='availability no';out.textContent='Number unavailable. Enter a valid 10-digit number.';return;}
  out.className='availability';out.textContent='Checking…';
  try{
    const snap=await fbDb.collection('users').where('mobileNumber','==',n).limit(1).get();
    if(snap.empty){out.className='availability no';out.textContent='This number is not available on KP WhatsApp.';return;}
    const d=snap.docs[0],u=d.data();if(d.id===fbUser.uid){out.className='availability no';out.textContent='This is your own number.';return;}
    out.className='availability ok';out.textContent='This number is available on KP WhatsApp.';
    await startChatWithUser(d.id);
  }catch(e){console.error(e);out.className='availability no';out.textContent='Could not check this number right now.';}
}
async function startChatWithUser(uid){
  const u=realUsers.find(x=>x.uid===uid) || (await fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid).get()).data();
  if(!u||uid===fbUser.uid)return;
  const chatId=await ensurePrivateChat(uid,u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber,u.peerId||'');
  let c=chats.find(x=>x.firestoreId===chatId);
  if(!c){c={id:chatId,firestoreId:chatId,name:u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber,initial:(u.displayName||u.firstName||'U')[0].toUpperCase(),preview:'No messages yet',time:'',sortTime:Date.now(),online:!!u.online,unread:0,color:'green',group:false,messages:[],peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:uid,phone:u.mobileNumber?'+91 '+u.mobileNumber:'KP '+(u.phoneNumber||''),photoURL:u.photoURL||'',startedAt:'Today'};chats.unshift(c)}
  else{c.name=u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber;c.peerId=u.peerId||'';c.recipientPeerId=u.peerId||'';c.otherUid=uid;c.phone=u.mobileNumber?'+91 '+u.mobileNumber:'KP '+(u.phoneNumber||'');c.photoURL=u.photoURL||'';c.sortTime=Date.now();}
  closePage(); renderChats(); await loadFirebaseMessages(c); await openChat(c.id);
}
async function deleteMessage(messageId,idx){
  if(!fbUser||!current||!messageId)return;
  const m=current.messages[idx]; if(!m)return;
  const isMe=(m[0]==='me'||m[0]==='me-gif'||m[0]==='me-media');
  if(!isMe){toast('You can delete only your own messages');return;}
  if(!confirm('Delete this message?'))return;
  try{
    await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId||current.id).collection('messages').doc(messageId).delete();
    toast('Message deleted');
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not delete this message');}
}
function renderMessages(){
 const list=current?.messages||[];
 const html=list.map((m,idx)=>{
   const kind=m[0],time=m[2]||'',isMe=kind==='me'||kind==='me-gif'||kind==='me-media',state=m[4]||(isMe?'read':null),docId=m[5]?.id||'';
   let payload={},content='',caption='',reply='';
   if(kind==='gif'||kind==='me-gif'){try{payload=typeof m[1]==='string'&&m[1].startsWith('{')?JSON.parse(m[1]):{url:m[1]};}catch(e){payload={url:m[1]}}caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||'';content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" loading="lazy" alt="GIF"></div>`;}
   else if(kind==='media'||kind==='me-media'){try{payload=JSON.parse(m[1]);}catch(e){payload={url:'',name:'Media',type:''}}caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||'';if((payload.type||'').startsWith('image/'))content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url)}" loading="lazy" alt="${escapeHtml(payload.name||'image')}"></div>`;else if((payload.type||'').startsWith('audio/'))content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><audio controls src="${escapeHtml(payload.url)}"></audio></div>`;else content=`<div class="fileCard"><span>📎</span><a href="${escapeHtml(payload.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#b8ff22;text-decoration:none;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${escapeHtml(payload.name||'Download file')}</a></div>`;}
   else {content=`<div class="txt">${escapeHtml(m[1]||'')}</div>`;reply=m[3]||'';}
   const quote=reply?`<div class="replyQuote">↩ ${escapeHtml(reply)}</div>`:'';const cap=caption?`<div class="msgMediaCaption">${escapeHtml(caption)}</div>`:'';const status=isMe?deliveryIcon(state):'';const del=isMe&&docId?`<span class="msgActions"><button class="msgDeleteBtn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();deleteMessage('${docId}',${idx})" title="Delete message" aria-label="Delete message"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 7h16M9 7V4h6v3M7 7l1 13h8l1-13M10 11v6M14 11v6"/></svg></button></span>`:'';
   return `<div class="msg ${isMe?'me':'in'} ${kind.includes('media')||kind.includes('gif')?'fileMsg':''}">${quote}${content}${cap}<div class="meta">${escapeHtml(time)} ${status}${del}</div></div>`;
 }).join('');
 document.getElementById('messages').innerHTML='<div class="love-fx" id="loveFx"></div><div class="chatStarted">Chat started · '+escapeHtml(current?.startedAt||'Today')+'</div><div class="day">TODAY</div>'+html;
 const el=document.getElementById('messages');if(el)el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight;
}

/* ===== FINAL GROUP FIXES ===== */
function groupAvatarHTML(c, cls='avatar'){
  const url=c?.avatar||'';
  if(url) return `<div class="${cls} ${c?.color||'green'}"><img src="${escapeHtml(url)}" alt="" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:inherit;display:block">${c?.online?'<span class="dot"></span>':''}</div>`;
  return `<div class="${cls} ${c?.color||'green'}">${escapeHtml(c?.initial||'G')}</div>`;
}

function renderChats(){
 const q=(document.getElementById('search')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
 const list=chats.filter(c=>(activeTab==='all'||(activeTab==='unread'&&c.unread)||(activeTab==='groups'&&c.group))&&((c.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(c.phone||'').includes(q)));
 list.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));
 document.getElementById('chatList').innerHTML=list.map(c=>{
   const badge=c.group ? (c.messageCount||0) : (c.unread||0);
   const avatar=c.group ? groupAvatarHTML(c,'avatar') : (c.photoURL?`<div class="avatar ${c.color||'green'}"><img src="${escapeHtml(c.photoURL)}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:50%">${c.online?'<span class="dot"></span>':''}</div>`:`<div class="avatar ${c.color||'green'}">${escapeHtml(c.initial||'U')}${c.online?'<span class="dot"></span>':''}</div>`);
   return `<div class="row" onclick="openChat('${String(c.id).replace(/'/g,"\'")}')">${avatar}<div class="rowmain"><div class="rline"><span class="name">${escapeHtml(c.name)}</span><span class="time">${escapeHtml(c.time||'')}</span></div><div class="rline"><span class="preview">${escapeHtml(c.preview||'')}</span>${badge?`<span class="unread">${badge>999?'999+':badge}</span>`:''}</div></div></div>`;
 }).join('')||'<div class="empty"><div><strong>No chats yet</strong>Tap + to find people who are registered in KP WhatsApp.</div></div>';
}

function renderMessages(){
 const list=current?.messages||[];
 const html=list.map((m,idx)=>{
   const kind=m[0],time=m[2]||'',isMe=kind==='me'||kind==='me-gif'||kind==='me-media',state=m[4]||(isMe?'read':null),docId=m[5]?.id||'';
   if(kind==='system') return `<div class="systemMessage"><span>${escapeHtml(m[1]||'')}</span></div>`;
   let payload={},content='',caption='',reply='';
   if(kind==='gif'||kind==='me-gif'){try{payload=typeof m[1]==='string'&&m[1].startsWith('{')?JSON.parse(m[1]):{url:m[1]};}catch(e){payload={url:m[1]}}caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||'';content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" loading="lazy" alt="GIF"></div>`;}
   else if(kind==='media'||kind==='me-media'){try{payload=JSON.parse(m[1]);}catch(e){payload={url:'',name:'Media',type:''}}caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||'';if((payload.type||'').startsWith('image/'))content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url)}" loading="lazy" alt="${escapeHtml(payload.name||'image')}"></div>`;else if((payload.type||'').startsWith('audio/'))content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><audio controls src="${escapeHtml(payload.url)}"></audio></div>`;else content=`<div class="fileCard"><span>📎</span><a href="${escapeHtml(payload.url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#b8ff22;text-decoration:none;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${escapeHtml(payload.name||'Download file')}</a></div>`;}
   else {content=`<div class="txt">${escapeHtml(m[1]||'')}</div>`;reply=m[3]||'';}
   const quote=reply?`<div class="replyQuote">↩ ${escapeHtml(reply)}</div>`:'';
   const cap=caption?`<div class="msgMediaCaption">${escapeHtml(caption)}</div>`:'';
   const status=isMe?deliveryIcon(state):'';
   const del=isMe&&docId?`<span class="msgActions"><button class="msgDeleteBtn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();deleteMessage('${docId}',${idx})" title="Delete message" aria-label="Delete message"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 7h16M9 7V4h6v3M7 7l1 13h8l1-13M10 11v6M14 11v6"/></svg></button></span>`:'';
   return `<div class="msg ${isMe?'me':'in'} ${kind.includes('media')||kind.includes('gif')?'fileMsg':''}">${quote}${content}${cap}<div class="meta">${escapeHtml(time)} ${status}${del}</div></div>`;
 }).join('');
 document.getElementById('messages').innerHTML='<div class="love-fx" id="loveFx"></div><div class="chatStarted">Chat started · '+escapeHtml(current?.startedAt||'Today')+'</div><div class="day">TODAY</div>'+html;
 const el=document.getElementById('messages');if(el)el.scrollTop=el.scrollHeight;
}

async function finishCreateGroup(){
 if(!fbUser||!fbDb){toast('Please sign in first');return;}
 const name=groupDraft.name||'New Group', about=groupDraft.about||'', type=groupDraft.type||groupType||'public';
 let avatar=groupDraft.avatar||groupAvatarUrl||'';
 toast('Creating group…');
 try{
   if(avatar&&avatar.startsWith('data:')){
     const blob=await (await fetch(avatar)).blob();
     const f=new File([blob],'group-avatar.jpg',{type:blob.type||'image/jpeg'});
     const u=await cloudinaryUpload(f); avatar=u.secure_url;
   }
   const memberIds=[fbUser.uid,...selectedGroupMembers.filter(id=>id&&id!==fbUser.uid)];
   const ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc();
   const now=firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
   const unreadCount={}; memberIds.forEach(id=>unreadCount[id]=0);
   const welcome=`Welcome to ${name}! This group was created successfully.`;
   await ref.set({type:'group',name,about,groupType:type,avatar:avatar||'',avatarType:avatar?'gif':'initial',memberIds,adminIds:[fbUser.uid],createdBy:fbUser.uid,createdAt:now,lastMessage:welcome,lastMessageAt:now,lastMessageSenderId:fbUser.uid,messageCount:1,unreadCount});
   await ref.collection('messages').add({senderId:fbUser.uid,type:'system',text:welcome,createdAt:now,edited:false,deleted:false,replyTo:null,media:null});
   const c={id:ref.id,firestoreId:ref.id,name,initial:name[0]?.toUpperCase()||'G',preview:welcome,time:'now',sortTime:Date.now(),online:false,unread:0,messageCount:1,color:'green',group:true,groupType:type,avatar,avatarType:avatar?'gif':'initial',about,adminIds:[fbUser.uid],memberIds,messages:[['system',welcome,'now']],startedAt:'Today'};
   chats.unshift(c); current=c;
   groupAvatarUrl='';selectedGroupMembers=[];groupDraft={name:'',about:'',type:'public',avatar:''};
   closeModal();renderChats();openChat(c.id);toast('Group created');
 }catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Group creation failed');}
}

async function loadFirebaseChats(){
 if(!fbUser||!fbDb)return;
 try{
  const snap=await fbDb.collection('chats').where('memberIds','array-contains',fbUser.uid).get();
  const remote=[];
  for(const d of snap.docs){
   const x=d.data();
   if(x.type==='group'){
    remote.push({id:d.id,firestoreId:d.id,name:x.name||'Group',initial:(x.name||'G')[0].toUpperCase(),preview:x.lastMessage||'No messages yet',time:x.lastMessageAt?.toDate?x.lastMessageAt.toDate().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'',sortTime:x.lastMessageAt?.toMillis?x.lastMessageAt.toMillis():0,online:false,unread:x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0,messageCount:x.messageCount||0,color:'green',group:true,groupType:x.groupType||'private',avatar:x.avatar||'',avatarType:x.avatarType||'initial',about:x.about||'',adminIds:x.adminIds||[],memberIds:x.memberIds||[],messages:[],startedAt:x.createdAt?.toDate?x.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleDateString():'Today'});
   }else{
    const other=(x.memberIds||[]).find(id=>id!==fbUser.uid);let u={};if(other){const us=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(other).get();if(us.exists)u=us.data();}
    let online=false;if(other){try{const ps=await fbRtdb.ref('presence/'+other).once('value');online=ps.val()?.state==='online'}catch(e){}}
    remote.push({id:d.id,firestoreId:d.id,name:u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber||'Unknown user',initial:(u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber||'U')[0].toUpperCase(),preview:x.lastMessage||'No messages yet',time:x.lastMessageAt?.toDate?x.lastMessageAt.toDate().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'',sortTime:x.lastMessageAt?.toMillis?x.lastMessageAt.toMillis():0,online,unread:x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0,color:'green',group:false,messages:[],peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:other,phone:u.mobileNumber?'+91 '+u.mobileNumber:'KP '+(u.phoneNumber||''),photoURL:u.photoURL||'',startedAt:x.createdAt?.toDate?x.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleDateString():'Today'});
   }
  }
  remote.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));chats.splice(0,chats.length,...remote);renderChats();if(current&&!chats.some(c=>c.id===current.id))current=null;if(current)await loadFirebaseMessages(current);
 }catch(e){console.warn('load chats',e);renderChats()}
}

async function loadFirebaseMessages(c){
 try{const snap=await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId||c.id).collection('messages').orderBy('createdAt','asc').limit(500).get();c.messages=snap.docs.map(d=>{const m=d.data();if(m.type==='system')return ['system',m.text||'',m.createdAt?.toDate?m.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'', '', '', {id:d.id}];const me=m.senderId===fbUser.uid,time=m.createdAt?.toDate?m.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'';let kind=me?'me':'in';if(m.type==='gif')kind=me?'me-gif':'gif';if(['image','audio','file'].includes(m.type))kind=me?'me-media':'media';return [kind,m.media?JSON.stringify({...m.media,caption:m.caption||'',reply:m.replyTo?.text||''}):m.text||'',time,m.replyTo?.text||'',m.readBy?.includes(fbUser.uid)?'read':m.deliveredTo?.includes(fbUser.uid)?'delivered':me?'sent':null,{id:d.id}];});if(c.group)c.messageCount=snap.size;if(current===c)renderMessages();renderChats();}catch(e){console.warn('load messages',e)}
}

function startFirebaseMessageListener(c){
 if(fbUnsubMessages)fbUnsubMessages();if(!fbUser||!c?.firestoreId)return;
 fbUnsubMessages=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId).collection('messages').orderBy('createdAt','asc').limit(500).onSnapshot(async s=>{
  c.messages=s.docs.map(d=>{const m=d.data();if(m.type==='system')return ['system',m.text||'',m.createdAt?.toDate?m.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'', '', '', {id:d.id}];const me=m.senderId===fbUser.uid,time=m.createdAt?.toDate?m.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'';let kind=me?'me':'in';if(m.type==='gif')kind=me?'me-gif':'gif';if(['image','audio','file'].includes(m.type))kind=me?'me-media':'media';return [kind,m.media?JSON.stringify({...m.media,caption:m.caption||'',reply:m.replyTo?.text||''}):m.text||'',time,m.replyTo?.text||'',m.readBy?.includes(fbUser.uid)?'read':m.deliveredTo?.includes(fbUser.uid)?'delivered':me?'sent':null,{id:d.id}]});
  if(c.group){c.messageCount=s.size;c.preview=s.docs.length?((s.docs[s.docs.length-1].data().text)||c.preview):c.preview;c.sortTime=Date.now();c.time=new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});}
  renderMessages();renderChats();
 });
}

async function persistTextMessage(text){
 if(!fbUser||!current)return false;
 let chatId=current.firestoreId||current.id;if(!chatId)return false;
 const ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(chatId);
 const snap=await ref.get();if(!snap.exists)return false;const data=snap.data();
 const msg={senderId:fbUser.uid,type:'text',text,createdAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),edited:false,deleted:false,replyTo:null,media:null,reactions:{},deliveredTo:[],readBy:[]};
 await ref.collection('messages').add(msg);
 const updates={lastMessage:text,lastMessageAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),lastMessageSenderId:fbUser.uid};
 if(data.type==='group') updates.messageCount=firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(1);
 else if(data.memberIds){const other=(data.memberIds||[]).find(id=>id!==fbUser.uid);if(other)updates[`unreadCount.${other}`]=firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(1);}
 await ref.set(updates,{merge:true});current.sortTime=Date.now();current.time=new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});return true;
}

// Make group GIFs visible immediately after selecting them, with a clear preview label.
function selectGif(url,forGroup=false){
 if(forGroup){
   groupAvatarUrl=url;
   const p=document.getElementById('groupAvatarPreview');
   if(p){p.src=url;p.style.display='block';p.dataset.gif='true';}
   closeModal();openModal('group');
   setTimeout(()=>{const img=document.getElementById('groupAvatarPreview');if(img){img.src=url;img.style.display='block';}},40);
   return;
 }
 pendingGifUrl=url;openGifSendPreview(url);
}

function openGroupDetails(){
 if(!current?.group)return;
 const members=(current.memberIds||[fbUser?.uid]).map(id=>id===fbUser?.uid?{id:'me',name:'You',initial:'Y',online:true,phone:current.phone||''}:chats.find(c=>c.otherUid===id||c.id===id)).filter(Boolean);
 const admins=(current.adminIds||[fbUser?.uid]).map(id=>id===fbUser?.uid?{id:'me',name:'You',initial:'Y',online:true}:members.find(m=>m.otherUid===id||m.id===id)).filter(Boolean);
 const avatar=current.avatar?`<img class="groupDetailAvatar" src="${escapeHtml(current.avatar)}" alt="Group GIF/profile">`:`<div class="groupDetailAvatar" style="display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:34px;font-weight:800">${escapeHtml(current.initial)}</div>`;
 openPage('groupInfo',{avatar,name:current.name,about:current.about||'No description',type:current.groupType||'private',members,admins});
}

renderChats();renderMessages();

/* ===== KP WHATSAPP FINAL HARDENING PATCH ===== */
const KPServerTS=()=>firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
function kpTime(v){try{return v?.toDate?v.toDate().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):''}catch(e){return ''}}
function kpMillis(v){try{return v?.toMillis?v.toMillis():0}catch(e){return 0}}
function kpMessageKind(m){const me=m.senderId===fbUser?.uid; if(m.type==='system')return 'system'; if(m.type==='gif')return me?'me-gif':'gif'; if(['image','audio','file'].includes(m.type))return me?'me-media':'media'; return me?'me':'in'}
function kpMessageArray(d){const m=d.data(),kind=kpMessageKind(m),time=kpTime(m.createdAt);if(kind==='system')return ['system',m.text||'',time,'','',{id:d.id,raw:m}];let payload=m.media?JSON.stringify({...m.media,caption:m.caption||'',reply:m.replyTo?.text||''}):m.text||'';const me=m.senderId===fbUser?.uid;const state=m.readBy?.includes(fbUser?.uid)?'read':m.deliveredTo?.includes(fbUser?.uid)?'delivered':me?'sent':null;return [kind,payload,time,m.replyTo?.text||'',state,{id:d.id,raw:m}]}

async function markChatRead(c){
 if(!fbUser||!c?.firestoreId)return;
 try{await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId).set({[`unreadCount.${fbUser.uid}`]:0},{merge:true});c.unread=0;renderChats();}catch(e){console.warn('mark read',e)}
}

async function loadFirebaseChats(){
 if(!fbUser||!fbDb)return;
 try{
  const snap=await fbDb.collection('chats').where('memberIds','array-contains',fbUser.uid).get();
  const remote=[];
  for(const d of snap.docs){
   const x=d.data();
   if(!x.memberIds?.includes(fbUser.uid))continue;
   if(x.type==='group'){
    remote.push({id:d.id,firestoreId:d.id,name:x.name||'Group',initial:(x.name||'G')[0].toUpperCase(),preview:x.lastMessage||'No messages yet',time:kpTime(x.lastMessageAt),sortTime:kpMillis(x.lastMessageAt)||kpMillis(x.createdAt),online:false,unread:x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0,messageCount:Number(x.messageCount||0),color:'green',group:true,groupType:x.groupType||'private',avatar:x.avatar||'',avatarType:x.avatarType||'initial',about:x.about||'',adminIds:x.adminIds||[],memberIds:x.memberIds||[],messages:[],startedAt:x.createdAt?.toDate?x.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleDateString():'Today'});
   }else{
    const other=(x.memberIds||[]).find(id=>id!==fbUser.uid);let u={};
    if(other){const us=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(other).get();if(us.exists)u=us.data();}
    let online=false;if(other){try{online=(await fbRtdb.ref('presence/'+other).once('value')).val()?.state==='online'}catch(e){}}
    remote.push({id:d.id,firestoreId:d.id,name:u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber||u.mobileNumber||'Unknown user',initial:(u.displayName||u.firstName||u.phoneNumber||'U')[0].toUpperCase(),preview:x.lastMessage||'No messages yet',time:kpTime(x.lastMessageAt),sortTime:kpMillis(x.lastMessageAt)||kpMillis(x.createdAt),online,unread:x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0,color:'green',group:false,messages:[],peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:other,phone:u.mobileNumber?'+91 '+u.mobileNumber:'KP '+(u.phoneNumber||''),photoURL:u.photoURL||'',startedAt:x.createdAt?.toDate?x.createdAt.toDate().toLocaleDateString():'Today'});
   }
  }
  remote.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));
  chats.splice(0,chats.length,...remote);renderChats();
  if(current&&!chats.some(c=>c.id===current.id))current=null;
  if(current)await loadFirebaseMessages(current);
 }catch(e){console.warn('load chats',e);renderChats()}
}

async function loadFirebaseMessages(c){
 if(!c?.firestoreId||!fbDb)return;
 try{
  const snap=await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId).collection('messages').orderBy('createdAt','asc').limit(500).get();
  c.messages=snap.docs.map(kpMessageArray);
  if(c.group){
   const chatSnap=await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId).get();
   if(chatSnap.exists){const x=chatSnap.data();c.messageCount=Number(x.messageCount||snap.size);c.preview=x.lastMessage||c.preview;c.sortTime=kpMillis(x.lastMessageAt)||c.sortTime;c.time=kpTime(x.lastMessageAt)||c.time}
  }
  if(current===c){renderMessages();await markChatRead(c)} renderChats();
 }catch(e){console.warn('load messages',e)}
}

function startFirebaseMessageListener(c){
 if(fbUnsubMessages){try{fbUnsubMessages()}catch(e){}fbUnsubMessages=null}
 if(!fbUser||!c?.firestoreId)return;
 fbUnsubMessages=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId).collection('messages').orderBy('createdAt','asc').limit(500).onSnapshot(async s=>{
  c.messages=s.docs.map(kpMessageArray);
  const chatSnap=await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId).get().catch(()=>null);
  if(chatSnap?.exists){const x=chatSnap.data();c.preview=x.lastMessage||c.preview;c.sortTime=kpMillis(x.lastMessageAt)||c.sortTime;c.time=kpTime(x.lastMessageAt)||c.time;c.unread=x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0;if(c.group)c.messageCount=Number(x.messageCount||s.size)}
  const idx=chats.findIndex(x=>x.id===c.id);if(idx>=0)chats[idx]=c;
  renderMessages();renderChats();
  if(current===c)await markChatRead(c);
 }).bind(null);
}

async function ensurePrivateChat(otherUid,otherName='',otherPeerId=''){
 if(!fbUser||!otherUid||otherUid===fbUser.uid)return null;
 const ids=[fbUser.uid,otherUid].sort(),id=ids.join('_'),ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(id);
 const snap=await ref.get();
 if(!snap.exists){const unread={};unread[fbUser.uid]=0;unread[otherUid]=0;await ref.set({type:'private',memberIds:ids,createdAt:KPServerTS(),lastMessage:'',lastMessageAt:KPServerTS(),lastMessageSenderId:'',unreadCount:unread,peerIds:{[fbUser.uid]:peerId||'',[otherUid]:otherPeerId||''}})}
 return id;
}

async function persistFirestoreMessage(type,payload,text='',caption='',replyText=''){
 if(!fbUser||!current)return false;
 let chatId=current.firestoreId||current.id;
 if(!chatId&&current.otherUid){chatId=await ensurePrivateChat(current.otherUid,current.name,current.peerId);current.firestoreId=chatId}
 if(!chatId)return false;
 const ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(chatId),chatSnap=await ref.get();if(!chatSnap.exists)throw new Error('Chat does not exist');
 const data=chatSnap.data(),members=data.memberIds||[fbUser.uid];
 const msgRef=ref.collection('messages').doc();
 const message={senderId:fbUser.uid,type,text:text||'',createdAt:KPServerTS(),edited:false,deleted:false,replyTo:replyText?{text:replyText}:null,media:payload||null,caption:caption||'',reactions:{},deliveredTo:[fbUser.uid],readBy:[fbUser.uid]};
 await msgRef.set(message);
 const updates={lastMessage:type==='text'?text:(type==='gif'?'GIF':'Media'),lastMessageAt:KPServerTS(),lastMessageSenderId:fbUser.uid};
 const unread={...(data.unreadCount||{})};
 for(const uid of members){if(uid!==fbUser.uid)unread[uid]=firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(1)}
 updates.unreadCount=unread;
 if(data.type==='group')updates.messageCount=firebase.firestore.FieldValue.increment(1);
 await ref.set(updates,{merge:true});
 current.sortTime=Date.now();current.time=new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});current.preview=updates.lastMessage;
 return msgRef.id;
}

async function persistTextMessage(text){if(!text||!current)return false;await persistFirestoreMessage('text',null,text);return true}

async function sendMessage(){
 const input=document.getElementById('messageInput'),t=input?.value.trim();if(!t||!current)return;
 input.value='';try{await persistTextMessage(t);toast('Message sent')}catch(e){console.error(e);input.value=t;toast('Message could not be sent')}
}

async function sendPendingGif(){
 if(!pendingGifUrl||!current)return;
 const caption=document.getElementById('gifCaption')?.value.trim()||'',reply=document.getElementById('gifReply')?.value.trim()||'',url=pendingGifUrl;
 try{await persistFirestoreMessage('gif',{url},'',caption,reply);pendingGifUrl=null;closeModal();toast('GIF sent')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('GIF could not be sent')}
}

async function sendPendingMedia(){
 if(!pendingMediaFile||!current)return;
 const caption=document.getElementById('mediaCaption')?.value.trim()||'',reply=document.getElementById('mediaReply')?.value.trim()||'';
 try{toast('Uploading…');const u=await cloudinaryUpload(pendingMediaFile);const type=pendingMediaFile.type?.startsWith('image/')?'image':pendingMediaFile.type?.startsWith('audio/')?'audio':'file';await persistFirestoreMessage(type,{url:u.secure_url,name:pendingMediaFile.name,type:pendingMediaFile.type,bytes:pendingMediaFile.size},'',caption,reply);cleanupPending();closeModal();toast('Media sent')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Upload failed')}
}

async function deleteMessage(messageId,idx){
 if(!fbUser||!current?.firestoreId||!messageId)return;
 const m=current.messages?.[idx],raw=m?.[5]?.raw;if(!raw)return;
 if(raw.senderId!==fbUser.uid){toast('You can delete only your own messages');return}
 if(!confirm('Delete this message for everyone?'))return;
 try{await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId).collection('messages').doc(messageId).delete();toast('Message deleted')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not delete this message')}
}

function renderMessages(){
 const list=current?.messages||[];
 const html=list.map((m,idx)=>{
  const kind=m[0],time=m[2]||'',isMe=['me','me-gif','me-media'].includes(kind),state=m[4]||(isMe?'read':null),docId=m[5]?.id||'';
  if(kind==='system')return `<div class="systemMessage"><span>${escapeHtml(m[1]||'')}</span></div>`;
  let payload={},content='',caption='',reply='';
  if(kind==='gif'||kind==='me-gif'){try{payload=typeof m[1]==='string'&&m[1].startsWith('{')?JSON.parse(m[1]):{url:m[1]}}catch(e){payload={url:m[1]}}caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||'';content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" loading="lazy" alt="GIF"></div>`}
  else if(kind==='media'||kind==='me-media'){try{payload=JSON.parse(m[1])}catch(e){payload={}}caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||'';if((payload.type||'').startsWith('image/'))content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" loading="lazy" alt="${escapeHtml(payload.name||'image')}"></div>`;else if((payload.type||'').startsWith('audio/'))content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><audio controls src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}"></audio></div>`;else content=`<div class="fileCard"><span>📎</span><a href="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" style="color:#b8ff22;text-decoration:none;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${escapeHtml(payload.name||'Download file')}</a></div>`}
  else{content=`<div class="txt">${escapeHtml(m[1]||'')}</div>`;reply=m[3]||''}
  const quote=reply?`<div class="replyQuote">↩ ${escapeHtml(reply)}</div>`:'';const cap=caption?`<div class="msgMediaCaption">${escapeHtml(caption)}</div>`:'';const status=isMe?deliveryIcon(state):'';const del=isMe&&docId?`<button class="msgDeleteBtn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();deleteMessage('${docId}',${idx})" title="Delete for everyone" aria-label="Delete message"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 7h16M9 7V4h6v3M7 7l1 13h8l1-13M10 11v6M14 11v6"/></svg></button>`:'';
  return `<div class="msg ${isMe?'me':'in'} ${kind.includes('media')||kind.includes('gif')?'fileMsg':''}">${quote}${content}${cap}<div class="meta">${escapeHtml(time)} ${status}${del}</div></div>`;
 }).join('');
 const box=document.getElementById('messages');if(!box)return;
 box.innerHTML='<div class="love-fx" id="loveFx"></div><div class="chatStarted">Chat started · '+escapeHtml(current?.startedAt||'Today')+'</div><div class="day">TODAY</div>'+html;
 box.scrollTop=box.scrollHeight;
 const hn=document.getElementById('headName'),hs=document.getElementById('headStatus'),ha=document.getElementById('headAvatar');if(hn)hn.textContent=current?.name||'';
 if(hs)hs.textContent=current?.group?((current?.memberIds||[]).length+' members'):(current?.online?'online':'last seen recently');
 if(ha){if(current?.group&&current?.avatar){ha.innerHTML=`<img src="${escapeHtml(current.avatar)}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:50%" alt="">`}else{ha.textContent=current?.initial||'U'}}
}

async function openChat(id){
 const c=chats.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));if(!c)return;current=c;c.unread=0;renderChats();document.getElementById('app').classList.add('chat-open');await loadFirebaseMessages(c);startFirebaseMessageListener(c);await markChatRead(c);renderMessages();
}

async function finishCreateGroup(){
 if(!fbUser||!fbDb){toast('Please sign in first');return}
 const name=(groupDraft.name||document.getElementById('groupName')?.value||'New Group').trim()||'New Group';
 const about=(groupDraft.about||document.getElementById('groupAbout')?.value||'').trim();const type=groupDraft.type||groupType||'public';
 let avatar=groupDraft.avatar||groupAvatarUrl||'';const memberIds=[...new Set([fbUser.uid,...(selectedGroupMembers||[]).filter(id=>id&&id!==fbUser.uid)])];
 toast('Creating group…');
 try{
  if(avatar.startsWith('data:')){const blob=await (await fetch(avatar)).blob();const f=new File([blob],avatar.includes('image/gif')?'group.gif':'group-avatar.jpg',{type:blob.type||'image/jpeg'});avatar=(await cloudinaryUpload(f)).secure_url}
  const ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(),unread={};memberIds.forEach(id=>unread[id]=0);const welcome=`Welcome to ${name}! You are now in this group.`;const ts=KPServerTS();
  await ref.set({type:'group',name,about,groupType:type,avatar,avatarType:avatar?'gif':'initial',memberIds,adminIds:[fbUser.uid],createdBy:fbUser.uid,createdAt:ts,lastMessage:welcome,lastMessageAt:ts,lastMessageSenderId:fbUser.uid,messageCount:1,unreadCount:unread});
  await ref.collection('messages').doc().set({senderId:fbUser.uid,type:'system',text:welcome,createdAt:ts,edited:false,deleted:false,replyTo:null,media:null,readBy:[fbUser.uid],deliveredTo:memberIds});
  const c={id:ref.id,firestoreId:ref.id,name,initial:name[0]?.toUpperCase()||'G',preview:welcome,time:new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}),sortTime:Date.now(),online:false,unread:0,messageCount:1,color:'green',group:true,groupType:type,avatar,avatarType:avatar?'gif':'initial',about,adminIds:[fbUser.uid],memberIds,messages:[['system',welcome,new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}),'','',{id:'welcome',raw:{senderId:fbUser.uid,type:'system'}}]],startedAt:'Today'};
  chats.unshift(c);current=c;groupAvatarUrl='';selectedGroupMembers=[];groupDraft={name:'',about:'',type:'public',avatar:''};closeModal();renderChats();openChat(c.id);toast('Group created successfully');
 }catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Group creation failed: '+(e.message||''))}
}

 pendingGifUrl=url;openGifSendPreview(url);
}

/* Stronger unique 6-digit reservation. A Firestore transaction is the source of truth. */
async function completeFirebaseProfile(){
 setProfileError('');const name=document.getElementById('profileName')?.value.trim(),phone=(document.getElementById('profilePhone')?.value||'').replace(/\D/g,''),mobile=(document.getElementById('profileMobile')?.value||'').replace(/\D/g,''),age=Number(document.getElementById('profileAge')?.value),pass=document.getElementById('profilePassword')?.value||'';
 if(!name)return setProfileError('Enter your name.');if(!/^\d{6}$/.test(phone))return setProfileError('Enter a unique 6-digit KP number.');if(mobile&&!/^\d{10}$/.test(mobile))return setProfileError('Enter a valid 10-digit mobile number.');if(!Number.isInteger(age)||age<13||age>120)return setProfileError('Enter a valid age.');if(pass.length<6)return setProfileError('Password must be at least 6 characters.');
 let createdAuth=false;
 try{
  const numberRef=fbDb.collection('phoneNumbers').doc(phone);let existing=(await numberRef.get()).data();
  if(existing?.uid&&existing.uid!==fbUser?.uid)return setProfileError('This 6-digit number is already in use. Choose another number.');
  if(!fbUser){const cred=await fbAuth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(kpAuthEmail(phone),pass);fbUser=cred.user;createdAuth=true}else if(!fbUser.providerData.some(p=>p.providerId==='password')){await fbUser.linkWithCredential(firebase.auth.EmailAuthProvider.credential(kpAuthEmail(phone),pass))}else{await fbUser.updatePassword(pass)}
  try{await fbDb.runTransaction(async tx=>{const s=await tx.get(numberRef);if(s.exists&&s.data().uid!==fbUser.uid)throw new Error('KP_NUMBER_TAKEN');tx.set(numberRef,{uid:fbUser.uid,email:kpAuthEmail(phone),updatedAt:KPServerTS()},{merge:true})})}catch(e){if(createdAuth){await fbUser.delete().catch(()=>{});fbUser=null}throw e}
  const ref=fbDb.collection('users').doc(fbUser.uid),snap=await ref.get(),old=snap.exists?snap.data():{};if(old.phoneNumber&&old.phoneNumber!==phone)await fbDb.collection('phoneNumbers').doc(old.phoneNumber).delete().catch(()=>{});
  const data={uid:fbUser.uid,firstName:name.split(/\s+/)[0],lastName:name.split(/\s+/).slice(1).join(' '),displayName:name,phoneCountryCode:'+91',phoneNumber:phone,mobileNumber:mobile,age,email:fbUser.email||old.email||kpAuthEmail(phone),photoURL:fbUser.photoURL||old.photoURL||'',avatarType:fbUser.photoURL?'google':'svg',bio:old.bio||'',peerId:peerId||old.peerId||'',createdAt:old.createdAt||KPServerTS(),updatedAt:KPServerTS(),lastSeen:KPServerTS(),privacy:old.privacy||{lastSeen:'everyone',profilePhoto:'everyone',about:'everyone',readReceipts:true},settings:old.settings||{notifications:true,sounds:true,darkMode:true},blockedUsers:old.blockedUsers||[]};
  await ref.set(data,{merge:true});fbUserProfile=data;document.getElementById('authGate').classList.add('hidden');document.getElementById('app').classList.remove('authLocked');await startFirebasePresence();initPeer();await loadFirebaseChats();toast('Account ready');
 }catch(e){console.error(e);setProfileError(e?.message==='KP_NUMBER_TAKEN'?'This 6-digit number is already in use. Choose another number.':firebaseAuthMessage(e));}
}



/* FINAL REAL-DATA OVERRIDES */
function openPage(type,extra=null){if(type==='calls'){openCallsPage();return}if(type==='settings'){const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');pi.innerHTML=`<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Settings</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">KP WhatsApp account</div></div></div><div class="pageCard pageList" onclick="openAccountPage()"><div class="pageIcon">👤</div><div><b>Account</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Google account, number and sign out</div></div></div><div class="pageCard pageList" onclick="toast('Privacy settings')"><div class="pageIcon">⌾</div><div><b>Privacy</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Last seen, profile and read receipts</div></div></div><div class="pageCard pageList" onclick="toast('Notifications settings')"><div class="pageIcon">♢</div><div><b>Notifications</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Message and call alerts</div></div></div>`;pv.classList.add('show');return}return window.__oldOpenPage?window.__oldOpenPage(type,extra):void 0}
function startVoiceCall(){if(!current||current.group){toast('Voice calls are 1-on-1 only');return}const rid=current.peerId||current.recipientPeerId;if(!rid){toast('This user is not ready for voice calling');return}if(!peer||!peer.open){toast('Voice calling is still connecting…');return}showCallModal('outgoing',current.name,current.initial||'A');ensureLocalAudio().then(stream=>{localStream=stream;activeCall=peer.call(rid,stream,{metadata:{uid:fbUser.uid,name:fbUserProfile?.displayName||fbUser?.displayName||'KP User',phone:fbUserProfile?.phoneNumber||'',photoURL:fbUserProfile?.photoURL||'',peerId}});if(!activeCall)throw new Error('Call failed');logCall('calling');bindCall(activeCall,current.name,current.initial||'A')}).catch(e=>{console.error(e);stopLocalStream();hideCallModal();toast('Could not start voice call')})}
function handleIncomingCall(call){if(activeCall||incomingCall){try{call.close()}catch(e){}return}incomingCall=call;const meta=call.metadata||{};const callerName=meta.name||'Incoming caller';showCallModal('incoming',callerName,(callerName[0]||'U').toUpperCase());document.getElementById('callSub').textContent='Incoming call';document.getElementById('callPeer').textContent=meta.phone?'+91 '+meta.phone:'';playRingtone()}
function showCallModal(type,name,initial){const o=document.getElementById('callOverlay');document.getElementById('callAvatar').textContent=initial||'A';document.getElementById('callTitle').textContent=name||'Voice call';const sub=document.getElementById('callSub'),a=document.getElementById('callActions'),timer=document.getElementById('callTimer');timer.textContent='';if(type==='incoming'){sub.textContent='Ringing…';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction accept" onclick="acceptIncomingCall()" title="Answer"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 4.5 9 4l2 4-2 2a13 13 0 0 0 5 5l2-2 4 2-.5 3a2 2 0 0 1-2.2 1.5C9.5 18.2 5.8 14.5 4.5 6.7A2 2 0 0 1 6 4.5Z"/></svg></button><button class="callAction end" onclick="rejectIncomingCall()" title="Reject"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}else if(type==='outgoing'){sub.textContent='Calling…';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction end" onclick="endVoiceCall()" title="End call"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}else{sub.textContent='Connected';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction" id="muteCallBtn" onclick="toggleMute()" title="Mute"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 3a3 3 0 0 0-3 3v6a3 3 0 0 0 6 0V6a3 3 0 0 0-3-3Z"/><path d="M19 10v2a7 7 0 0 1-14 0v-2M12 19v3M8 22h8"/></svg></button><button class="callAction" onclick="appendCallNote()" title="Notes"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 4h12v16H6z"/><path d="M9 8h6M9 12h6M9 16h4"/></svg></button><button class="callAction end" onclick="endVoiceCall()" title="End call"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}o.classList.add('show')}
function initPeer(){if(typeof Peer==='undefined'){toast('PeerJS could not load');return}try{peer=new Peer(getMyPeerId(),{debug:1});peer.on('open',async id=>{peerId=id;window.KP_PEER_ID=id;if(fbUser)try{await fbDb.collection('users').doc(fbUser.uid).set({peerId:id,updatedAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true})}catch(e){}});peer.on('call',handleIncomingCall);peer.on('error',err=>{if(err?.type==='unavailable-id'){localStorage.removeItem('kpwa_peer_id');setTimeout(initPeer,500)}else console.warn('PeerJS',err)});peer.on('disconnected',()=>{try{peer.reconnect()}catch(e){}})}catch(e){console.error(e)}}


/* =========================================================
   KP WHATSAPP — USERNAME ONLY FINAL OVERRIDES
   No phone numbers / no Google login / no OTP.
   ========================================================= */
const KP_USERNAME_RE=/^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{3,32}$/;
function kpUsername(v){return String(v||'').trim().toLowerCase()}
function kpUsernameEmail(u){return `${kpUsername(u)}@kpwa-user.invalid`}
function shuffleKP(a){const x=[...a];for(let i=x.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[x[i],x[j]]=[x[j],x[i]]}return x}

function authBackToLogin(){
  document.getElementById('profileSetup')?.classList.remove('show');
  document.getElementById('loginPanel')?.classList.remove('hide');
  setProfileError('');setAuthError('');
}
function openCreateAccount(){
  document.getElementById('loginPanel')?.classList.add('hide');
  document.getElementById('profileSetup')?.classList.add('show');
  const ids=['profileUsername','profileName','profileAge','profilePassword'];ids.forEach(id=>{const e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=''});
  setProfileError('');
}

async function firebaseUsernameLogin(){
  setAuthError('');
  const username=kpUsername(document.getElementById('loginUsername')?.value);
  const pass=document.getElementById('passwordAuth')?.value||'';
  if(!KP_USERNAME_RE.test(username)){setAuthError('Enter a valid username (3–32 letters, numbers, . _ or -).');return}
  if(!pass){setAuthError('Enter your password.');return}
  try{
    const snap=await fbDb.collection('usernames').doc(username).get();
    if(!snap.exists){setAuthError('No account exists for this username.');return}
    const data=snap.data()||{};
    await fbAuth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(data.email||kpUsernameEmail(username),pass);
  }catch(e){console.error(e);setAuthError(e?.code==='auth/wrong-password'||e?.code==='auth/invalid-credential'?'Incorrect username or password.':firebaseAuthMessage(e))}
}

async function completeUsernameProfile(){
  setProfileError('');
  const username=kpUsername(document.getElementById('profileUsername')?.value);
  const name=document.getElementById('profileName')?.value.trim();
  const age=Number(document.getElementById('profileAge')?.value);
  const pass=document.getElementById('profilePassword')?.value||'';
  if(!KP_USERNAME_RE.test(username))return setProfileError('Username must be 3–32 characters: letters, numbers, . _ or -.');
  if(!name)return setProfileError('Enter your name.');
  if(!Number.isInteger(age)||age<13||age>120)return setProfileError('Enter a valid age.');
  if(pass.length<6)return setProfileError('Password must be at least 6 characters.');
  let cred=null;
  try{
    const usernameRef=fbDb.collection('usernames').doc(username);
    const taken=await usernameRef.get();
    if(taken.exists)return setProfileError('That username is already in use. Choose another one.');
    cred=await fbAuth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(kpUsernameEmail(username),pass);
    fbUser=cred.user;
    const uid=fbUser.uid;
    await fbDb.runTransaction(async tx=>{
      const check=await tx.get(usernameRef);
      if(check.exists)throw new Error('USERNAME_TAKEN');
      tx.set(usernameRef,{uid,email:kpUsernameEmail(username),username,createdAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()});
      tx.set(fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid),{
        uid,username,firstName:name.split(/\s+/)[0],lastName:name.split(/\s+/).slice(1).join(' '),displayName:name,age,
        email:kpUsernameEmail(username),photoURL:'',avatarType:'initial',bio:'',peerId:'',
        createdAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),updatedAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),lastSeen:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        privacy:{lastSeen:'everyone',profilePhoto:'everyone',about:'everyone',readReceipts:true},settings:{notifications:true,sounds:true,darkMode:true},blockedUsers:[]
      });
    });
    fbUserProfile=(await fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid).get()).data();
    document.getElementById('authGate').classList.add('hidden');document.getElementById('app').classList.remove('authLocked');
    await startFirebasePresence();initPeer();await loadFirebaseChats();renderChats();toast('Account created successfully');
  }catch(e){
    console.error(e);
    if(e.message==='USERNAME_TAKEN'){if(fbUser){await fbUser.delete().catch(()=>{})}fbUser=null;setProfileError('That username is already in use. Choose another one.');return}
    if(e.code==='auth/email-already-in-use')return setProfileError('That username is already in use. Choose another one.');
    setProfileError(firebaseAuthMessage(e)||e.message||'Could not create account.');
  }
}

/* Remove any legacy Google/number buttons if an older cached DOM survives. */
function removeLegacyAuthNodes(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.googleBtn,#loginPhone,#profilePhone,#profileMobile').forEach(el=>{const wrap=el.closest('.passwordWrap')||el;wrap.remove()});
  document.querySelectorAll('.authDivider').forEach(e=>e.remove());
  document.querySelectorAll('.authHint').forEach(e=>{if(e.closest('#authGate'))e.textContent='Username + password only.'});
}

async function openRealUsers(){
  if(!fbUser){toast('Please sign in first');return}
  const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');
  pi.innerHTML=`<div class="newChatFull"><div class="newChatTop"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div class="newChatTitle"><b>Start a New Chat</b><span>Real KP WhatsApp users</span></div></div><div class="newChatSearch"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"/><path d="m16 16 5 5"/></svg><input class="field" id="userSearch" placeholder="Search username or name" oninput="filterRealUsers()"></div><div class="usernameHint">Users appear in a changing order. The list is shuffled instead of sorted by account creation.</div><div class="newChatUsers" id="realUserList"><div style="padding:18px;color:#777">Loading users…</div></div></div>`;
  pv.classList.add('show');
  await loadRealUsers();
}
let realUsers=[];
async function loadRealUsers(){
  if(!fbUser)return;
  try{
    const snap=await fbDb.collection('users').get();
    realUsers=[];snap.forEach(d=>{if(d.id!==fbUser.uid){const u=d.data()||{};realUsers.push({...u,uid:d.id})}});
    realUsers=shuffleKP(realUsers);renderRealUsers(realUsers);
  }catch(e){console.error(e);const b=document.getElementById('realUserList');if(b)b.innerHTML='<div style="padding:18px;color:#ff6677">Could not load users. Check Firestore Rules.</div>'}
}
function renderRealUsers(list){
  const box=document.getElementById('realUserList');if(!box)return;
  box.innerHTML=list.map(u=>`<div class="userRow" onclick="startChatWithUser('${u.uid}')"><div class="userAvatar">${u.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(u.photoURL)}">`:escapeHtml((u.displayName||u.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase())}</div><div class="userMeta"><b>${escapeHtml(u.displayName||'Unknown user')}</b><small>@${escapeHtml(u.username||'unknown')} · ${u.online?'Online':'KP WhatsApp user'}</small></div><span class="randomBadge">${u.online?'ONLINE':'USER'}</span></div>`).join('')||'<div style="padding:18px;color:#777">No other real accounts yet.</div>';
}
function filterRealUsers(){
  const q=kpUsername(document.getElementById('userSearch')?.value);
  const filtered=realUsers.filter(u=>(u.username||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(u.displayName||'').toLowerCase().includes(q));
  renderRealUsers(filtered);
}
async function startChatWithUser(uid){
  const u=realUsers.find(x=>x.uid===uid);if(!u||uid===fbUser?.uid)return;
  try{
    const chatId=await ensurePrivateChat(uid,u.displayName||u.username||'User',u.peerId||'');
    let c=chats.find(x=>x.firestoreId===chatId);
    if(!c){c={id:chatId,firestoreId:chatId,name:u.displayName||u.username,initial:(u.displayName||u.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase(),preview:'No messages yet',time:'',sortTime:Date.now(),online:!!u.online,unread:0,color:'green',group:false,messages:[],peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:uid,username:u.username||'',photoURL:u.photoURL||'',startedAt:'Today'};chats.push(c)}
    else{Object.assign(c,{name:u.displayName||u.username,initial:(u.displayName||u.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase(),peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:uid,username:u.username||'',photoURL:u.photoURL||'',online:!!u.online})}
    renderChats();closePage();openChat(c.id);await loadFirebaseMessages(c);
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not open this chat. Check Firestore Rules.')}
}

/* Rebuild chat rows with username, no phone-number fallback. */
function renderChats(){
  const list=document.getElementById('chatList');if(!list)return;
  const q=(document.getElementById('search')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
  const arr=chats.filter(c=>(activeTab==='all'||(activeTab==='unread'&&c.unread)||(activeTab==='groups'&&c.group))&&((c.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(c.username||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)));
  arr.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));
  list.innerHTML=arr.map(c=>{const av=c.avatar?`<img src="${escapeHtml(c.avatar)}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover">`:(c.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(c.photoURL)}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover">`:escapeHtml(c.initial||'U'));return `<div class="chatRow ${current?.id===c.id?'selected':''}" onclick="openChat('${c.id}')"><div class="avatar green">${av}</div><div class="chatMeta"><div class="chatName"><b>${escapeHtml(c.name||'User')}</b>${c.username&&!c.group?`<small style="display:block;color:#777">@${escapeHtml(c.username)}</small>`:''}</div><div class="chatPreview">${escapeHtml(c.preview||'')}</div></div><div class="chatSide"><small>${escapeHtml(c.time||'')}</small>${c.unread?`<span class="unread">${c.unread}</span>`:''}${c.group&&c.messageCount?`<span class="unread">${c.messageCount}</span>`:''}</div></div>`}).join('')||'<div class="empty">No chats yet.<br>Tap + to start a new chat.</div>';
}

/* Contact info/profile uses username instead of phone. */

  if(!person)return;
  const shared=chats.filter(g=>g.group&&(g.memberIds||[]).includes(person.id)&&(g.memberIds||[]).includes(fbUser?.uid));
  const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');
  pi.innerHTML=`<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Profile</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Contact information</div></div><button class="iconBtn" onclick="openProfileEdit()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 17.5-.5 3 3-.5L19 7.5 16.5 5zM14.5 7 17 9.5"/></svg></button></div><div class="pageCard profileHero"><div class="profileAvatarBig">${person.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(person.photoURL)}">`:escapeHtml((person.name||person.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase())}</div><h2>${escapeHtml(person.name||'User')}</h2><div class="profileUsernameBadge">@${escapeHtml(person.username||'unknown')}</div><small style="color:#888;margin-top:5px">${person.online?'Online':'Offline'}</small></div><div class="pageCard profileActions"><button class="secondary" onclick="startChatWithUser('${person.id}')">Message</button><button class="secondary" onclick="startVoiceCall()">Audio call</button></div><div class="pageCard"><b>Groups in common</b>${shared.length?shared.map(g=>`<div class="memberRow" onclick="openChat('${g.id}')"><div class="miniAvatar">${escapeHtml(g.initial)}</div><div><b>${escapeHtml(g.name)}</b><small>${(g.memberIds||[]).length} members</small></div></div>`).join(''):'<div style="color:#777;padding-top:10px">No groups in common</div>'}</div><button class="secondary" onclick="clearChatFor('${person.id}')">Clear chat</button><button class="secondary danger" onclick="blockContact('${person.id}')">Block</button><button class="secondary danger" onclick="toast('Report submitted')">Report</button>`;
  pv.classList.add('show');
}

async function callByUsername(){
  const username=kpUsername(document.getElementById('callUsernameInput')?.value);const out=document.getElementById('callLookupResult');
  if(!KP_USERNAME_RE.test(username)){if(out)out.innerHTML='<div style="color:#ff6878">Enter a valid username.</div>';return}
  try{const s=await fbDb.collection('usernames').doc(username).get();if(!s.exists){if(out)out.innerHTML='<div style="color:#ff6878">No user found with that username.</div>';return}const u=(await fbDb.collection('users').doc(s.data().uid).get()).data();if(!u||u.uid===fbUser.uid){if(out)out.innerHTML='<div style="color:#ff6878">That username is not available for calling.</div>';return}await startChatWithUser(u.uid);setTimeout(()=>{current={...current,peerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:u.uid,name:u.displayName||username,username};startVoiceCall()},250)}catch(e){console.error(e);if(out)out.innerHTML='<div style="color:#ff6878">Could not find this user.</div>'}
}
async function logCall(status){if(!fbUser||!current?.otherUid)return;try{await fbDb.collection('calls').add({callerId:fbUser.uid,receiverId:current.otherUid,participants:[fbUser.uid,current.otherUid],type:'voice',status,createdAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),otherName:current.name,otherUsername:current.username||''})}catch(e){console.warn(e)}}
async function loadCallHistory(){const box=document.getElementById('callHistory');if(!box||!fbUser)return;try{const snap=await fbDb.collection('calls').where('participants','array-contains',fbUser.uid).limit(20).get();box.innerHTML=snap.docs.map(d=>{const c=d.data();return `<div class="memberRow"><div class="miniAvatar">☎</div><div style="flex:1"><b>${escapeHtml(c.otherName||'Unknown user')}</b><small>@${escapeHtml(c.otherUsername||'unknown')} · ${escapeHtml(c.status||'call')}</small></div></div>`}).join('')||'<div style="color:#777;padding-top:10px">No calls yet.</div>'}catch(e){box.innerHTML='<div style="color:#777;padding-top:10px">No call history yet.</div>'}}

function openPage(type,extra=null){
  if(type==='calls'){openCallsPage();return}
  if(type==='profile'){openProfilePage();return}
  if(type==='settings'){
    const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');
    pi.innerHTML=`<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Settings</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">KP WhatsApp</div></div></div><div class="pageCard pageList" onclick="openProfilePage()"><div class="pageIcon">◎</div><div><b>Profile</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Photo, name, username and bio</div></div></div><div class="pageCard pageList" onclick="toast('Privacy settings are ready for the next section')"><div class="pageIcon">⌾</div><div><b>Privacy</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Profile visibility and read receipts</div></div></div><div class="pageCard pageList" onclick="toast('Notification settings are ready for the next section')"><div class="pageIcon">♢</div><div><b>Notifications</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Messages and calls</div></div></div><div class="pageCard"><b>Account</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px;margin-top:5px">@${escapeHtml(fbUserProfile?.username||'')}</div><button class="secondary" style="margin-top:10px" onclick="firebaseLogout()">Sign out</button><button class="secondary accountDanger" onclick="deleteMyAccount()">Delete account</button></div>`;pv.classList.add('show');return
  }
  if(window.__oldOpenPage) return window.__oldOpenPage(type,extra);
}

function nav(btn,type){
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav button').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));btn?.classList.add('active');
  if(type==='chats'){closePage();closeChat();return}
  if(type==='calls'){openCallsPage();return}
  if(type==='profile'){openProfilePage();return}
  if(type==='status'){openPage('status');return}
}

function closePage(){document.getElementById('pageView')?.classList.remove('show')}

async function deleteMyAccount(){
  if(!fbUser)return;const ok=confirm('Delete your KP WhatsApp account?');if(!ok)return;
  try{
    const uid=fbUser.uid,username=fbUserProfile?.username;
    if(username)await fbDb.collection('usernames').doc(username).delete().catch(()=>{});
    await fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid).delete();
    await fbUser.delete();
    location.reload();
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast('For security, sign in again and retry account deletion.')}
}

function startVoiceCall(){
  if(!current||current.group){toast('Voice calls are 1-on-1 only');return}
  const rid=current.peerId||current.recipientPeerId;if(!rid){toast('This user is not online for voice calling yet.');return}
  if(!peer||!peer.open){toast('Voice calling is still connecting…');return}
  showCallModal('outgoing',current.name,current.initial||'A');
  ensureLocalAudio().then(stream=>{localStream=stream;activeCall=peer.call(rid,stream,{metadata:{uid:fbUser.uid,name:fbUserProfile?.displayName||'KP User',username:fbUserProfile?.username||'',peerId}});if(!activeCall)throw new Error('Call failed');bindCall(activeCall,current.name,current.initial||'A');logCall('calling')}).catch(e=>{console.error(e);stopLocalStream();hideCallModal();toast('Could not start voice call')})
}
function handleIncomingCall(call){if(activeCall||incomingCall){try{call.close()}catch(e){}return}incomingCall=call;const meta=call.metadata||{};const callerName=meta.name||'Incoming caller';showCallModal('incoming',callerName,(callerName[0]||'U').toUpperCase());document.getElementById('callPeer').textContent=meta.username?'@'+meta.username:'';playRingtone()}

/* Keep current user profile synced after Firebase auth. */
if(typeof fbAuth!=='undefined'&&fbAuth){fbAuth.onAuthStateChanged(async user=>{
  fbUser=user||null;
  if(!user){document.getElementById('authGate')?.classList.remove('hidden');document.getElementById('app')?.classList.add('authLocked');return}
  try{const snap=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(user.uid).get();if(!snap.exists){authBackToLogin();return}fbUserProfile=snap.data();document.getElementById('authGate')?.classList.add('hidden');document.getElementById('app')?.classList.remove('authLocked');removeLegacyAuthNodes();await startFirebasePresence();initPeer();await loadFirebaseChats();renderChats()}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not load your profile')}
});}
setTimeout(removeLegacyAuthNodes,250);

  const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');
  const p=fbUserProfile||{};
  pi.innerHTML=`<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Account</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Your KP WhatsApp account</div></div></div>
  <div class="pageCard profileHero"><div class="profileAvatarBig">${p.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(p.photoURL)}">`:escapeHtml((p.displayName||p.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase())}</div><h2>${escapeHtml(p.displayName||'KP User')}</h2><div class="profileUsernameBadge">@${escapeHtml(p.username||'')}</div><div style="color:#888;margin-top:8px">${escapeHtml(p.bio||'No bio yet')}</div></div>
  <div class="pageCard"><button class="primary" onclick="openProfileEdit()">Edit profile</button><button class="secondary" style="margin-top:10px" onclick="firebaseLogout()">Sign out</button><button class="secondary accountDanger" style="margin-top:10px" onclick="deleteMyAccount()">Delete account</button></div>`;
  pv.classList.add('show');
}

async function loadFirebaseChats(){
  if(!fbUser||!fbDb)return;
  try{
    const snap=await fbDb.collection('chats').where('memberIds','array-contains',fbUser.uid).get();
    const remote=[];
    for(const d of snap.docs){
      const x=d.data()||{};
      const other=(x.memberIds||[]).find(id=>id!==fbUser.uid);
      if(x.type==='group'){
        remote.push({id:d.id,firestoreId:d.id,name:x.name||'Group',initial:(x.name||'G')[0].toUpperCase(),preview:x.lastMessage||'No messages yet',time:kpTime(x.lastMessageAt),sortTime:kpMillis(x.lastMessageAt)||kpMillis(x.createdAt),online:false,unread:x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0,messageCount:Number(x.messageCount||0),color:'green',group:true,memberIds:x.memberIds||[],adminIds:x.adminIds||[],avatar:x.avatar||'',avatarType:x.avatarType||'initial',messages:[],startedAt:kpTime(x.createdAt)||'Today'});
      }else{
        let u={};
        if(other){const us=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(other).get();if(us.exists)u=us.data()||{}}
        let online=false;
        if(other&&fbRtdb){try{const ps=await fbRtdb.ref('presence/'+other).once('value');online=ps.val()?.state==='online'}catch(e){}}
        const display=u.displayName||u.firstName||u.username||'Unknown user';
        remote.push({id:d.id,firestoreId:d.id,name:display,username:u.username||'',initial:(display[0]||'U').toUpperCase(),preview:x.lastMessage||'No messages yet',time:kpTime(x.lastMessageAt),sortTime:kpMillis(x.lastMessageAt)||kpMillis(x.createdAt),online,unread:x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0,color:'green',group:false,messages:[],peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:other,photoURL:u.photoURL||'',startedAt:kpTime(x.createdAt)||'Today'});
      }
    }
    remote.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));
    chats.splice(0,chats.length,...remote);
    renderChats();
    if(current&&!chats.some(c=>c.id===current.id))current=null;
  }catch(e){console.error('loadFirebaseChats:',e);toast('Could not load chats: '+(e.code||e.message||'permission error'))}
}

async function ensurePrivateChat(otherUid,otherName='',otherPeerId=''){
  if(!fbUser||!otherUid||otherUid===fbUser.uid)throw new Error('Invalid chat user');
  const ids=[fbUser.uid,otherUid].sort();
  const chatId=ids.join('_');
  const ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(chatId);
  const snap=await ref.get();
  if(!snap.exists){
    const unread={};unread[fbUser.uid]=0;unread[otherUid]=0;
    const peerIds={};peerIds[fbUser.uid]=window.peerId||'';peerIds[otherUid]=otherPeerId||'';
    await ref.set({type:'private',memberIds:ids,createdAt:KPServerTS(),lastMessage:'',lastMessageAt:KPServerTS(),lastMessageSenderId:'',unreadCount:unread,peerIds});
  }
  return chatId;
}

async function startChatWithUser(uid){
  if(!fbUser||uid===fbUser.uid)return;
  try{
    let u=realUsers.find(x=>x.uid===uid);
    if(!u){const us=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid).get();if(!us.exists)throw new Error('User not found');u={...us.data(),uid}}
    const chatId=await ensurePrivateChat(uid,u.displayName||u.username||'User',u.peerId||'');
    let c=chats.find(x=>x.firestoreId===chatId);
    const display=u.displayName||u.username||'User';
    if(!c){c={id:chatId,firestoreId:chatId,name:display,username:u.username||'',initial:(display[0]||'U').toUpperCase(),preview:'No messages yet',time:'',sortTime:Date.now(),online:false,unread:0,color:'green',group:false,messages:[],peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:uid,photoURL:u.photoURL||'',startedAt:'Today'};chats.unshift(c)}
    else Object.assign(c,{name:display,username:u.username||'',peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:uid,photoURL:u.photoURL||'',initial:(display[0]||'U').toUpperCase()});
    renderChats();closePage();current=c;document.getElementById('app').classList.add('chat-open');await loadFirebaseMessages(c);startFirebaseMessageListener(c);await markChatRead(c);renderMessages();
  }catch(e){console.error('startChatWithUser:',e);toast('Could not open this chat: '+(e.code||e.message||'Firestore error'))}
}

function renderChats(){
  const list=document.getElementById('chatList');if(!list)return;
  const q=(document.getElementById('search')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
  const arr=chats.filter(c=>(activeTab==='all'||(activeTab==='unread'&&c.unread)||(activeTab==='groups'&&c.group))&&((c.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(c.username||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)));
  arr.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));
  list.innerHTML=arr.map(c=>{const av=c.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(c.photoURL)}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:50%">`:escapeHtml(c.initial||'U');return `<div class="chatRow ${current?.id===c.id?'selected':''}" onclick="openChat('${String(c.id).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')"><div class="avatar green">${av}</div><div class="chatMeta"><div class="chatName"><b>${escapeHtml(c.name||'User')}</b>${c.username&&!c.group?`<small style="display:block;color:#777">@${escapeHtml(c.username)}</small>`:''}</div><div class="chatPreview">${escapeHtml(c.preview||'')}</div></div><div class="chatSide"><small>${escapeHtml(c.time||'')}</small>${c.unread?`<span class="unread">${c.unread}</span>`:''}${c.group&&c.messageCount?`<span class="unread">${c.messageCount}</span>`:''}</div></div>`}).join('')||'<div class="empty">No chats yet.<br>Tap + to start a new chat.</div>';
}

async function openChat(id){
  const c=chats.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));if(!c)return;
  current=c;c.unread=0;renderChats();document.getElementById('app').classList.add('chat-open');
  try{await loadFirebaseMessages(c);startFirebaseMessageListener(c);await markChatRead(c);renderMessages()}catch(e){console.error('openChat:',e);toast('Could not open this chat: '+(e.code||e.message||'Firestore error'))}
}

async function deleteMyAccount(){
  if(!fbUser)return;
  if(!confirm('Delete your KP WhatsApp account?'))return;
  try{
    const uid=fbUser.uid,username=fbUserProfile?.username;
    if(username)await fbDb.collection('usernames').doc(username).delete().catch(()=>{});
    await fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid).delete();
    await fbUser.delete();
    location.reload();
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not delete account: '+(e.code||e.message||'error'))}
}

function startVoiceCall(){
  if(!current||current.group){toast('Voice calls are 1-on-1 only');return}
  const rid=current.peerId||current.recipientPeerId;if(!rid){toast('This user is not online for voice calling yet.');return}
  if(!peer||!peer.open){toast('Voice calling is still connecting…');return}
  showCallModal('outgoing',current.name,current.initial||'A');
  ensureLocalAudio().then(stream=>{localStream=stream;activeCall=peer.call(rid,stream,{metadata:{uid:fbUser.uid,name:fbUserProfile?.displayName||'KP User',username:fbUserProfile?.username||'',peerId}});if(!activeCall)throw new Error('Call failed');bindCall(activeCall,current.name,current.initial||'A');logCall('calling')}).catch(e=>{console.error(e);stopLocalStream();hideCallModal();toast('Could not start voice call')})
}

function handleIncomingCall(call){
  if(activeCall||incomingCall){try{call.close()}catch(e){}return}
  incomingCall=call;const meta=call.metadata||{};const callerName=meta.name||'Incoming caller';
  showCallModal('incoming',callerName,(callerName[0]||'U').toUpperCase());
  const cp=document.getElementById('callPeer');if(cp)cp.textContent=meta.username?'@'+meta.username:'';
  playRingtone();
}

if(typeof fbAuth!=='undefined'&&fbAuth){
  fbAuth.onAuthStateChanged(async user=>{
    fbUser=user||null;
    if(!user){document.getElementById('authGate')?.classList.remove('hidden');document.getElementById('app')?.classList.add('authLocked');return}
    try{
      const snap=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(user.uid).get();
      if(!snap.exists){toast('Profile not found. Please create your username profile.');return}
      fbUserProfile=snap.data()||{};
      document.getElementById('authGate')?.classList.add('hidden');document.getElementById('app')?.classList.remove('authLocked');
      removeLegacyAuthNodes();initPeer();await startFirebasePresence();await loadFirebaseChats();renderChats();
    }catch(e){console.error('auth/profile:',e);toast('Firebase error: '+(e.code||e.message||'permission error'))}
  });
}
setTimeout(removeLegacyAuthNodes,250);

let fbUnsubOtherProfile=null;
async function syncOpenChatProfile(c){
  if(fbUnsubOtherProfile){try{fbUnsubOtherProfile()}catch(e){}fbUnsubOtherProfile=null}
  if(!fbUser||!c||c.group||!c.otherUid||!fbDb)return;
  try{
    const ref=fbDb.collection('users').doc(c.otherUid);
    const snap=await ref.get();
    if(snap.exists){
      const u=snap.data()||{};
      Object.assign(c,{name:u.displayName||u.firstName||u.username||c.name,username:u.username||c.username||'',photoURL:u.photoURL||'',initial:((u.displayName||u.firstName||u.username||'U')[0]||'U').toUpperCase(),peerId:u.peerId||c.peerId||''});
      renderChats();updateHeadAvatar();renderMessages();
    }
    fbUnsubOtherProfile=ref.onSnapshot(ds=>{
      if(!ds.exists)return;
      const u=ds.data()||{};
      Object.assign(c,{name:u.displayName||u.firstName||u.username||c.name,username:u.username||'',photoURL:u.photoURL||'',initial:((u.displayName||u.firstName||u.username||'U')[0]||'U').toUpperCase(),peerId:u.peerId||c.peerId||''});
      renderChats();
      if(current===c){updateHeadAvatar();renderMessages();}
    });
  }catch(e){console.warn('profile sync',e)}
}

function updateHeadAvatar(){
  const ha=document.getElementById('headAvatar');if(!ha||!current)return;
  const src=current.photoURL||current.avatar||'';
  ha.innerHTML=src?`<img src="${escapeHtml(src)}" alt="" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;border-radius:50%">`:escapeHtml(current.initial||'U');
  if(!current.group&&current.online)ha.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<span class="dot"></span>');
}

function renderChats(){
  const list=document.getElementById('chatList');if(!list)return;
  const q=(document.getElementById('search')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
  const arr=chats.filter(c=>(activeTab==='all'||(activeTab==='unread'&&c.unread)||(activeTab==='groups'&&c.group))&&((c.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(c.username||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)));
  arr.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));
  const kpaiRow = (!q || 'kpai'.includes(q) || 'ai assistant'.includes(q)) && activeTab==='all' ? `
    <div class="chatRow kpaiChatRow" onclick="openKPAI()" role="button" tabindex="0" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' '){event.preventDefault();openKPAI()}">
      <div class="avatar kpaiListAvatar"><div class="kpaiMiniOrb"></div></div>
      <div class="chatMeta"><div class="chatName"><b>KPAI</b><small>AI assistant</small></div><div class="chatPreview">Ask KPAI anything…</div></div>
      <div class="chatSide"><small>AI</small></div>
    </div>` : '';
  list.innerHTML=kpaiRow + arr.map(c=>{
    const src=c.group?c.avatar:c.photoURL;
    const av=src?`<img src="${escapeHtml(src)}" alt="" onerror="this.remove()">`:escapeHtml(c.initial||'U');
    const badge=c.unread?`<span class="unread">${c.unread}</span>`:'';
    const groupCount=c.group&&c.messageCount?`<span class="unread">${c.messageCount}</span>`:'';
    return `<div class="chatRow ${current?.id===c.id?'selected':''}" data-chat-id="${escapeHtml(String(c.id))}" onclick="openChat('${String(c.id).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')"><div class="avatar green">${av}</div><div class="chatMeta"><div class="chatName"><b>${escapeHtml(c.name||'User')}</b>${c.username&&!c.group?`<small>@${escapeHtml(c.username)}</small>`:''}</div><div class="chatPreview">${escapeHtml(c.preview||'No messages yet')}</div></div><div class="chatSide"><small>${escapeHtml(c.time||'')}</small>${badge}${groupCount}</div></div>`;
  }).join('')||'<div class="empty">No chats yet.<br>Tap + to start a new chat.</div>';
}

async function openChat(id){
  const c=chats.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));if(!c)return;
  current=c;c.unread=0;renderChats();document.getElementById('app').classList.add('chat-open');
  try{
    if(c.group){await loadFirebaseMessages(c);startFirebaseMessageListener(c);await markChatRead(c);renderMessages();return;}
    await syncOpenChatProfile(c);
    if(!c.firestoreId&&c.otherUid)c.firestoreId=await ensurePrivateChat(c.otherUid,c.name,c.peerId||'');
    await loadFirebaseMessages(c);startFirebaseMessageListener(c);await markChatRead(c);renderMessages();
  }catch(e){console.error('openChat',e);toast('Could not open this chat: '+(e.code||e.message||'Firestore error'))}
}

async function startChatWithUser(uid){
  if(!fbUser||uid===fbUser.uid)return;
  try{
    let u=realUsers.find(x=>x.uid===uid);
    if(!u){const us=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid).get();if(!us.exists)throw new Error('User not found');u={...us.data(),uid}}
    const chatId=await ensurePrivateChat(uid,u.displayName||u.username||'User',u.peerId||'');
    let c=chats.find(x=>x.firestoreId===chatId||x.id===chatId);
    const display=u.displayName||u.firstName||u.username||'User';
    if(!c){c={id:chatId,firestoreId:chatId,name:display,username:u.username||'',initial:(display[0]||'U').toUpperCase(),preview:'No messages yet',time:'',sortTime:Date.now(),online:false,unread:0,color:'green',group:false,messages:[],peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:uid,photoURL:u.photoURL||'',startedAt:'Today'};chats.unshift(c)}
    else Object.assign(c,{name:display,username:u.username||'',initial:(display[0]||'U').toUpperCase(),peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||'',otherUid:uid,photoURL:u.photoURL||''});
    closePage();renderChats();await openChat(c.id);
  }catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not open this chat: '+(e.code||e.message||'Firestore error'))}
}

async function ensurePrivateChat(otherUid,otherName='',otherPeerId=''){
  if(!fbUser||!otherUid||otherUid===fbUser.uid)throw new Error('Invalid chat user');
  const ids=[fbUser.uid,otherUid].sort(),chatId=ids.join('_'),ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(chatId),snap=await ref.get();
  if(!snap.exists){
    const unread={};ids.forEach(id=>unread[id]=0);
    const peerIds={};peerIds[fbUser.uid]=window.peerId||'';peerIds[otherUid]=otherPeerId||'';
    await ref.set({type:'private',memberIds:ids,createdAt:KPServerTS(),lastMessage:'',lastMessageAt:KPServerTS(),lastMessageSenderId:'',unreadCount:unread,peerIds});
  }
  return chatId;
}

  const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');

    <div class="newChatActions"><button class="newChatAction primaryAction" onclick="focusUserSearch()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"/><path d="m16 16 5 5"/></svg>New chat</button><button class="newChatAction" onclick="openGroupCreator()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="9" cy="8" r="3"/><circle cx="17" cy="10" r="2.5"/><path d="M3 20a6 6 0 0 1 12 0M14 17a5 5 0 0 1 7 3"/></svg>Create group</button></div>

  </div>`;
  pv.classList.add('show');await loadRealUsers();
}
function focusUserSearch(){document.getElementById('userSearch')?.focus()}

  <button class="primary" onclick="openGroupMembersPage()">Add members</button></div></div>`;
