}
function setGroupType2(t){groupDraft.type=t;document.getElementById('privateType2')?.classList.toggle('active',t==='private');document.getElementById('publicType2')?.classList.toggle('active',t==='public')}
function previewGroupPhoto2(file){if(!file)return;const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>{groupAvatarUrl=r.result;groupDraft.avatar=r.result;const w=document.getElementById('groupAvatarPreviewWrap');if(w)w.innerHTML=`<img src="${escapeHtml(r.result)}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover">`};r.readAsDataURL(file)}
async function openGroupMembersPage(){
  const name=document.getElementById('groupName2')?.value.trim();if(!name){toast('Enter a group name');return}
  groupDraft={name,about:document.getElementById('groupAbout2')?.value.trim()||'',type:groupDraft.type||'private',avatar:groupAvatarUrl||''};selectedGroupMembers=[];
  await loadRealUsers();
  const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');
  pi.innerHTML=`<div class="newChatPage"><div class="newChatTop"><button class="pageBack" onclick="openGroupCreator()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div class="newChatTitle"><b>Add members</b><span>Select people to add to ${escapeHtml(groupDraft.name)}</span></div></div><div class="newChatSearch"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="10.8" cy="10.8" r="6.8"/><path d="m16 16 5 5"/></svg><input class="field" id="groupMemberSearch" placeholder="Search username or name" oninput="filterGroupMembers()"></div><div class="newChatUsers" id="groupMemberList"></div><div class="groupCreateFooter"><button class="primary" onclick="finishCreateGroup()">Create group</button></div></div>`;
  pv.classList.add('show');renderGroupMembers(realUsers);
}
function renderGroupMembers(list){const box=document.getElementById('groupMemberList');if(!box)return;box.innerHTML=list.map(u=>{const sel=selectedGroupMembers.includes(u.uid),src=u.photoURL||'';return `<div class="groupMemberPick ${sel?'selected':''}" onclick="toggleGroupMemberPage('${u.uid}')"><input type="checkbox" ${sel?'checked':''}><span class="groupTick">✓</span><div class="groupPickAvatar">${src?`<img src="${escapeHtml(src)}">`:escapeHtml((u.displayName||u.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase())}</div><div style="min-width:0"><b style="display:block;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis">${escapeHtml(u.displayName||u.username||'User')}</b><small style="color:#777">@${escapeHtml(u.username||'')}</small></div><span style="color:#777">›</span></div>`}).join('')||'<div style="padding:18px;color:#777">No other registered users.</div>'}
function filterGroupMembers(){const q=kpUsername(document.getElementById('groupMemberSearch')?.value);renderGroupMembers(realUsers.filter(u=>(u.username||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)||(u.displayName||'').toLowerCase().includes(q)))}
function toggleGroupMemberPage(uid){selectedGroupMembers.includes(uid)?selectedGroupMembers=selectedGroupMembers.filter(x=>x!==uid):selectedGroupMembers.push(uid);filterGroupMembers()}

async function finishCreateGroup(){
  if(!fbUser||!fbDb)return toast('Please sign in first');
  const name=(groupDraft.name||'New Group').trim();if(!name)return toast('Enter a group name');
  let avatar=groupDraft.avatar||groupAvatarUrl||'';const memberIds=[...new Set([fbUser.uid,...selectedGroupMembers.filter(id=>id&&id!==fbUser.uid)])];
  try{
    toast('Creating group…');
    if(avatar.startsWith('data:')){const blob=await (await fetch(avatar)).blob();const f=new File([blob],'group-avatar',{type:blob.type||'image/jpeg'});avatar=(await cloudinaryUpload(f)).secure_url}
    const ref=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(),unread={};memberIds.forEach(id=>unread[id]=0);const ts=KPServerTS(),welcome=`Welcome to ${name}! This group was created successfully.`;
    await ref.set({type:'group',name,about:groupDraft.about||'',groupType:groupDraft.type||'private',avatar,avatarType:avatar?'media':'initial',memberIds,adminIds:[fbUser.uid],createdBy:fbUser.uid,createdAt:ts,lastMessage:welcome,lastMessageAt:ts,lastMessageSenderId:fbUser.uid,messageCount:1,unreadCount:unread});
    const mr=ref.collection('messages').doc();await mr.set({senderId:fbUser.uid,type:'system',text:welcome,createdAt:ts,edited:false,deleted:false,readBy:[fbUser.uid],deliveredTo:memberIds});
    const c={id:ref.id,firestoreId:ref.id,name,initial:name[0].toUpperCase(),preview:welcome,time:new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}),sortTime:Date.now(),online:false,unread:0,messageCount:1,group:true,groupType:groupDraft.type||'private',avatar,avatarType:avatar?'media':'initial',about:groupDraft.about||'',adminIds:[fbUser.uid],memberIds,messages:[['system',welcome,new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}),'','',{id:mr.id,raw:{senderId:fbUser.uid,type:'system'}}]],startedAt:'Today'};
    chats.unshift(c);current=c;selectedGroupMembers=[];groupAvatarUrl='';groupDraft={name:'',about:'',type:'private',avatar:''};closePage();renderChats();document.getElementById('app').classList.add('chat-open');startFirebaseMessageListener(c);renderMessages();toast('Group created successfully');
  }catch(e){console.error('finishCreateGroup',e);toast('Group creation failed: '+(e.code||e.message||'Firestore error'))}
}

/* Group info: show actual profile photos and member controls. */
async function openGroupDetails(){
  if(!current?.group)return;
  const ids=current.memberIds||[];const members=[];
  for(const id of ids){if(id===fbUser?.uid){members.push({id:'me',uid:id,name:fbUserProfile?.displayName||'You',username:fbUserProfile?.username||'',initial:((fbUserProfile?.displayName||fbUserProfile?.username||'Y')[0]).toUpperCase(),photoURL:fbUserProfile?.photoURL||'',online:true});continue}let u={};try{const s=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(id).get();if(s.exists)u=s.data()||{}}catch(e){}members.push({id,uid:id,name:u.displayName||u.firstName||u.username||'User',username:u.username||'',initial:((u.displayName||u.username||'U')[0]).toUpperCase(),photoURL:u.photoURL||'',online:false})}
  const admins=current.adminIds||[];const avatar=current.avatar?`<div class="groupDetailAvatar"><img src="${escapeHtml(current.avatar)}" alt=""></div>`:`<div class="groupDetailAvatar" style="display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:34px;font-weight:800;color:#b8ff22">${escapeHtml(current.initial)}</div>`;
  const rows=members.map(m=>`<div class="memberRow contactMember"><div class="miniAvatar">${m.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(m.photoURL)}" alt="">`:escapeHtml(m.initial)}</div><div style="flex:1"><b>${escapeHtml(m.name)}</b><small>@${escapeHtml(m.username||'')}</small></div>${admins.includes(m.uid)?'<span style="color:#b8ff22;font-size:10px">ADMIN</span>':''}${admins.includes(fbUser?.uid)&&m.uid!==fbUser?.uid?`<button class="iconBtn" onclick="removeGroupMember('${m.uid}')" title="Remove"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>`:''}</div>`).join('');
  const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');
  pi.innerHTML=`<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Group info</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">${members.length} members</div></div></div><div class="pageCard groupDetailHero">${avatar}<h2>${escapeHtml(current.name)}</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">${escapeHtml(current.about||'No description')}</div><div class="detailPills"><span>${escapeHtml(current.groupType||'private')}</span><span>${members.length} members</span></div></div><div class="pageCard"><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center"><b>Members</b><button class="secondary" onclick="addMembersToCurrentGroup()">＋ Add</button></div>${rows}</div><button class="secondary danger" onclick="exitCurrentGroup()">Exit group</button></div>`;
  pv.classList.add('show');
}
async function addMembersToCurrentGroup(){if(!current?.group)return;await loadRealUsers();const old=[...(current.memberIds||[])];selectedGroupMembers=[];const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');pi.innerHTML=`<div class="newChatPage"><div class="newChatTop"><button class="pageBack" onclick="openGroupDetails()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div class="newChatTitle"><b>Add group members</b><span>Select additional users</span></div></div><div class="newChatUsers" id="groupMemberList"></div><div class="groupCreateFooter"><button class="primary" onclick="saveAddedGroupMembers('${current.id}')">Add selected members</button></div></div>`;renderGroupMembers(realUsers.filter(u=>!old.includes(u.uid)))}
async function saveAddedGroupMembers(groupId){if(!current?.group||!selectedGroupMembers.length)return toast('Select at least one user');try{const old=current.memberIds||[],members=[...new Set([...old,...selectedGroupMembers])],unread={...(current.unreadCount||{})};selectedGroupMembers.forEach(id=>unread[id]=0);await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(groupId).set({memberIds:members,unreadCount:unread},{merge:true});current.memberIds=members;selectedGroupMembers=[];await openGroupDetails();toast('Members added')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not add members')}}
async function removeGroupMember(uid){if(!current?.group||!current.adminIds?.includes(fbUser.uid))return;try{const members=(current.memberIds||[]).filter(id=>id!==uid);await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.id).set({memberIds:members},{merge:true});current.memberIds=members;await openGroupDetails();toast('Member removed')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not remove member')}}
async function exitCurrentGroup(){if(!current?.group)return;if(!confirm('Exit this group?'))return;try{const members=(current.memberIds||[]).filter(id=>id!==fbUser.uid);await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.id).set({memberIds:members},{merge:true});chats.splice(chats.findIndex(x=>x.id===current.id),1);closePage();closeChat();current=null;renderChats();toast('You left the group')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not leave group')}}

  <div class="pageCard groupWizard"><div style="text-align:center"><div id="groupAvatarPreviewWrap" class="groupDetailAvatar" style="margin:0 auto 10px;width:100px;height:100px;border-radius:50%;overflow:hidden;background:#171717;display:grid;place-items:center;color:#b8ff22;font-size:30px;font-weight:900">${groupDraft.avatar?`<img src="${escapeHtml(groupDraft.avatar)}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover">`:'G'}</div><div style="display:flex;justify-content:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap"><label class="secondary uploadBtn">Photo<input id="groupPhotoInput2" type="file" accept="image/*" hidden onchange="previewGroupPhoto2(this.files[0])"></label><button class="secondary" onclick="openGifPicker(true)">GIF</button></div></div>
  <input class="field" id="groupName2" placeholder="Group name" maxlength="60" value="${escapeHtml(groupDraft.name||'')}"><textarea class="callNote" id="groupAbout2" placeholder="Description / purpose" maxlength="160">${escapeHtml(groupDraft.about||'')}</textarea>
  <div class="groupType"><button class="${groupDraft.type==='private'?'active':''}" id="privateType2" onclick="setGroupType2('private')">Private</button><button class="${groupDraft.type==='public'?'active':''}" id="publicType2" onclick="setGroupType2('public')">Public</button></div>
  <button class="primary" onclick="openGroupMembersPage()">Add members</button></div></div>`;
  pv.classList.add('show');
};
function selectGif(url,forGroup=false){
  if(forGroup){groupAvatarUrl=url;groupDraft={...(groupDraft||{}),avatar:url};closeModal();openGroupCreator(true);return;}
  pendingGifUrl=url;openGifSendPreview(url);
}

  const chat=document.getElementById('chatPanel');
  if(chat&&!document.getElementById('chatLoadingOverlay')){
    chat.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div class="chatLoadingOverlay" id="chatLoadingOverlay"><div style="text-align:center"><div class="kpSpinner"></div><div class="kpLoadingText">Loading chat…</div></div></div>');

/* Sender profile cache for tiny message avatars. */
const kpSenderCache=window.kpSenderCache||{};window.kpSenderCache=kpSenderCache;
async function kpGetUser(uid){
  if(!uid)return null;if(kpSenderCache[uid])return kpSenderCache[uid];
  try{const s=await fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid).get();if(s.exists){kpSenderCache[uid]={uid,...(s.data()||{})};return kpSenderCache[uid]}}catch(e){console.warn('sender profile',e)}
  return null;
}
function kpAvatarSmall(u,initial='U'){
  return u?.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(u.photoURL)}" alt="">`:escapeHtml((u?.displayName||u?.username||initial||'U')[0].toUpperCase());
}

/* Accurate message unread counts + compact sender DP in group messages. */
function kpRenderMessageHTML(m,idx){
  const kind=m[0],time=m[2]||'',isMe=['me','me-gif','me-media'].includes(kind),state=m[4]||(isMe?'read':null),docId=m[5]?.id||'',raw=m[5]?.raw||{},sender=kpSenderCache[raw.senderId];
  if(kind==='system')return `<div class="systemMessage"><span>${escapeHtml(m[1]||'')}</span></div>`;
  let payload={},content='',caption='',reply='';
  if(kind==='gif'||kind==='me-gif'){try{payload=typeof m[1]==='string'&&m[1].startsWith('{')?JSON.parse(m[1]):{url:m[1]}}catch(e){payload={url:m[1]}}caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||'';content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" loading="lazy" alt="GIF"></div>`}
  else if(kind==='media'||kind==='me-media'){try{payload=JSON.parse(m[1])}catch(e){payload={}}caption=payload.caption||'';reply=payload.reply||'';if((payload.type||'').startsWith('image/'))content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><img src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" loading="lazy" alt="${escapeHtml(payload.name||'image')}"></div>`;else if((payload.type||'').startsWith('audio/'))content=`<div class="msgMediaWrap"><audio controls src="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}"></audio></div>`;else content=`<div class="fileCard"><span>📎</span><a href="${escapeHtml(payload.url||'')}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${escapeHtml(payload.name||'File')}</a></div>`}
  else{content=`<div class="txt">${escapeHtml(m[1]||'')}</div>`;reply=m[3]||''}
  const quote=reply?`<div class="replyQuote">↩ ${escapeHtml(reply)}</div>`:'';const cap=caption?`<div class="msgMediaCaption">${escapeHtml(caption)}</div>`:'';const status=isMe?deliveryIcon(state):'';const del=isMe&&docId?`<button class="msgDeleteBtn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();selectMessage('${String(docId).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}',${idx})" title="Select message" aria-label="Select message"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 7h16M9 7V4h6v3M7 7l1 13h8l1-13M10 11v6M14 11v6"/></svg></button>`:'';
  const body=`<div class="msg ${isMe?'me':'in'} ${kind.includes('media')||kind.includes('gif')?'fileMsg':''}" data-message-id="${escapeHtml(docId)}" data-message-index="${idx}">${!isMe&&current?.group?`<div class="kp-sender-name">@${escapeHtml(sender?.username||raw.senderUsername||'user')}</div>`:''}${quote}${content}${cap}<div class="meta">${escapeHtml(time)} ${status}${del}</div></div>`;
  return isMe?`<div class="kp-msg-line me-line">${body}</div>`:`<div class="kp-msg-line"><div class="kp-msg-avatar">${kpAvatarSmall(sender,(sender?.displayName||raw.senderUsername||'U'))}</div>${body}</div>`;
}
async function kpEnsureSenderProfiles(list){
  if(!current?.group)return;
  const ids=[...new Set(list.map(m=>m?.[5]?.raw?.senderId).filter(Boolean))].filter(id=>!kpSenderCache[id]);
  if(!ids.length)return;await Promise.all(ids.map(kpGetUser));if(current)renderMessages();
}

const __kpOldRenderMessages=window.renderMessages;
function renderMessages(){
  const box=document.getElementById('messages');if(!box||!current)return;
  const list=current.messages||[];
  box.innerHTML='<div class="love-fx" id="loveFx"></div><div class="chatStarted">Chat started · '+escapeHtml(current.startedAt||'Today')+'</div><div class="day">TODAY</div>'+list.map(kpRenderMessageHTML).join('');
  updateHeadAvatar();
  const hn=document.getElementById('headName'),hs=document.getElementById('headStatus');if(hn)hn.textContent=current.name||'';if(hs)hs.textContent=current.group?`${(current.memberIds||[]).length} members`:(current.online?'online':'last seen recently');
  bindLongPressMessages();box.scrollTop=box.scrollHeight;
  kpEnsureSenderProfiles(list);
}
window.renderMessages=renderMessages;

/* Long press + selection toolbar is already present; make selection visually reliable. */
function selectMessage(id,idx){if(!id)return;selectedMessageIds.add(id);const bar=document.getElementById('messageSelectBar');bar?.classList.add('show');const count=document.getElementById('messageSelectCount');if(count)count.textContent=`${selectedMessageIds.size} selected`;renderMessages();}

/* Real unread count in every chat; group badge = unread, never total messages. */
function renderChats(){
  const list=document.getElementById('chatList');if(!list)return;const q=(document.getElementById('search')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
  const arr=chats.filter(c=>(activeTab==='all'||(activeTab==='unread'&&Number(c.unread)>0)||(activeTab==='groups'&&c.group))&&(((c.name||'')+' '+(c.username||'')).toLowerCase().includes(q)));
  arr.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));
  list.innerHTML=arr.map(c=>{const url=c.group?c.avatar:c.photoURL;const av=url?`<div class="avatar green"><img src="${escapeHtml(url)}" alt=""></div>`:`<div class="avatar green">${escapeHtml(c.initial||'U')}</div>`;const badge=Number(c.unread||0);return `<div class="row ${current?.id===c.id?'selected':''}" onclick="openChat('${String(c.id).replace(/'/g,"\\'")}')">${av}<div class="rowmain"><div class="rline"><span class="name">${escapeHtml(c.name||'User')}</span><span class="time">${escapeHtml(c.time||'')}</span></div><div class="rline"><span class="preview">${escapeHtml(c.preview||'No messages yet')}</span>${badge?`<span class="unread">${badge>99?'99+':badge}</span>`:''}</div></div></div>`}).join('')||'<div class="empty"><div><strong>No chats yet</strong>Tap + to find people registered in KP WhatsApp.</div></div>';
}

/* Profile photo is the single source of truth; refresh open chat/header immediately. */
async function uploadProfilePhoto(file){
  if(!file||!fbUser)return;try{toast('Uploading photo…');const up=await cloudinaryUpload(file);const url=up.secure_url;await fbDb.collection('users').doc(fbUser.uid).set({photoURL:url,avatarType:'photo',updatedAt:KPServerTS()},{merge:true});fbUserProfile={...(fbUserProfile||{}),photoURL:url,avatarType:'photo'};kpSenderCache[fbUser.uid]=fbUserProfile;chats.forEach(c=>{if(c.otherUid===fbUser.uid)c.photoURL=url});if(current?.otherUid===fbUser.uid)current.photoURL=url;renderChats();renderMessages();openProfileEdit();toast('Profile photo updated')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Photo upload failed')}}
async function removeProfilePhoto(){if(!fbUser)return;try{await fbDb.collection('users').doc(fbUser.uid).set({photoURL:'',avatarType:'initial',updatedAt:KPServerTS()},{merge:true});fbUserProfile={...(fbUserProfile||{}),photoURL:'',avatarType:'initial'};kpSenderCache[fbUser.uid]=fbUserProfile;renderChats();renderMessages();openProfileEdit();toast('Profile photo removed')}catch(e){toast('Could not remove photo')}}

/* Contact profile realtime DP listener for every private chat. */
function attachProfileListener(c){if(!c||c.group||!c.otherUid||!fbDb)return;if(window.kpProfileUnsubs?.[c.otherUid])return;window.kpProfileUnsubs=window.kpProfileUnsubs||{};window.kpProfileUnsubs[c.otherUid]=fbDb.collection('users').doc(c.otherUid).onSnapshot(s=>{if(!s.exists)return;const u=s.data()||{};Object.assign(c,{name:u.displayName||u.username||c.name,username:u.username||c.username,initial:(u.displayName||u.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase(),photoURL:u.photoURL||'',peerId:u.peerId||'',recipientPeerId:u.peerId||''});kpSenderCache[c.otherUid]={uid:c.otherUid,...u};renderChats();if(current===c)renderMessages()},e=>console.warn('profile listener',e))}

/* Loading state while a chat is fetched. */
const __kpOpenChatBase=openChat;
async function openChat(id){
  const c=chats.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id));if(!c)return;current=c;document.getElementById('app')?.classList.add('chat-open');const ov=document.getElementById('chatLoadingOverlay');ov?.classList.add('show');
  try{if(!c.group&&c.otherUid)attachProfileListener(c);await loadFirebaseMessages(c);startFirebaseMessageListener(c);await markChatRead(c);renderMessages();renderChats()}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not open this chat: '+(e.code||e.message||'Firestore error'))}finally{setTimeout(()=>ov?.classList.remove('show'),180)}
}
window.openChat=openChat;

/* Group admin operations: add/remove/edit only for admins. */
async function kpEditCurrentGroup(){
  if(!current?.group)return;if(!(current.adminIds||[]).includes(fbUser?.uid))return toast('Only group admins can edit group details.');
  const name=prompt('Group name',current.name||'');if(name===null)return;const about=prompt('Group bio / description',current.about||'');if(about===null)return;
  try{await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId).set({name:name.trim()||current.name,about:about.trim(),updatedAt:KPServerTS()},{merge:true});current.name=name.trim()||current.name;current.about=about.trim();renderChats();renderMessages();openGroupDetails();toast('Group details updated')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not update group')}
}
async function kpChangeGroupPhoto(file){
  if(!file||!current?.group)return;if(!(current.adminIds||[]).includes(fbUser?.uid))return toast('Only group admins can change the group photo.');
  try{toast('Uploading group photo…');const up=await cloudinaryUpload(file);await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId).set({avatar:up.secure_url,avatarType:'photo',updatedAt:KPServerTS()},{merge:true});current.avatar=up.secure_url;renderChats();renderMessages();openGroupDetails();toast('Group photo updated')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Group photo upload failed')}
}
async function kpAddGroupMembers(ids){if(!current?.group)return;if(!(current.adminIds||[]).includes(fbUser?.uid))return toast('Only admins can add members.');const add=[...new Set((ids||[]).filter(id=>id&&id!==fbUser.uid))];if(!add.length)return toast('Select at least one user.');try{const members=[...new Set([...(current.memberIds||[]),...add])],unread={...(current.unreadCount||{})};add.forEach(id=>unread[id]=0);await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId).set({memberIds:members,unreadCount:unread},{merge:true});current.memberIds=members;toast('Members added');openGroupDetails()}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not add members')}}
async function removeGroupMember(uid){if(!current?.group)return;if(!(current.adminIds||[]).includes(fbUser?.uid))return toast('Only admins can remove members.');if(uid===fbUser.uid)return toast('Use Exit group to leave yourself.');if(!confirm('Remove this member from the group?'))return;try{const members=(current.memberIds||[]).filter(id=>id!==uid);await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId).set({memberIds:members},{merge:true});current.memberIds=members;toast('Member removed');openGroupDetails()}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not remove member')}}
async function addMembersToCurrentGroup(){if(!current?.group)return;if(!(current.adminIds||[]).includes(fbUser?.uid))return toast('Only group admins can add members.');await loadRealUsers();const candidates=realUsers.filter(u=>!(current.memberIds||[]).includes(u.uid));const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');pi.innerHTML=`<div class="newChatPage"><div class="newChatTop"><button class="pageBack" onclick="openGroupDetails()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div class="newChatTitle"><b>Add members</b><span>Only group admins can add people</span></div></div><div class="newChatUsers" id="groupMemberList"></div><button class="primary" style="margin:12px" onclick="kpAddSelectedGroupMembers()">Add selected members</button></div>`;renderGroupMembers(candidates);pv.classList.add('show')}
async function kpAddSelectedGroupMembers(){await kpAddGroupMembers(selectedGroupMembers);selectedGroupMembers=[]}

/* Replace group info with accurate DPs, admin controls and edit tools. */
async function openGroupDetails(){
  if(!current?.group)return;const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');
  const members=await Promise.all((current.memberIds||[]).map(async uid=>{if(uid===fbUser.uid)return {uid,name:fbUserProfile?.displayName||'You',username:fbUserProfile?.username||'',photoURL:fbUserProfile?.photoURL||'',isSelf:true};const u=await kpGetUser(uid);return {uid,name:u?.displayName||u?.username||'User',username:u?.username||'',photoURL:u?.photoURL||'',isSelf:false}}));
  const isAdmin=(current.adminIds||[]).includes(fbUser.uid);const avatar=current.avatar?`<div class="groupDetailAvatar"><img src="${escapeHtml(current.avatar)}" alt="Group photo"></div>`:`<div class="groupDetailAvatar" style="color:#b8ff22;font-size:34px;font-weight:900">${escapeHtml(current.initial||'G')}</div>`;
  const rows=members.map(m=>`<div class="memberRow"><div class="miniAvatar">${kpAvatarSmall(m,m.name)}</div><div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><b>${escapeHtml(m.name)}</b><small style="display:block;color:#777">@${escapeHtml(m.username)}</small></div>${(current.adminIds||[]).includes(m.uid)?'<span style="color:#b8ff22;font-size:10px;font-weight:800">ADMIN</span>':''}${isAdmin&&!m.isSelf?`<button class="iconBtn" onclick="removeGroupMember('${m.uid}')" title="Remove member"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>`:''}</div>`).join('');
  pi.innerHTML=`<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Group info</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">${members.length} members · ${isAdmin?'Admin':'Member'}</div></div></div><div class="pageCard groupDetailHero">${avatar}<h2>${escapeHtml(current.name)}</h2><div style="color:#888;font-size:12px">${escapeHtml(current.about||'No description')}</div><div class="detailPills"><span>${escapeHtml(current.groupType||'private')}</span><span>${members.length} members</span></div>${isAdmin?`<div class="groupEditRow"><button class="secondary" onclick="kpEditCurrentGroup()">Edit name & bio</button><label class="secondary" style="display:grid;place-items:center;cursor:pointer">Change DP<input type="file" accept="image/*" hidden onchange="kpChangeGroupPhoto(this.files[0])"></label><button class="secondary" onclick="openCurrentGroupGifPicker()">Change GIF</button></div>`:''}</div><div class="pageCard"><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:8px"><b>Members</b>${isAdmin?'<button class="secondary" style="width:auto;padding:0 14px" onclick="addMembersToCurrentGroup()">＋ Add</button>':''}</div>${rows}</div><button class="secondary danger" onclick="exitCurrentGroup()">Exit group</button>`;
  pv.classList.add('show');
}

/* GIF picker loading animation and smaller first viewport. */
async function searchGifs(q,forGroup=false){
  const grid=document.getElementById('gifGrid');if(!grid)return;q=(q||'malayali reaction').trim()||'malayali reaction';grid.innerHTML='<div class="gifLoading"><div class="gifSpinner"></div><span>Loading GIFs…</span></div>';
  try{const endpoint=`https://api.giphy.com/v1/gifs/search?api_key=${encodeURIComponent(GIPHY_KEY)}&q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}&limit=18&rating=g`;const r=await fetch(endpoint);if(!r.ok)throw new Error('Giphy '+r.status);const j=await r.json();const items=j.data||[];grid.innerHTML=items.map(g=>{const img=g.images?.fixed_width_small?.webp||g.images?.fixed_width?.webp||g.images?.fixed_width?.url;const full=g.images?.original?.url||img;return `<img src="${escapeHtml(img||'')}" loading="lazy" onclick="selectGif('${String(full||'').replace(/'/g,"\\'")}',${forGroup})" alt="GIF">`}).join('')||'<div style="color:#777;padding:15px">No GIFs found.</div>'}catch(e){console.error(e);grid.innerHTML='<div class="gifLoading"><span>Could not load GIFs.</span><button class="secondary" onclick="searchGifs(\''+String(q).replace(/'/g,"\\'")+'\','+forGroup+')">Retry</button></div>'}
}

/* Calls: history with DP + one-tap callback; call screen uses caller photo. */
async function logCall(status){if(!fbUser||!current?.otherUid)return;try{const u=await kpGetUser(current.otherUid);await fbDb.collection('calls').add({callerId:fbUser.uid,receiverId:current.otherUid,participants:[fbUser.uid,current.otherUid],type:'voice',status,createdAt:KPServerTS(),otherName:current.name,otherUsername:current.username||u?.username||'',otherPhotoURL:u?.photoURL||current.photoURL||''})}catch(e){console.warn(e)}}
function kpCallAvatarHTML(photo,name){return photo?`<img src="${escapeHtml(photo)}" alt="">`:escapeHtml((name||'U')[0].toUpperCase())}
function showCallModal(type,name,initial,photo){const o=document.getElementById('callOverlay');if(!o)return;const av=document.getElementById('callAvatar');if(av)av.innerHTML=photo?`<img src="${escapeHtml(photo)}" alt="">`:escapeHtml(initial||'A');document.getElementById('callTitle').textContent=name||'Voice call';document.getElementById('callPeer').textContent='';const sub=document.getElementById('callSub'),a=document.getElementById('callActions');document.getElementById('callTimer').textContent='';if(type==='incoming'){sub.textContent='Incoming voice call';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction accept" onclick="acceptIncomingCall()" aria-label="Answer"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 4.5 9 4l2 4-2 2a13 13 0 0 0 5 5l2-2 4 2-.5 3a2 2 0 0 1-2.2 1.5C9.5 18.2 5.8 14.5 4.5 6.7A2 2 0 0 1 6 4.5Z"/></svg></button><button class="callAction end" onclick="rejectIncomingCall()" aria-label="Reject"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}else if(type==='outgoing'){sub.textContent='Calling…';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction end" onclick="endVoiceCall()" aria-label="End call"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}else{sub.textContent='Connected';a.innerHTML='<button class="callAction" id="muteCallBtn" onclick="toggleMute()" aria-label="Mute"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 3a3 3 0 0 0-3 3v6a3 3 0 0 0 6 0V6a3 3 0 0 0-3-3Z"/><path d="M19 10v2a7 7 0 0 1-14 0v-2M12 19v3M8 22h8"/></svg></button><button class="callAction" onclick="appendCallNote()" aria-label="Notes"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 4h12v16H6z"/><path d="M9 8h6M9 12h6M9 16h4"/></svg></button><button class="callAction end" onclick="endVoiceCall()" aria-label="End call"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>'}o.classList.add('show')}
function handleIncomingCall(call){if(activeCall||incomingCall){try{call.close()}catch(e){}return}incomingCall=call;const meta=call.metadata||{};const callerName=meta.name||meta.username||'Incoming caller';showCallModal('incoming',callerName,(callerName[0]||'U').toUpperCase(),meta.photoURL||'');document.getElementById('callPeer').textContent=meta.username?'@'+meta.username:'';playRingtone()}
function startVoiceCall(){if(!current||current.group){toast('Voice calls are 1-on-1 only');return}const rid=current.peerId||current.recipientPeerId;if(!rid){toast('This user is not ready for voice calling yet.');return}if(!peer||!peer.open){toast('Voice calling is still connecting…');return}const photo=current.photoURL||kpSenderCache[current.otherUid]?.photoURL||'';showCallModal('outgoing',current.name,current.initial||'A',photo);ensureLocalAudio().then(stream=>{localStream=stream;activeCall=peer.call(rid,stream,{metadata:{uid:fbUser.uid,name:fbUserProfile?.displayName||'KP User',username:fbUserProfile?.username||'',photoURL:fbUserProfile?.photoURL||'',peerId}});if(!activeCall)throw new Error('Call failed');bindCall(activeCall,current.name,current.initial||'A');logCall('calling')}).catch(e=>{console.error(e);stopLocalStream();hideCallModal();toast('Could not start voice call')})}
function openCallsPage(){const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');pi.innerHTML=`<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Calls</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Voice calls · username lookup</div></div></div><div class="pageCard"><input id="callUsernameInput" class="field" placeholder="Username to call"><button class="primary" onclick="callByUsername()">Find & Call</button><div id="callLookupResult" class="callResult"></div></div><div class="pageCard"><b>Recent calls</b><div id="callHistory"><div style="color:#777;padding-top:10px">Loading…</div></div></div>`;pv.classList.add('show');loadCallHistory()}
async function loadCallHistory(){const box=document.getElementById('callHistory');if(!box||!fbUser)return;try{const snap=await fbDb.collection('calls').where('participants','array-contains',fbUser.uid).limit(30).get();const docs=snap.docs.sort((a,b)=>(kpMillis(b.data().createdAt)-kpMillis(a.data().createdAt)));box.innerHTML=docs.map(d=>{const c=d.data(),other=c.callerId===fbUser.uid?c.receiverId:c.callerId;return `<div class="memberRow"><div class="miniAvatar">${c.otherPhotoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(c.otherPhotoURL)}" alt="">`:'☎'}</div><div style="flex:1;min-width:0"><b>${escapeHtml(c.otherName||'Unknown user')}</b><small style="display:block;color:#777">@${escapeHtml(c.otherUsername||'unknown')} · ${escapeHtml(c.status||'call')}</small></div><button class="iconBtn" onclick="callbackUser('${escapeHtml(other)}')" title="Call back"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 4.5 9 4l2 4-2 2a13 13 0 0 0 5 5l2-2 4 2-.5 3a2 2 0 0 1-2.2 1.5C9.5 18.2 5.8 14.5 4.5 6.7A2 2 0 0 1 6 4.5Z"/></svg></button></div>`}).join('')||'<div style="color:#777;padding-top:10px">No calls yet.</div>'}catch(e){console.error(e);box.innerHTML='<div style="color:#777;padding-top:10px">No call history yet.</div>'}}
async function callbackUser(uid){try{const u=await kpGetUser(uid);if(!u)return toast('User not found');await startChatWithUser(uid);setTimeout(()=>startVoiceCall(),250)}catch(e){toast('Could not call user')}}

 const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');pv.classList.add('show');pi.innerHTML=`<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Status</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Recent updates</div></div></div><div class="pageCard"><div class="statusComposer"><input class="field" id="statusTextInput" maxlength="180" placeholder="Write a status…"><button class="primary" style="width:auto;padding:0 16px" onclick="publishKPStatus()">Post</button></div><label class="secondary" style="display:grid;place-items:center;margin-top:8px;cursor:pointer">Photo / video<input type="file" id="statusFile" accept="image/*,video/*" hidden onchange="publishKPStatusFile(this.files[0])"></label></div><div class="pageCard"><b>Recent status</b><div id="statusScroller" class="statusScroller"><div class="statusEmpty">Loading statuses…</div></div></div>`;

async function loadKPStatuses(){const box=document.getElementById('statusScroller');if(!box||!fbUser)return;try{const snap=await fbDb.collection('status').limit(50).get();const docs=snap.docs.sort((a,b)=>kpMillis(b.data().createdAt)-kpMillis(a.data().createdAt));box.innerHTML=docs.map(d=>{const x=d.data();const u=kpSenderCache[x.userId]||{};return `<div class="statusItem"><div class="statusAvatar">${x.photoURL||u.photoURL?`<img src="${escapeHtml(x.photoURL||u.photoURL)}" alt="">`:escapeHtml((x.displayName||u.displayName||x.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase())}</div><div class="statusName">${escapeHtml(x.displayName||u.displayName||x.username||'User')}</div><div class="statusTime">${escapeHtml(kpTime(x.createdAt))}</div></div>`}).join('')||'<div class="statusEmpty">No status updates yet.</div>'}catch(e){console.error(e);box.innerHTML='<div class="statusEmpty">Could not load statuses.</div>'}}

/* KPAI: local assistant UI with typing animation; no external AI key required. */
function openKPAI(){const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');pv.classList.add('show');pi.innerHTML=`<div class="kpaiPage"><div class="kpaiHead"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div class="kpaiOrb"></div><div><h2 style="margin:0">KPAI</h2><div style="color:#69eaff;font-size:11px">KP AI Assistant · Online</div></div></div><div class="kpaiBody" id="kpaiBody"><div class="kpaiMsg"><b>KPAI</b><br>Hi! I’m KPAI. Ask me about KP WhatsApp, groups, calls, profiles, GIFs, status or your KP projects.</div></div><div class="kpaiTyping" id="kpaiTyping">KPAI is typing…</div><div class="kpaiInput"><input id="kpaiInput" placeholder="Message KPAI" onkeydown="if(event.key===&quot;Enter&quot;)sendKPAI()"><button onclick="sendKPAI()">➤</button></div></div>`}
function kpaiReply(t){const q=t.toLowerCase();if(q.includes('group'))return 'To create a group, open + → Create Group, choose a photo or GIF, add real users, then create it. Group admins can add/remove members and edit group details.';if(q.includes('call'))return 'KP WhatsApp supports 1-to-1 voice calls. Open Calls, search a username, or use the call button inside a chat. Incoming calls show the caller profile photo.';if(q.includes('dp')||q.includes('profile')||q.includes('photo'))return 'Your profile photo is uploaded to Cloudinary and saved as photoURL in Firebase. The same DP can appear in chats, message avatars, groups, calls and profile screens.';if(q.includes('gif'))return 'Open the GIF picker to search reactions, moods, love, football and gaming GIFs. GIFs are kept compact in messages and the picker shows a loading animation.';if(q.includes('status'))return 'Status lets you post text or media and view recent updates in a horizontal scrolling row.';if(q.includes('firebase'))return 'Firebase Authentication handles the username login identity, Firestore stores users/chats/groups/messages, and Realtime Database handles presence/call state.';if(q.includes('game')||q.includes('gta')||q.includes('greek'))return 'KPAI can help you plan KP game features, UI, Firebase systems, maps, weapons, bots and performance ideas.';return 'I can help with KP WhatsApp features, Firebase, profiles, groups, voice calls, status, GIFs, or your KP game projects. Try asking “How do I create a group?”'}
function sendKPAI(){const input=document.getElementById('kpaiInput'),body=document.getElementById('kpaiBody'),typing=document.getElementById('kpaiTyping');const t=input?.value.trim();if(!t)return;body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<div class="kpaiMsg me">${escapeHtml(t)}</div>`);input.value='';body.scrollTop=body.scrollHeight;typing.style.display='block';setTimeout(()=>{typing.style.display='none';body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<div class="kpaiMsg"><b>KPAI</b><br>${escapeHtml(kpaiReply(t))}</div>`);body.scrollTop=body.scrollHeight},650)}

/* Center KPAI navigation item. */
(function setupKPNav(){const navEl=document.querySelector('.nav');if(!navEl||navEl.querySelector('.kpaiNav'))return;const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='kpaiNav';b.innerHTML='<span class="kpaiIcon">AI</span><span>KPAI</span><i class="kpaiDot"></i>';b.onclick=()=>{if(callPageLocked){toast('End the call first');return}document.querySelectorAll('.nav button').forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('active'));b.classList.add('active');openKPAI()};const buttons=[...navEl.children];navEl.insertBefore(b,buttons[Math.ceil(buttons.length/2)]||null)})();

/* Make group message unread counts update from the chat document, not message total. */
const __kpBaseListener=startFirebaseMessageListener;
startFirebaseMessageListener=function(c){
  if(fbUnsubMessages){try{fbUnsubMessages()}catch(e){}fbUnsubMessages=null}if(!fbUser||!c?.firestoreId)return;
  fbUnsubMessages=fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId).collection('messages').orderBy('createdAt','asc').limit(500).onSnapshot(async snap=>{
    c.messages=snap.docs.map(kpMessageArray);const chatSnap=await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId).get().catch(()=>null);if(chatSnap?.exists){const x=chatSnap.data();c.preview=x.lastMessage||c.preview;c.sortTime=kpMillis(x.lastMessageAt)||c.sortTime;c.time=kpTime(x.lastMessageAt)||c.time;c.unread=Number(x.unreadCount?.[fbUser.uid]||0);if(c.group)c.messageCount=Number(x.messageCount||snap.size)}
    const idx=chats.findIndex(x=>x.id===c.id);if(idx>=0)chats[idx]=c;renderChats();if(current===c){renderMessages();await kpEnsureSenderProfiles(c.messages)}
  },e=>{console.error('message listener',e);toast('Could not sync messages')});
};

<script>
/* FINAL FUNCTIONAL PATCH */
async function markChatRead(c){
  if(!fbUser||!c?.firestoreId)return;
  c.unread=0; renderChats();
  try{await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId).set({['unreadCount.'+fbUser.uid]:0},{merge:true});}catch(e){console.warn('markChatRead',e)}
}
function closeChat(){
  if(current){current.unread=0;renderChats();markChatRead(current).catch(()=>{});}
  document.getElementById('app')?.classList.remove('chat-open');
  document.getElementById('chatLoadingOverlay')?.classList.remove('show');
}
window.closeChat=closeChat;
async function openChat(id){
  const c=chats.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id)); if(!c)return;
  current=c;c.unread=0;renderChats();document.getElementById('app')?.classList.add('chat-open');
  const ov=document.getElementById('chatLoadingOverlay');ov?.classList.add('show');
  try{
    if(!c.group&&c.otherUid)attachProfileListener(c);
    await loadFirebaseMessages(c);
    await markChatRead(c);
    startFirebaseMessageListener(c);
    renderMessages();renderChats();
  }catch(e){console.error('openChat',e);toast('Could not open this chat: '+(e.code||e.message||'Firestore error'));}
  finally{setTimeout(()=>document.getElementById('chatLoadingOverlay')?.classList.remove('show'),220);}
}
window.openChat=openChat;
function renderChats(){
 const list=document.getElementById('chatList');if(!list)return;
 const q=(document.getElementById('search')?.value||'').toLowerCase().trim();
 const arr=chats.filter(c=>(activeTab==='all'||(activeTab==='unread'&&Number(c.unread)>0)||(activeTab==='groups'&&c.group))&&(((c.name||'')+' '+(c.username||'')).toLowerCase().includes(q)));
 arr.sort((a,b)=>(b.sortTime||0)-(a.sortTime||0));
 const kpaiRow = (!q || 'kpai'.includes(q) || 'ai assistant'.includes(q)) && activeTab==='all' ? `
    <div class="chatRow kpaiChatRow" onclick="openKPAI()" role="button" tabindex="0" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'||event.key===' '){event.preventDefault();openKPAI()}">
      <div class="avatar kpaiListAvatar"><div class="kpaiMiniOrb"></div></div>
      <div class="chatMeta"><div class="chatName"><b>KPAI</b><small>AI assistant</small></div><div class="chatPreview">Ask KPAI anything…</div></div>
      <div class="chatSide"><small>AI</small></div>
    </div>` : '';
 list.innerHTML=kpaiRow + arr.map(c=>{
   const url=c.group?c.avatar:c.photoURL;
   const av=url?'<div class="avatar green"><img src="'+escapeHtml(url)+'" alt=""></div>':'<div class="avatar green">'+escapeHtml(c.initial||'U')+'</div>';
   const badge=Number(c.unread||0)>0?'<span class="unread">'+Number(c.unread||0)+'</span>':'';
   const cid=String(c.id).replace(/'/g,"\\'");
   return '<div class="chatRow '+(current?.id===c.id?'selected':'')+'" onclick="openChat(\''+cid+'\')">'+av+'<div class="chatMeta"><div class="chatName"><b>'+escapeHtml(c.name||'User')+'</b>'+(c.username&&!c.group?'<small>@'+escapeHtml(c.username)+'</small>':'')+'</div><div class="chatPreview">'+escapeHtml(c.preview||'No messages yet')+'</div></div><div class="chatSide"><small>'+escapeHtml(c.time||'')+'</small>'+badge+'</div></div>';
 }).join('')||(kpaiRow || '<div class="empty">No chats yet.<br>Tap + to start a new chat.</div>');
}
window.renderChats=renderChats;

/* Status viewer */
let kpStatuses=[],kpStatusIndex=0,kpStatusTimer=null,kpStatusPaused=false,kpCurrentStatus=null;
function statusDisplayDate(v){const d=v?.toDate?v.toDate():new Date(v||Date.now());const now=new Date();const y=new Date(Date.now()-86400000);if(d.toDateString()===now.toDateString())return 'Today';if(d.toDateString()===y.toDateString())return 'Yesterday';return d.toLocaleDateString([], {day:'numeric',month:'short'});}
async function openStatusPage(){
 const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');pv.classList.add('show');
 pi.innerHTML='<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Status</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Updates from KP users</div></div></div><div class="pageCard"><div class="statusComposer"><input class="field" id="statusTextInput" maxlength="180" placeholder="Write a status…"><button class="primary" style="width:auto;padding:0 16px" onclick="publishKPStatus()">Post</button></div><label class="secondary" style="display:grid;place-items:center;margin-top:8px;cursor:pointer">Photo / video<input type="file" id="statusFile" accept="image/*,video/*" hidden onchange="publishKPStatusFile(this.files[0])"></label></div><div class="pageCard"><b>Recent status</b><div id="statusScroller" class="statusScroller"><div class="statusEmpty">Loading statuses…</div></div></div>';
 await loadKPStatuses();
}
async function loadKPStatuses(){
 const box=document.getElementById('statusScroller');if(!box||!fbUser)return;
 try{
   const snap=await fbDb.collection('status').limit(80).get();const now=Date.now();
   kpStatuses=snap.docs.map(d=>({id:d.id,...d.data()})).filter(x=>{const t=kpMillis(x.createdAt)||0;return !t||now-t<86400000}).sort((a,b)=>(kpMillis(b.createdAt)-kpMillis(a.createdAt)));
   box.innerHTML=kpStatuses.map(x=>{const u=kpSenderCache[x.userId]||{};const src=x.photoURL||u.photoURL||'';const name=x.displayName||u.displayName||x.username||'User';return '<div class="statusItem" onclick="openStatusViewer(\''+x.id+'\')"><div class="statusAvatar">'+(src?'<img src="'+escapeHtml(src)+'" alt="">':escapeHtml(name[0].toUpperCase()))+'</div><div class="statusName">'+escapeHtml(name)+'</div><div class="statusTime">'+escapeHtml(statusDisplayDate(x.createdAt))+' · '+escapeHtml(kpTime(x.createdAt))+'</div></div>'}).join('')||'<div class="statusEmpty">No status updates yet.</div>';
 }catch(e){console.error(e);box.innerHTML='<div class="statusEmpty">Could not load statuses.</div>';}
}
async function publishKPStatus(){const text=document.getElementById('statusTextInput')?.value.trim();if(!text)return toast('Write something first');try{await fbDb.collection('status').add({userId:fbUser.uid,username:fbUserProfile?.username||'',displayName:fbUserProfile?.displayName||'KP User',photoURL:fbUserProfile?.photoURL||'',type:'text',text,createdAt:KPServerTS()});toast('Status posted');loadKPStatuses();}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not post status');}}
async function publishKPStatusFile(file){if(!file)return;try{toast('Uploading status…');const up=await cloudinaryUpload(file);await fbDb.collection('status').add({userId:fbUser.uid,username:fbUserProfile?.username||'',displayName:fbUserProfile?.displayName||'KP User',photoURL:fbUserProfile?.photoURL||'',type:file.type.startsWith('video/')?'video':'image',mediaURL:up.secure_url,createdAt:KPServerTS()});toast('Status posted');loadKPStatuses();}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not post status');}}
function openStatusViewer(id){kpStatusIndex=kpStatuses.findIndex(x=>x.id===id);if(kpStatusIndex<0)return;document.getElementById('statusViewer')?.classList.add('show');renderCurrentStatus();}
function closeStatusViewer(){clearTimeout(kpStatusTimer);document.getElementById('statusViewer')?.classList.remove('show');document.getElementById('statusMenu')?.classList.remove('show');kpCurrentStatus=null;}
function renderCurrentStatus(){
 clearTimeout(kpStatusTimer);const x=kpStatuses[kpStatusIndex];if(!x)return closeStatusViewer();kpCurrentStatus=x;const u=kpSenderCache[x.userId]||{};const name=x.displayName||u.displayName||x.username||'User';const src=x.photoURL||u.photoURL||'';
 document.getElementById('statusViewerName').textContent=name;document.getElementById('statusViewerTime').textContent=statusDisplayDate(x.createdAt)+' · '+kpTime(x.createdAt);document.getElementById('statusViewerAvatar').innerHTML=src?'<img src="'+escapeHtml(src)+'" alt="">':escapeHtml(name[0].toUpperCase());document.getElementById('statusDeleteBtn').style.display=x.userId===fbUser.uid?'block':'none';
 const body=document.getElementById('statusViewerBody');body.innerHTML=x.type==='image'&&x.mediaURL?'<img src="'+escapeHtml(x.mediaURL)+'" alt="Status">':x.type==='video'&&x.mediaURL?'<video src="'+escapeHtml(x.mediaURL)+'" autoplay playsinline></video>':'<div class="statusViewerText">'+escapeHtml(x.text||'')+'</div>';
 const progress=document.getElementById('statusProgress');progress.innerHTML=kpStatuses.map((_,i)=>'<i class="'+(i<kpStatusIndex?'done':'')+'"><span></span></i>').join('');const active=progress.children[kpStatusIndex];if(active){active.querySelector('span').style.transition='width 6s linear';requestAnimationFrame(()=>active.querySelector('span').style.width='100%');kpStatusTimer=setTimeout(nextStatus,6000);}
 body.onpointerdown=()=>{clearTimeout(kpStatusTimer);kpStatusPaused=true};body.onpointerup=()=>{if(kpStatusPaused){kpStatusPaused=false;nextStatus()}};body.onpointercancel=()=>{kpStatusPaused=false;nextStatus()};
}
function nextStatus(){kpStatusPaused=false;if(kpStatusIndex<kpStatuses.length-1){kpStatusIndex++;renderCurrentStatus()}else closeStatusViewer();}
function toggleStatusMenu(){document.getElementById('statusMenu')?.classList.toggle('show')}
async function deleteCurrentStatus(){const x=kpCurrentStatus;if(!x||x.userId!==fbUser.uid)return toast('You can delete only your own status');if(!confirm('Delete this status?'))return;try{await fbDb.collection('status').doc(x.id).delete();closeStatusViewer();await loadKPStatuses();toast('Status deleted')}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not delete status')}}
async function openStatusAuthorChat(){const x=kpCurrentStatus;if(!x)return;if(x.userId===fbUser.uid)return toggleStatusMenu();try{await startChatWithUser(x.userId);closeStatusViewer()}catch(e){toast('Could not open chat')}}
async function replyToStatus(){const x=kpCurrentStatus,input=document.getElementById('statusReplyInput'),text=input?.value.trim();if(!x||!text||!fbUser)return;if(x.userId===fbUser.uid)return toast('You cannot reply to your own status');try{const chatId=await ensurePrivateChat(x.userId,x.displayName||x.username||'User','');const ids=[fbUser.uid,x.userId].sort();await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(chatId).collection('messages').add({senderId:fbUser.uid,type:'text',text,createdAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),statusId:x.id,statusOwnerId:x.userId,statusPreview:x.text||x.type||'Status',replyTo:null,media:null,reactions:{},deliveredTo:[],readBy:[]});const unread={};unread[fbUser.uid]=0;unread[x.userId]=1;await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(chatId).set({memberIds:ids,lastMessage:text,lastMessageAt:firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),lastMessageSenderId:fbUser.uid,unreadCount:unread},{merge:true});input.value='';toast('Status reply sent');closeStatusViewer();}catch(e){console.error(e);toast('Could not send status reply')}}

<script id="calling-upgrade-js">
/* ===== UPGRADED CALLING WITH SPEAKING DETECTION ===== */
let kpAudioCtx = null;
let kpRemoteAnalyser = null;
let kpLocalAnalyser = null;
let kpSpeakingInterval = null;
let kpRemoteSpeaking = false;
let kpLocalSpeaking = false;

function kpEnsureAudioCtx(){
  if(!kpAudioCtx){
    try{
      kpAudioCtx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    }catch(e){ console.warn('AudioContext not supported', e); return null; }
  }
  if(kpAudioCtx.state==='suspended'){ kpAudioCtx.resume().catch(()=>{}); }
  return kpAudioCtx;
}

function kpCreateAnalyser(stream){
  const ctx = kpEnsureAudioCtx();
  if(!ctx || !stream) return null;
  try{
    const source = ctx.createMediaStreamSource(stream);
    const analyser = ctx.createAnalyser();
    analyser.fftSize = 256;
    analyser.smoothingTimeConstant = 0.6;
    source.connect(analyser);
    return analyser;
  }catch(e){ console.warn('analyser create failed', e); return null; }
}

function kpGetVolume(analyser){
  if(!analyser) return 0;
  const data = new Uint8Array(analyser.frequencyBinCount);
  analyser.getByteFrequencyData(data);
  let sum = 0;
  // focus on voice frequencies (skip very low)
  for(let i=4;i<data.length*0.6;i++) sum+=data[i];
  return sum / (data.length*0.6);
}

function kpUpdateSpeakingUI(){
  const remoteVol = kpGetVolume(kpRemoteAnalyser);
  const localVol = kpGetVolume(kpLocalAnalyser);
  const remoteThreshold = 12; // tweak
  const localThreshold = 14;
  
  const isRemoteSpeaking = remoteVol > remoteThreshold;
  const isLocalSpeaking = localVol > localThreshold;

  // Remote avatar animation
  const wrapper = document.getElementById('callAvatarWrapper');
  const sub = document.getElementById('callSub');
  const waves = document.getElementById('remoteWaves');
  if(wrapper){
    if(isRemoteSpeaking && !wrapper.classList.contains('speaking')){
      wrapper.classList.add('speaking');
      if(sub){ sub.textContent='Speaking...'; sub.classList.add('speaking'); }
      // animate wave heights based on volume
      if(waves){
        const bars = waves.querySelectorAll('i');
        bars.forEach(b=>{
          const h = 8 + Math.min(18, remoteVol*0.6 + Math.random()*6);
          b.style.height = h+'px';
        });
      }
    } else if(!isRemoteSpeaking && wrapper.classList.contains('speaking')){
      // small debounce to avoid flicker
      setTimeout(()=>{
        const volNow = kpGetVolume(kpRemoteAnalyser);
        if(volNow < remoteThreshold){
          wrapper.classList.remove('speaking');
          if(sub && sub.textContent==='Speaking...'){ sub.textContent='Listening...'; sub.classList.remove('speaking'); }
        }
      }, 300);
    }
  }

  // Local preview animation
  const localPrev = document.getElementById('localPreview');
  const localBars = document.getElementById('localBars');
  if(localPrev){
    if(isLocalSpeaking){
      localPrev.classList.add('speaking');
      if(localBars){
        const bars = localBars.querySelectorAll('i');
        bars.forEach(b=>{
          const h = 6 + Math.min(14, localVol*0.5 + Math.random()*4);
          b.style.height = h+'px';
        });
      }
    } else {
      // debounce
      setTimeout(()=>{
        if(kpGetVolume(kpLocalAnalyser) < localThreshold){
          localPrev?.classList.remove('speaking');
        }
      }, 250);
    }
  }

  // Update global flags
  kpRemoteSpeaking = isRemoteSpeaking;
  kpLocalSpeaking = isLocalSpeaking;
}

function kpStartSpeakingDetection(){
  kpStopSpeakingDetection();
  kpSpeakingInterval = setInterval(kpUpdateSpeakingUI, 110);
}

function kpStopSpeakingDetection(){
  if(kpSpeakingInterval){ clearInterval(kpSpeakingInterval); kpSpeakingInterval=null; }
  kpRemoteSpeaking=false; kpLocalSpeaking=false;
  const w=document.getElementById('callAvatarWrapper');
  if(w) w.classList.remove('speaking');
  const lp=document.getElementById('localPreview');
  if(lp) lp.classList.remove('speaking');
}

// Override showCallModal with upgraded UI

      <button class="callAction accept" onclick="acceptIncomingCall()" title="Answer"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 4.5 9 4l2 4-2 2a13 13 0 0 0 5 5l2-2 4 2-.5 3a2 2 0 0 1-2.2 1.5C9.5 18.2 5.8 14.5 4.5 6.7A2 2 0 0 1 6 4.5Z"/></svg></button>

      <button class="callAction end" onclick="endVoiceCall()" title="End call"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>

  }
  if(typeof callPageLocked!=='undefined') callPageLocked=true;

// Override hideCallModal
const _oldHideCallModal = window.hideCallModal;
window.hideCallModal = function(){
  kpStopSpeakingDetection();
  // cleanup analysers
  kpRemoteAnalyser=null;
  kpLocalAnalyser=null;
  try{ if(kpAudioCtx && kpAudioCtx.state!=='closed'){ /* keep ctx for reuse */ } }catch(e){}
  const o=document.getElementById('callOverlay');
  if(o){ o.classList.remove('show'); o.classList.remove('incoming'); }
  if(typeof callPageLocked!=='undefined') callPageLocked=false;
  if(typeof _oldHideCallModal==='function' && _oldHideCallModal!==window.hideCallModal){
    // avoid recursion
  }
};

// Override bindCall to add analysers
const _oldBindCall = window.bindCall;
window.bindCall = function(call, name, initial){
  // call original logic for stream handling but we wrap
  call.on('stream', remoteStream=>{
    const audio=document.getElementById('remoteAudio');
    if(audio){ audio.srcObject=remoteStream; audio.play().catch(()=>{}); }
    // create remote analyser
    kpRemoteAnalyser = kpCreateAnalyser(remoteStream);
    // show active UI
    if(window.showCallModal) showCallModal('active', name, initial);
    if(typeof startCallTimer==='function') startCallTimer();
    kpStartSpeakingDetection();
  });
  call.on('close', ()=>{ 
    kpStopSpeakingDetection();
    if(typeof endVoiceCall==='function') endVoiceCall(false);
  });
  call.on('error', err=>{
    console.warn('Call error', err);
    kpStopSpeakingDetection();
    if(typeof endVoiceCall==='function') endVoiceCall(false);
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Voice call ended');
  });
  // Also if already has localStream, create analyser for it
  if(typeof localStream!=='undefined' && localStream){
    kpLocalAnalyser = kpCreateAnalyser(localStream);
    kpStartSpeakingDetection();
  }
};

// Override ensureLocalAudio to hook local analyser
const _oldEnsureLocalAudio = window.ensureLocalAudio;
window.ensureLocalAudio = function(){
  const p = (_oldEnsureLocalAudio ? _oldEnsureLocalAudio() : navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({audio:true,video:false}));
  return p.then(stream=>{
    kpLocalAnalyser = kpCreateAnalyser(stream);
    kpStartSpeakingDetection();
    return stream;
  });
};

// Enhanced toggleMute with UI feedback
const _oldToggleMute = window.toggleMute;
window.toggleMute = function(){
  if(typeof localStream==='undefined' || !localStream) return;
  const track = localStream.getAudioTracks()[0];
  if(!track) return;
  track.enabled = !track.enabled;
  const b=document.getElementById('muteCallBtn');
  const localStatus=document.getElementById('localStatus');
  const localPrev=document.getElementById('localPreview');
  if(b){
    b.classList.toggle('muted', !track.enabled);
    b.innerHTML = track.enabled
      ? '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M12 3a3 3 0 0 0-3 3v6a3 3 0 0 0 6 0V6a3 3 0 0 0-3-3Z"/><path d="M19 10v2a7 7 0 0 1-14 0v-2M12 19v3M8 22h8"/></svg>'
      : '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 4 16 16M9 9v3a3 3 0 0 0 5.1 2.1M15 9V6a3 3 0 0 0-5.2-2M19 10v2a7 7 0 0 1-11.9 5M12 19v3M8 22h8"/></svg>';
  }
  if(localStatus) localStatus.textContent = track.enabled ? 'Mic on • Speaking detection active' : 'Mic muted';
  if(localPrev){
    if(!track.enabled) localPrev.classList.remove('speaking');
  }
  if(typeof toast==='function') toast(track.enabled?'Mic on':'Mic muted');
};

// Auto init
console.log('Upgraded Calling UI loaded with speaking detection');
</script>

<script id="nav-fix-js">
// ===== FINAL NAVIGATION FIX - Unifies all nav / page logic =====
(function(){
  console.log('Applying FINAL NAV FIX');

  // Ensure global state exists
  if(typeof callPageLocked==='undefined') window.callPageLocked = false;
  if(typeof previousPageBeforeCall==='undefined') window.previousPageBeforeCall = '';

  // Robust closePage that always clears inline styles
  function finalClosePage(){
    const pv = document.getElementById('pageView');
    if(pv){
      pv.classList.remove('show');
      pv.style.display = ''; // IMPORTANT: clear inline display:none that KPAI left
      pv.style.opacity = '';
    }
    // If we were in a call, don't reset callPageLocked here - it's handled by call modal
    // But if no active call, ensure it's false so navigation isn't blocked
    try{
      if((typeof activeCall==='undefined' || !activeCall) && (typeof incomingCall==='undefined' || !incomingCall)){
        window.callPageLocked = false;
      }
    }catch(e){}
    // Restore body scroll if needed
  }
  window.closePage = finalClosePage;
  window.kpClosePage = finalClosePage;

  // Robust openPageBase
  function finalEnsurePageViewVisible(){
    const pv = document.getElementById('pageView');
    if(!pv) return null;
    pv.style.display = ''; // clear any leftover display:none
    pv.style.opacity = '';
    pv.classList.add('show');
    // Ensure inner scrolls to top
    const pi = document.getElementById('pageInner');
    if(pi) pi.scrollTop = 0;
    return pv;
  }

  // Override openProfilePage to use finalEnsurePageViewVisible
  const _origOpenProfilePage = window.openProfilePage;
  window.openProfilePage = function(){
    if(window.callPageLocked){ if(typeof toast==='function') toast('End the call first'); return; }
    const pv = finalEnsurePageViewVisible();
    const pi = document.getElementById('pageInner');
    if(!pv||!pi) return;
    // Try original if exists and it renders, else fallback to our own render
    if(_origOpenProfilePage && _origOpenProfilePage!==window.openProfilePage){
      // Call original but ensure it also shows
      try{
        const p = (typeof fbUserProfile!=='undefined' && fbUserProfile) ? fbUserProfile : {};
        pi.innerHTML = `
          <div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"/></svg></button><div><h2>Profile</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Your KP WhatsApp profile</div></div></div>
          <div class="pageCard profileHero"><div class="profileAvatarBig">${p.photoURL?`<img src="${p.photoURL}">`:((p.displayName||p.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase())}</div><h2>${(p.displayName||'KP User')}</h2><div class="profileUsernameBadge">@${p.username||''}</div><div style="color:#888;margin-top:8px">${p.bio||'No bio yet'}</div></div>
          <div class="pageCard profileActions"><button class="primary" onclick="if(typeof openProfileEdit==='function') openProfileEdit(); else toast('Edit profile')">Edit profile</button><button class="secondary" onclick="openPage('settings')">Settings</button></div>
          <div class="pageCard"><b>Account</b><div style="color:#777;margin-top:8px">Username login enabled</div><div style="color:#777;margin-top:4px">Phone numbers are not used.</div></div>
        `;
        return;
      }catch(e){ console.warn('openProfilePage fallback', e); }
    }
    // fallback already rendered
  };

  // Override openCallsPage similarly
  const _origOpenCallsPage = window.openCallsPage;
  window.openCallsPage = function(){
    if(window.callPageLocked){ if(typeof toast==='function') toast('End the call first'); return; }
    finalEnsurePageViewVisible();
    if(_origOpenCallsPage && _origOpenCallsPage!==window.openCallsPage){
      try{ return _origOpenCallsPage(); }catch(e){ console.warn(e); }
    }
    // fallback simple
    const pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');
    if(pi) pi.innerHTML = `<div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button><div><h2>Calls</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Voice calls</div></div></div><div class="pageCard">No calls yet</div>`;
  };

  // Override openStatusPage
  const _origOpenStatusPage = window.openStatusPage;
  window.openStatusPage = function(){
    if(window.callPageLocked){ if(typeof toast==='function') toast('End the call first'); return; }
    finalEnsurePageViewVisible();
    if(_origOpenStatusPage && _origOpenStatusPage!==window.openStatusPage){
      try{ return _origOpenStatusPage(); }catch(e){ console.warn(e); }
    }
  };

  // Override openPage central router
  const _origOpenPage = window.openPage;
  window.openPage = function(type, extra=null){
    if(window.callPageLocked && type!=='call'){
      if(typeof toast==='function') toast('End the call first');
      return;
    }
    const pv = finalEnsurePageViewVisible();
    if(!pv) return;
    // Route
    if(type==='profile') return window.openProfilePage();
    if(type==='calls') return window.openCallsPage();
    if(type==='status'){
      if(typeof openStatusPage==='function') return openStatusPage();
    }
    if(type==='settings'){
      const pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');
      if(pi){
        pi.innerHTML = `
          <div class="pageHeader"><button class="pageBack" onclick="closePage()"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"/></svg></button><div><h2>Settings</h2><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">KP WhatsApp</div></div></div>
          <div class="pageCard pageList" onclick="openProfilePage()"><div class="pageIcon">◎</div><div><b>Profile</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Photo, name, username and bio</div></div></div>
          <div class="pageCard pageList" onclick="if(typeof openKPAISettings==='function') openKPAISettings(); else if(window.openKPAI) openKPAI(); else toast('KPAI settings')"><div class="pageIcon">🤖</div><div><b>KPAI Agent</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">API key, mode, image generation</div></div></div>
          <div class="pageCard pageList" onclick="toast('Privacy settings coming soon')"><div class="pageIcon">⌾</div><div><b>Privacy</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Last seen, profile and read receipts</div></div></div>
          <div class="pageCard pageList" onclick="toast('Notifications')"><div class="pageIcon">♢</div><div><b>Notifications</b><div style="color:#777;font-size:12px">Messages and calls</div></div></div>
          <div class="pageCard"><b>About</b><div style="color:#777;margin-top:6px">KP WhatsApp - Upgraded build with speaking animation & full navigation fix</div></div>
        `;
      }
      return;
    }
    // Fallback to original for other types (groupInfo etc)
    if(_origOpenPage && _origOpenPage!==window.openPage){
      try{ return _origOpenPage(type, extra); }catch(e){ console.warn('openPage fallback error', e); }
    }
  };

  // Final unified nav - this is the ONLY nav that should run
  window.nav = function(btn, type){
    try{
      if(window.callPageLocked){
        if(typeof toast==='function') toast('End the call before leaving');
        return;
      }
      // Update active state
      document.querySelectorAll('.nav button').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
      if(btn) btn.classList.add('active');

      if(type==='chats'){
        finalClosePage();
        if(typeof closeChat==='function') closeChat();
        return;
      }
      if(type==='calls'){
        return window.openCallsPage();
      }
      if(type==='profile'){
        return window.openProfilePage();
      }
      if(type==='status'){
        return window.openPage('status');
      }
      // If KPAI nav (custom)
      if(type==='kpai' || (btn && btn.classList.contains('kpaiNav'))){
        if(window.openKPAI) return window.openKPAI();
      }
    }catch(e){ console.error('nav error', e); }
  };

  // Fix KPAI back button to use closePage properly - override kpaiOpenFixed again to ensure back calls closePage
  if(window.kpaiOpenFixed){
    const _origKpaiOpenFixed = window.kpaiOpenFixed;
    window.kpaiOpenFixed = function(){
      try{
        _origKpaiOpenFixed();
        // After rendering, fix back button onclick to use closePage
        setTimeout(()=>{
          const backBtn = document.querySelector('#pageInner .kpaiBack');
          if(backBtn){
            backBtn.onclick = function(){ finalClosePage(); };
          }
        }, 100);
      }catch(e){ console.error('kpaiOpenFixed wrapper', e); if(_origKpaiOpenFixed) _origKpaiOpenFixed(); }
    };
    window.openKPAI = window.kpaiOpenFixed;
  }

  // Ensure closeChat also clears pageView display issues
  const _origCloseChat = window.closeChat;
  window.closeChat = function(){
    try{
      if(_origCloseChat && _origCloseChat!==window.closeChat){
        // Call original logic but not its pageView handling
        const app = document.getElementById('app');
        if(app) app.classList.remove('chat-open');
        const ov = document.getElementById('chatLoadingOverlay');
        if(ov) ov.classList.remove('show');
        if(typeof current!=='undefined' && current){
          current.unread=0;
          if(typeof renderChats==='function') renderChats();
          if(typeof markChatRead==='function') markChatRead(current).catch(()=>{});
        }
      } else {
        const app=document.getElementById('app'); if(app) app.classList.remove('chat-open');
      }
    }catch(e){ console.warn(e); }
  };

  // Fix all elements with onclick="closePage()" that might have been set to inline display:none before - ensure they now call finalClosePage
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
    const target = e.target.closest('[onclick*="pageView"][onclick*="classList.remove"]');
    if(target){
      // If someone clicks old style back button that does direct classList.remove, intercept
      const pv = document.getElementById('pageView');
      if(pv && pv.style.display==='none'){
        pv.style.display='';
      }
    }
  }, true);

  // Reset callPageLocked on page load
  window.callPageLocked = false;

  console.log('FINAL NAV FIX applied - profile, settings, calls, status should now work after KPAI');
})();
</script>


<script id="api-status-js">
// ===== API GREEN/RED STATUS INDICATOR =====
async function checkKPAIApiStatus(showToast=true){
  const dot = document.getElementById('kpaiStatusDot');
  const text = document.getElementById('kpaiStatusText');
  const headerDot = document.getElementById('kpaiHeaderStatusDot');
  const banner = document.getElementById('kpaiUnavailableBanner');
  
  const apiKey = (localStorage.getItem('kpai_api_key')||'').trim();
  const mode = localStorage.getItem('kpai_agent_mode')||'full';
  const imgModel = localStorage.getItem('kpai_image_model')||'pollinations';
  
  function setStatus(state, title, sub){
    if(!dot || !text) return;
    dot.className = 'kpaiStatusDot '+state;
    if(state==='green'){
      text.innerHTML = `<b style="color:#4ade80">${title}</b><small>${sub}</small>`;
    } else if(state==='red'){
      text.innerHTML = `<b style="color:#ff7a7a">${title}</b><small>${sub}</small>`;
    } else {
      text.innerHTML = `<b style="color:#fbbf24">${title}</b><small>${sub}</small>`;
    }
    if(headerDot){
      headerDot.className = 'kpaiHeaderStatus '+state;
      headerDot.title = title + ' - ' + sub;
    }
    // Show/hide unavailable banner in main chat
    if(banner){
      if(state==='red'){
        banner.classList.add('show');
        banner.querySelector('.msg').textContent = sub;
      } else {
        banner.classList.remove('show');
      }
    }
    // Update composer placeholder
    const input = document.getElementById('kpaiInput');
    if(input){
      if(state==='red'){
        input.placeholder = 'API ലഭ്യമല്ല - Settings-ൽ Key add ചെയ്യുക...';
      } else if(state==='green' && imgModel==='pollinations'){
        input.placeholder = 'Message KPAI (Free mode - Green ✅) or /image prompt';
      } else {
        input.placeholder = 'Message KPAI (API Green ✅)';
      }
    }
  }

  // Pollinations free mode is always green
  if(imgModel==='pollinations'){
    setStatus('green', 'API പൂർണമായും വർക്ക് ചെയ്യുന്നു ✅', 'Free mode (Pollinations) - Key വേണ്ട, Image + Chat ready');
    if(showToast && typeof toast==='function') toast('API Green - Free mode working ✅');
    return true;
  }

  // If no key
  if(!apiKey){
    setStatus('red', 'API ലഭ്യമല്ല 🔴', 'Gemini Key ഇല്ല - Settings-ൽ API Key paste ചെയ്യുക അല്ലെങ്കിൽ Free mode select ചെയ്യുക');
    if(showToast && typeof toast==='function') toast('API Red - Key ഇല്ല');
    return false;
  }

  // Test Gemini key with a tiny request
  setStatus('yellow', 'API പരിശോധിക്കുന്നു...', 'Gemini Key test ചെയ്യുന്നു...');
  try{
    const testUrl = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models?key=${apiKey}`;
    const resp = await fetch(testUrl);
    const data = await resp.json().catch(()=>({}));
    if(resp.ok && data.models){
      setStatus('green', 'API പൂർണമായും വർക്ക് ചെയ്യുന്നു ✅', `Gemini Key valid - ${data.models.length} models available - Mode: ${mode}`);
      if(showToast && typeof toast==='function') toast('API Green - Key valid ✅');
      // Save valid flag
      localStorage.setItem('kpai_api_valid', '1');
      return true;
    } else {
      const errMsg = data.error?.message || `HTTP ${resp.status}`;
      setStatus('red', 'API Key തെറ്റ് / ലഭ്യമല്ല 🔴', errMsg + ' - Key ശരിയാണോ എന്ന് നോക്ക്');
      if(showToast && typeof toast==='function') toast('API Red - '+errMsg);
      localStorage.setItem('kpai_api_valid', '0');
      return false;
    }
  }catch(err){
    console.error('API check failed', err);
    setStatus('red', 'API പരിശോധിക്കാൻ കഴിഞ്ഞില്ല 🔴', (err.message||'Network error') + ' - Internet നോക്കുക അല്ലെങ്കിൽ Free mode ഉപയോഗിക്കുക');
    if(showToast && typeof toast==='function') toast('API Red - Network error');
    return false;
  }
}
window.checkKPAIApiStatus = checkKPAIApiStatus;

  if(_origKpaiOpenFixedForStatus) _origKpaiOpenFixedForStatus();
  // After UI rendered, check status and inject header dot and banner
  setTimeout(()=>{
    // Inject header status dot if not exists
    const headText = document.querySelector('.kpaiHeadText small');
    if(headText && !document.getElementById('kpaiHeaderStatusDot')){
      headText.innerHTML += ' <span class="kpaiHeaderStatus red" id="kpaiHeaderStatusDot" title="Checking..."></span>';
    }
    // Inject unavailable banner into composer area if not exists
    const shell = document.querySelector('.kpaiShell');
    const composer = document.querySelector('.kpaiComposer');
    if(shell && composer && !document.getElementById('kpaiUnavailableBanner')){
      const banner = document.createElement('div');
      banner.className = 'kpaiUnavailableBanner';
      banner.id = 'kpaiUnavailableBanner';
      banner.innerHTML = `<div class="icon">!</div><div style="flex:1"><b style="color:#ff9a9a">API ലഭ്യമല്ല</b><div class="msg" style="font-size:11px;color:#c99">Settings-ൽ API Key add ചെയ്യുക അല്ലെങ്കിൽ Free mode select ചെയ്യുക</div></div><button class="kpaiTestBtn" onclick="openKPAISettings()">Settings</button>`;
      shell.insertBefore(banner, composer);
    }
    // Run check
    checkKPAIApiStatus(false);
  }, 300);
};

window.sendKPAI = async function(){
  const apiKey = (localStorage.getItem('kpai_api_key')||'').trim();
  const imgModel = localStorage.getItem('kpai_image_model')||'pollinations';
  const isValid = localStorage.getItem('kpai_api_valid');
  
  // If red status and not free mode, show warning before sending
  if(!apiKey && imgModel!=='pollinations'){
    // Show red banner and message
    const banner = document.getElementById('kpaiUnavailableBanner');
    if(banner) banner.classList.add('show');
    
    // Add system message to chat
    const body = document.getElementById('kpaiBody');
    const input = document.getElementById('kpaiInput');
    const t = input?.value.trim()||'';
    if(t && body){
      // Let original send handle it, but it will fallback to local
      // Add extra warning
      if(typeof kpaiAppend==='function'){
        kpaiAppend('⚠️ API ലഭ്യമല്ല (Red) - Free mode-ൽ ആണ് reply തരുന്നത്. Full AI വേണമെങ്കിൽ Settings-ൽ Gemini Key add ചെയ്യുക അല്ലെങ്കിൽ Pollinations (Free) select ചെയ്യുക.', false);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // Call original
  if(_origSendKPAIForStatus) return _origSendKPAIForStatus();
};

// Also enhance saveKPAISettings to auto check after save
const _origSaveSettings = window.saveKPAISettings;
window.saveKPAISettings = async function(){
  if(_origSaveSettings) await _origSaveSettings();
  setTimeout(()=>{ checkKPAIApiStatus(true); }, 500);
};

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ()=>{ setTimeout(()=>checkKPAIApiStatus(false), 1500); });

console.log('API Status Green/Red indicator loaded');
