<script id="ultimate-pro-js">
// ===== ULTIMATE PRO FIXES =====
console.log('Applying ULTIMATE PRO fixes');

// 1. FULL SCREEN CALLING - ensure from anywhere
(function(){
  const origHandleIncoming = window.handleIncomingCall;
  window.handleIncomingCall = function(call){
    // Always show calling screen immediately, from any page
    console.log('Incoming call from anywhere, showing full screen');
    const meta = call.metadata||{};
    const callerName = meta.name||meta.username||'Incoming caller';
    const photo = meta.photoURL||'';
    // Ensure pageView is closed to show call full screen
    if(window.closePage) closePage();
    if(window.showCallModal){
      showCallModal('incoming', callerName, (callerName[0]||'U').toUpperCase(), photo);
      // Play ringtone
      if(typeof playRingtone==='function') playRingtone();
    }
    // Call original if needed
    if(origHandleIncoming && origHandleIncoming!==window.handleIncomingCall){
      // avoid double
      window.incomingCall = call;
    } else {
      window.incomingCall = call;
    }
  };
  
  // Ensure callOverlay is always full black
  const callOverlay = document.getElementById('callOverlay');
  if(callOverlay){
    callOverlay.style.background = '#000';
    callOverlay.style.inset = '0';
    callOverlay.style.width = '100vw';
    callOverlay.style.height = '100dvh';
  }
})();

// 2. GROUP LOADING - improve
window.refreshChats = async function(){
  const btn = document.getElementById('refreshChatsBtn');
  if(btn) btn.classList.add('loading');
  try{
    if(typeof loadFirebaseChats==='function'){
      await loadFirebaseChats();
    }
    if(typeof renderChats==='function') renderChats();
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Chats refreshed ✅');
  }catch(e){
    console.error(e);
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Refresh failed');
  }finally{
    if(btn) btn.classList.remove('loading');
  }
};

// Add refresh button to new chat page and home
function addRefreshButton(){
  const plusBtn = document.querySelector('.newChatFab, [onclick*="openRealUsers"], .headBtns');
  const chatList = document.getElementById('chatList');
  if(chatList && !document.getElementById('refreshChatsBtn')){
    const refreshDiv = document.createElement('div');
    refreshDiv.innerHTML = `
      <button id="refreshChatsBtn" class="refreshChatBtn" onclick="refreshChats()">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M3 12a9 9 0 1 0 9-9 9.75 9.75 0 0 0-6.74 2.74L3 8"/><path d="M3 3v5h5"/><path d="M21 12a9 9 0 1 1-9 9 9.75 9.75 0 0 1 6.74-2.74L21 16"/><path d="M21 21v-5h-5"/></svg>
        Refresh Chats
      </button>
    `;
    chatList.parentNode.insertBefore(refreshDiv.firstElementChild, chatList);
  }
}
setTimeout(addRefreshButton, 1000);
setTimeout(addRefreshButton, 3000);

// 3 & 4. REAL TICKS - blue tick when seen
async function markMessageAsRead(messageId, chatId){
  if(!fbUser || !messageId || !chatId) return;
  try{
    await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(chatId).collection('messages').doc(messageId).update({
      readBy: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.arrayUnion(fbUser.uid),
      deliveredTo: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.arrayUnion(fbUser.uid)
    });
  }catch(e){ console.warn('mark read failed', e); }
}

async function markMessageAsDelivered(messageId, chatId){
  if(!fbUser || !messageId || !chatId) return;
  try{
    await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(chatId).collection('messages').doc(messageId).update({
      deliveredTo: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.arrayUnion(fbUser.uid)
    });
  }catch(e){}
}

// Enhance renderMessages to mark as delivered/read when viewing
const _origRenderMessagesForTicks = window.renderMessages;
window.renderMessages = function(){
  if(_origRenderMessagesForTicks) _origRenderMessagesForTicks();
  // Mark all visible incoming messages as read
  try{
    const list = current?.messages||[];
    const chatId = current?.firestoreId;
    if(!chatId || !fbUser) return;
    list.forEach(m=>{
      const isIncoming = !['me','me-gif','me-media'].includes(m[0]);
      const docId = m[5]?.id;
      const raw = m[5]?.raw;
      if(isIncoming && docId && raw){
        const alreadyRead = raw.readBy?.includes(fbUser.uid);
        if(!alreadyRead){
          markMessageAsRead(docId, chatId);
        } else {
          const alreadyDelivered = raw.deliveredTo?.includes(fbUser.uid);
          if(!alreadyDelivered){
            markMessageAsDelivered(docId, chatId);
          }
        }
      }
    });
  }catch(e){ console.warn(e); }
};

// 5. GROUP ADMIN PROFILE CHANGE - ensure works
window.openGroupProfileChanger = function(){
  const input = document.createElement('input');
  input.type='file';
  input.accept='image/*,image/gif';
  input.onchange = async (e)=>{
    const file = e.target.files[0];
    if(!file) return;
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Uploading group photo...');
    try{
      // Use existing upload function if available
      if(typeof uploadToCloudinary==='function'){
        const url = await uploadToCloudinary(file);
        if(url && current?.firestoreId){
          await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId).update({avatar:url, avatarType: file.type.includes('gif')?'gif':'image'});
          current.avatar = url;
          if(typeof renderChats==='function') renderChats();
          if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Group photo updated ✅');
        }
      }
    }catch(err){
      console.error(err);
      if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Upload failed');
    }
  };
  input.click();
};

// Attach to group avatar click
document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
  const avatar = e.target.closest('.groupDetailAvatar');
  if(avatar && current?.group){
    const isAdmin = current.adminIds?.includes(fbUser?.uid);
    if(isAdmin){
      if(confirm('Change group photo?')) window.openGroupProfileChanger();
    }
  }
});

// 6. STATUS FIX - prevent download, fix bottom menu
(function(){
  // Disable right click and drag on status images
  document.addEventListener('contextmenu', function(e){
    if(e.target.closest('.statusViewerBody img, .statusViewerBody video')){
      e.preventDefault();
      return false;
    }
  });
  document.addEventListener('dragstart', function(e){
    if(e.target.closest('.statusViewerBody img')){
      e.preventDefault();
      return false;
    }
  });
  
  // Ensure nav doesn't disappear permanently after status
  const _origCloseStatusViewer = window.closeStatusViewer;
  window.closeStatusViewer = function(){
    if(_origCloseStatusViewer) _origCloseStatusViewer();
    // Restore nav if hidden
    const nav = document.querySelector('.nav');
    if(nav) nav.style.display='';
    // Ensure pageView closed
    if(window.closePage) closePage();
  };
})();

// 7. LONG PRESS MULTI-SELECT DELETE
window.selectedMessageIds = window.selectedMessageIds || new Set();
window.selectMessage = function(docId, idx){
  if(!docId) return;
  if(window.selectedMessageIds.has(docId)){
    window.selectedMessageIds.delete(docId);
  } else {
    window.selectedMessageIds.add(docId);
  }
  // Show select bar
  const bar = document.getElementById('messageSelectBar') || document.querySelector('.messageSelectBar');
  if(bar){
    if(window.selectedMessageIds.size>0){
      bar.classList.add('show');
      const countEl = bar.querySelector('.count');
      if(countEl) countEl.textContent = window.selectedMessageIds.size + ' selected';
    } else {
      bar.classList.remove('show');
    }
  }
  // Update UI
  if(typeof renderMessages==='function'){
    // Don't full re-render, just toggle class
    document.querySelectorAll('.msg[data-message-id]').forEach(el=>{
      const id = el.dataset.messageId;
      if(window.selectedMessageIds.has(id)){
        el.classList.add('msgLongSelected');
      } else {
        el.classList.remove('msgLongSelected');
      }
    });
  }
};

window.deleteSelectedMessages = async function(){
  if(window.selectedMessageIds.size===0) return;
  if(!confirm(`Delete ${window.selectedMessageIds.size} messages?`)) return;
  const chatId = current?.firestoreId;
  if(!chatId) return;
  try{
    for(const id of window.selectedMessageIds){
      await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(chatId).collection('messages').doc(id).delete();
    }
    window.selectedMessageIds.clear();
    const bar = document.querySelector('.messageSelectBar');
    if(bar) bar.classList.remove('show');
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Messages deleted');
    // Remove from local
    if(current?.messages){
      current.messages = current.messages.filter(m=>!window.selectedMessageIds.has(m[5]?.id));
    }
    if(typeof renderMessages==='function') renderMessages();
  }catch(e){
    console.error(e);
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Delete failed');
  }
};

window.cancelSelection = function(){
  window.selectedMessageIds.clear();
  const bar = document.querySelector('.messageSelectBar');
  if(bar) bar.classList.remove('show');
  document.querySelectorAll('.msgLongSelected').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('msgLongSelected'));
};

function bindLongPressMessages(){
  const box = document.getElementById('messages');
  if(!box) return;
  box.querySelectorAll('.msg[data-message-id]').forEach(el=>{
    let timer=null;
    let startX=0, startY=0;
    
    el.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e){
      startX = e.touches[0].clientX;
      startY = e.touches[0].clientY;
      timer = setTimeout(()=>{
        const id = el.dataset.messageId;
        const idx = Number(el.dataset.messageIndex);
        window.selectMessage(id, idx);
        if(navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(50);
      }, 600);
    }, {passive:true});
    
    el.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e){
      const dx = Math.abs(e.touches[0].clientX - startX);
      const dy = Math.abs(e.touches[0].clientY - startY);
      if(dx>10 || dy>10){
        clearTimeout(timer);
      }
    }, {passive:true});
    
    el.addEventListener('touchend', ()=>{ clearTimeout(timer); });
    
    el.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e){
      timer = setTimeout(()=>{
        const id = el.dataset.messageId;
        const idx = Number(el.dataset.messageIndex);
        window.selectMessage(id, idx);
      }, 700);
    });
    
    el.addEventListener('mouseup', ()=>{ clearTimeout(timer); });
    el.addEventListener('mouseleave', ()=>{ clearTimeout(timer); });
    
    // Tap to select when in selection mode
    el.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      if(window.selectedMessageIds.size>0){
        e.preventDefault();
        const id = el.dataset.messageId;
        const idx = Number(el.dataset.messageIndex);
        window.selectMessage(id, idx);
      }
    });
  });
}
window.bindLongPressMessages = bindLongPressMessages;

// Ensure select bar exists
function ensureSelectBar(){
  if(!document.querySelector('.messageSelectBar')){
    const bar = document.createElement('div');
    bar.className = 'messageSelectBar';
    bar.innerHTML = `
      <button onclick="cancelSelection()" style="background:none; border:none; color:#aaa; font-size:20px">✕</button>
      <span class="count" style="flex:1; color:#fff; font-weight:600">0 selected</span>
      <button onclick="deleteSelectedMessages()" style="background:#ff3045; border:none; color:white; padding:8px 16px; border-radius:20px; font-weight:700; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:6px">
        <svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M3 6h18"/><path d="M8 6V4h8v2"/><path d="M19 6v14a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V6"/></svg>
        Delete
      </button>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(bar);
  }
}
ensureSelectBar();
setTimeout(ensureSelectBar, 2000);

// 8. MORE GIFS - expand queries
window.gifCategory = function(btn, cat, forGroup){
  document.querySelectorAll('.gifTabs button').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  const queries = {
    malayali: 'malayalam malayali reaction kerala',
    mood: 'angry happy sad reaction mood emotion',
    love: 'love heart romantic kiss couple',
    football: 'football soccer goal celebration ronaldo neymar messi football skills',
    gaming: 'gaming gamer game reaction pubg free fire fortnite',
    trending: 'trending viral meme funny'
  };
  const q = queries[cat] || cat;
  const input = document.getElementById('gifSearch');
  if(input) input.value = '';
  if(typeof searchGifs==='function') searchGifs(q, forGroup);
};

window.searchGifs = async function(query, forGroup){
  const grid = document.getElementById('gifGrid');
  if(!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#777;padding:12px;grid-column:1/-1">Loading GIFs…</div>';
  try{
    // Use Giphy API or Tenor - fallback to more results
    const apiKey = 'GlVGYHkr3WSBnllca54iNt0yFbjz7L65';
    const limit = 32; // More GIFs
    const url = `https://api.giphy.com/v1/gifs/search?api_key=${apiKey}&q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=${limit}&rating=pg`;
    const res = await fetch(url);
    const data = await res.json();
    const gifs = data.data||[];
    if(gifs.length===0){
      grid.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#777;padding:12px;grid-column:1/-1">No GIFs found, try another search</div>';
      return;
    }
    grid.innerHTML = gifs.map(g=>{
      const gifUrl = g.images?.fixed_height?.url || g.images?.original?.url;
      return `<img src="${gifUrl}" loading="lazy" onclick="if(typeof selectGif==='function') selectGif('${gifUrl}', ${forGroup})" alt="GIF">`;
    }).join('');
  }catch(e){
    console.error('GIF search failed', e);
    grid.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#ff7a7a;padding:12px;grid-column:1/-1">GIF load failed, check internet</div>';
  }
};

// 9. NOTIFICATIONS - phone notifications
window.kpRequestNotificationPermission = async function(){
  if(!('Notification' in window)){
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Notifications not supported');
    return;
  }
  const perm = await Notification.requestPermission();
  if(perm==='granted'){
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Notifications enabled ✅');
    localStorage.setItem('kp_notifications_enabled', '1');
    document.querySelector('.kpNotificationPermission')?.classList.remove('show');
  } else {
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Notifications denied');
  }
};

window.kpShowNotification = function(title, body, icon){
  if(Notification.permission!=='granted') return;
  try{
    const n = new Notification(title, {body, icon: icon||'/favicon.ico', badge:'/favicon.ico'});
    n.onclick = function(){ window.focus(); n.close(); };
    // Vibrate
    if(navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([200,100,200]);
    // Sound
    const audio = new Audio('data:audio/wav;base64,UklGRigAAABXQVZFZm10IBAAAAABAAEARKwAAIhYAQACABAAZGF0YQQAAAAAAA==');
    audio.play().catch(()=>{});
  }catch(e){ console.warn('Notification failed', e); }
};

// Show permission banner if not granted
function checkNotificationPermission(){
  if(!('Notification' in window)) return;
  if(Notification.permission==='default' && !localStorage.getItem('kp_notifications_dismissed')){
    const banner = document.createElement('div');
    banner.className = 'kpNotificationPermission show';
    banner.innerHTML = `
      <div class="icon">🔔</div>
      <div class="content"><b>Enable Notifications?</b><small>Get notified for calls & messages even when app is in background</small></div>
      <button onclick="kpRequestNotificationPermission()">Allow</button>
      <button onclick="this.parentElement.remove(); localStorage.setItem('kp_notifications_dismissed','1')" style="background:transparent; border:1px solid #333; color:#777">Later</button>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(banner);
  }
}
setTimeout(checkNotificationPermission, 3000);

// Hook into Firebase message listener for notifications
const _origStartListener = window.startFirebaseMessageListener;
window.startFirebaseMessageListener = function(c){
  if(_origStartListener) _origStartListener(c);
  // Also listen for new messages for notification
  if(!c?.firestoreId || !fbUser) return;
  // Additional listener for notifications (separate)
  try{
    fbDb.collection('chats').doc(c.firestoreId).collection('messages').orderBy('createdAt','desc').limit(1).onSnapshot(snap=>{
      snap.docChanges().forEach(change=>{
        if(change.type==='added'){
          const data = change.doc.data();
          const isMe = data.senderId===fbUser.uid;
          if(!isMe && document.hidden){
            // Show notification if tab not visible
            const senderName = current?.name||'KP WhatsApp';
            const text = data.text|| (data.type==='gif'?'Sent a GIF':'Sent media');
            window.kpShowNotification(senderName, text);
          }
        }
      });
    });
  }catch(e){}
};

// Incoming call notification
const _origPlayRingtoneForNotif = window.playRingtone;
window.playRingtone = function(){
  if(_origPlayRingtoneForNotif) _origPlayRingtoneForNotif();
  if(document.hidden && Notification.permission==='granted'){
    window.kpShowNotification('Incoming Call 📞', 'Someone is calling you on KP WhatsApp');
  }
};

}

<script id="group-call-js">
// ===== GROUP CALLING FEATURE =====
console.log('Group Calling feature loading...');

window.groupCallState = {
  active: false,
  groupId: null,
  callId: null,
  participants: [], // {uid, name, peerId, photoURL, status: 'ringing'|'joined'|'muted', stream, analyser}
  localStream: null,
  peerCalls: new Map(), // uid -> peer call
  startedAt: null,
  timerInt: null,
  fbUnsub: null
};

function generateGroupCallId(groupId){
  return groupId + '_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8);
}

async function startGroupCall(){
  if(!current?.group){
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Group calls work only in groups');
    return;
  }
  if(groupCallState.active){
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Already in a group call');
    return;
  }
  if(!fbUser || !fbDb){
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Please sign in');
    return;
  }
  
  const groupId = current.firestoreId;
  const callId = generateGroupCallId(groupId);
  const memberIds = current.memberIds || [];
  
  if(memberIds.length < 2){
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Need at least 2 members for group call');
    return;
  }
  
  try{
    // Get local audio
    const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({audio:true, video:false});
    groupCallState.localStream = stream;
    groupCallState.active = true;
    groupCallState.groupId = groupId;
    groupCallState.callId = callId;
    groupCallState.startedAt = Date.now();
    
    // Create participants list - all members ringing except self
    const participants = memberIds.map(uid=>{
      const isSelf = uid===fbUser.uid;
      let name = 'User';
      let photo = '';
      if(isSelf){
        name = fbUserProfile?.displayName||'You';
        photo = fbUserProfile?.photoURL||'';
      } else {
        // Try to get from chats or cache
        const chat = chats.find(c=>c.otherUid===uid);
        name = chat?.name||'Member';
        photo = chat?.photoURL||'';
      }
      return {
        uid,
        name,
        photoURL: photo,
        peerId: '', // will be filled when they join
        status: isSelf ? 'joined' : 'ringing',
        isSelf,
        stream: isSelf ? stream : null,
        analyser: null
      };
    });
    groupCallState.participants = participants;
    
    // Save to Firebase - groupCalls collection
    await fbDb.collection('groupCalls').doc(groupId).set({
      callId,
      groupId,
      groupName: current.name||'Group',
      initiator: fbUser.uid,
      initiatorName: fbUserProfile?.displayName||'Someone',
      initiatorPeerId: window.KP_PEER_ID||localStorage.getItem('kpwa_peer_id')||'',
      startedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      status: 'ringing',
      participants: participants.map(p=>({uid:p.uid, name:p.name, status:p.status, peerId:p.peerId})),
      memberIds: memberIds
    });
    
    // Send system message to group chat about call
    await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(groupId).collection('messages').add({
      senderId: fbUser.uid,
      type: 'system',
      text: `📞 Group call started by ${fbUserProfile?.displayName||'Someone'} - Tap to join`,
      createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      groupCallId: callId,
      groupCallAction: 'started',
      readBy: [fbUser.uid],
      deliveredTo: memberIds
    });
    
    // Show group call UI
    showGroupCallUI();
    startGroupCallTimer();
    startGroupCallSpeakingDetection();
    
    // Listen for participants joining
    listenToGroupCallUpdates(groupId);
    
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast(`Calling ${memberIds.length-1} members... 📞`);
    
  }catch(e){
    console.error('startGroupCall failed', e);
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Could not start group call: '+(e.message||'mic permission?'));
    cleanupGroupCall();
  }
}
window.startGroupCall = startGroupCall;

function showGroupCallUI(){
  let overlay = document.getElementById('groupCallOverlay');
  if(!overlay){
    overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.id = 'groupCallOverlay';
    overlay.className = 'groupCallOverlay';
    overlay.innerHTML = `
      <div class="groupCallHeader">
        <div class="info">
          <b id="groupCallTitle">Group Call</b>
          <small id="groupCallSub">${groupCallState.participants.length} members • Ringing...</small>
        </div>
        <div class="groupCallTimer" id="groupCallTimer">00:00</div>
      </div>
      <div class="groupCallGrid" id="groupCallGrid"></div>
      <div class="groupCallActions">
        <button class="gAction mute" id="groupMuteBtn" onclick="toggleGroupMute()" title="Mute">
          <svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M12 3a3 3 0 0 0-3 3v6a3 3 0 0 0 6 0V6a3 3 0 0 0-3-3Z"/><path d="M19 10v2a7 7 0 0 1-14 0v-2M12 19v3M8 22h8"/></svg>
        </button>
        <button class="gAction add" onclick="if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Add participants coming soon')" title="Add">
          <svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M16 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H6a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2"/><circle cx="9" cy="7" r="4"/><path d="M22 8v6"/><path d="M19 11v6"/><path d="M19 11v-3"/><path d="M25 11h-6"/></svg>
        </button>
        <button class="gAction end" onclick="endGroupCall()" title="End call">
          <svg width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg>
        </button>
      </div>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);
  }
  
  overlay.classList.add('show');
  renderGroupCallGrid();
}

function renderGroupCallGrid(){
  const grid = document.getElementById('groupCallGrid');
  const title = document.getElementById('groupCallTitle');
  const sub = document.getElementById('groupCallSub');
  if(!grid) return;
  
  if(title) title.textContent = current?.name||'Group Call';
  const joined = groupCallState.participants.filter(p=>p.status==='joined').length;
  const total = groupCallState.participants.length;
  if(sub) sub.textContent = `${joined}/${total} joined • ${total-joined} ringing...`;
  
  grid.innerHTML = groupCallState.participants.map(p=>{
    const initial = (p.name||'U')[0].toUpperCase();
    const avatar = p.photoURL ? `<img src="${p.photoURL}" alt="">` : initial;
    const statusClass = p.status==='joined' ? 'joined' : 'ringing';
    const speakingClass = p.speaking ? 'speaking' : '';
    const mutedClass = p.muted ? 'muted' : '';
    const statusText = p.isSelf ? 'You' : (p.status==='joined' ? 'Joined' : 'Ringing...');
    const statusDot = p.status==='joined' ? (p.speaking ? 'speaking' : 'joined') : 'ringing';
    
    return `
      <div class="groupCallParticipant ${statusClass} ${speakingClass} ${mutedClass}" data-uid="${p.uid}">
        <div class="pAvatar">${avatar}</div>
        <div class="pName">${p.name}${p.isSelf?' (You)':''}</div>
        <div class="pStatus"><span class="dot ${statusDot}"></span>${statusText}</div>
        <div class="speakingBars"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>
      </div>
    `;
  }).join('');
}

function startGroupCallTimer(){
  if(groupCallState.timerInt) clearInterval(groupCallState.timerInt);
  groupCallState.timerInt = setInterval(()=>{
    const elapsed = Math.floor((Date.now() - groupCallState.startedAt)/1000);
    const m = String(Math.floor(elapsed/60)).padStart(2,'0');
    const s = String(elapsed%60).padStart(2,'0');
    const el = document.getElementById('groupCallTimer');
    if(el) el.textContent = `${m}:${s}`;
  }, 1000);
}

function stopGroupCallTimer(){
  if(groupCallState.timerInt){
    clearInterval(groupCallState.timerInt);
    groupCallState.timerInt = null;
  }
}

function listenToGroupCallUpdates(groupId){
  if(groupCallState.fbUnsub) groupCallState.fbUnsub();
  try{
    groupCallState.fbUnsub = fbDb.collection('groupCalls').doc(groupId).onSnapshot(snap=>{
      if(!snap.exists){
        // Call ended by initiator
        if(groupCallState.active){
          if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Group call ended');
          endGroupCall(false);
        }
        return;
      }
      const data = snap.data();
      const remoteParticipants = data.participants||[];
      
      // Update participants statuses
      let changed = false;
      remoteParticipants.forEach(rp=>{
        const local = groupCallState.participants.find(p=>p.uid===rp.uid);
        if(local && local.status!==rp.status){
          local.status = rp.status;
          local.peerId = rp.peerId||local.peerId;
          changed = true;
          // If new participant joined and has peerId, call them if we are initiator or already joined
          if(rp.status==='joined' && rp.peerId && rp.uid!==fbUser.uid && !groupCallState.peerCalls.has(rp.uid)){
            if(groupCallState.localStream){
              callGroupParticipant(rp.uid, rp.peerId);
            }
          }
        }
      });
      
      if(changed) renderGroupCallGrid();
    });
  }catch(e){ console.warn('listenToGroupCallUpdates failed', e); }
}

function callGroupParticipant(uid, peerId){
  if(!peer || !peer.open){
    console.warn('Peer not ready for group call');
    return;
  }
  if(!groupCallState.localStream){
    console.warn('No local stream for group call');
    return;
  }
  if(groupCallState.peerCalls.has(uid)){
    return; // already calling
  }
  try{
    console.log('Calling group participant', uid, peerId);
    const call = peer.call(peerId, groupCallState.localStream, {
      metadata: {
        type: 'groupCall',
        groupId: groupCallState.groupId,
        callId: groupCallState.callId,
        uid: fbUser.uid,
        name: fbUserProfile?.displayName||'User',
        photoURL: fbUserProfile?.photoURL||''
      }
    });
    if(call){
      groupCallState.peerCalls.set(uid, call);
      bindGroupCall(call, uid);
    }
  }catch(e){ console.error('callGroupParticipant failed', e); }
}

function bindGroupCall(call, uid){
  call.on('stream', remoteStream=>{
    console.log('Got remote stream for group participant', uid);
    const participant = groupCallState.participants.find(p=>p.uid===uid);
    if(participant){
      participant.stream = remoteStream;
      participant.status = 'joined';
      // Create audio element to play
      let audio = document.getElementById('groupAudio_'+uid);
      if(!audio){
        audio = document.createElement('audio');
        audio.id = 'groupAudio_'+uid;
        audio.autoplay = true;
        audio.playsInline = true;
        document.body.appendChild(audio);
      }
      audio.srcObject = remoteStream;
      audio.play().catch(()=>{});
      
      // Create analyser for speaking detection
      try{
        const ctx = window.kpAudioCtx || new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
        window.kpAudioCtx = ctx;
        if(ctx.state==='suspended') ctx.resume();
        const source = ctx.createMediaStreamSource(remoteStream);
        const analyser = ctx.createAnalyser();
        analyser.fftSize = 256;
        source.connect(analyser);
        participant.analyser = analyser;
      }catch(e){ console.warn('analyser failed for group', e); }
      
      renderGroupCallGrid();
    }
  });
  
  call.on('close', ()=>{
    console.log('Group call closed for', uid);
    groupCallState.peerCalls.delete(uid);
    const p = groupCallState.participants.find(x=>x.uid===uid);
    if(p){
      p.status = 'ringing';
      p.stream = null;
      const audio = document.getElementById('groupAudio_'+uid);
      if(audio){ audio.srcObject=null; audio.remove(); }
      renderGroupCallGrid();
    }
  });
  
  call.on('error', err=>{
    console.warn('Group call error for', uid, err);
    groupCallState.peerCalls.delete(uid);
  });
}

// Handle incoming group calls for all members
function handleIncomingGroupCall(call){
  const meta = call.metadata||{};
  if(meta.type!=='groupCall') return false; // not group call, handle as 1-1
  
  const groupId = meta.groupId;
  const callId = meta.callId;
  
  // Show incoming group call banner if not already in call
  if(groupCallState.active) {
    try{ call.close(); }catch(e){}
    return true;
  }
  
  // Find group name
  const groupChat = chats.find(c=>c.firestoreId===groupId);
  const groupName = groupChat?.name || meta.groupName || 'Group';
  
  showIncomingGroupCallBanner(groupId, callId, groupName, meta.name||'Someone', call);
  return true;
}

function showIncomingGroupCallBanner(groupId, callId, groupName, callerName, callObj){
  let banner = document.getElementById('incomingGroupCallBanner');
  if(!banner){
    banner = document.createElement('div');
    banner.id = 'incomingGroupCallBanner';
    banner.className = 'incomingGroupCall';
    document.body.appendChild(banner);
  }
  
  banner.innerHTML = `
    <div class="icon">👥</div>
    <div class="info">
      <b>${groupName} - Group Call</b>
      <small>${callerName} started a group call • Tap to join</small>
    </div>
    <div class="actions">
      <button class="reject" onclick="rejectIncomingGroupCall()"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="m6 6 12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button>
      <button class="accept" onclick="acceptIncomingGroupCall('${groupId}', '${callId}')"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M6 4.5 9 4l2 4-2 2a13 13 0 0 0 5 5l2-2 4 2-.5 3a2 2 0 0 1-2.2 1.5C9.5 18.2 5.8 14.5 4.5 6.7A2 2 0 0 1 6 4.5Z"/></svg></button>
    </div>
  `;
  banner.classList.add('show');
  
  // Store call for later
  window._incomingGroupCall = {groupId, callId, groupName, callerName, call: callObj};
  
  // Auto hide after 30s
  setTimeout(()=>{
    if(banner.classList.contains('show')){
      banner.classList.remove('show');
    }
  }, 30000);
  
  // Play ringtone
  if(typeof playRingtone==='function') playRingtone();
  
  // Notification
  if(typeof kpShowNotification==='function' && document.hidden){
    kpShowNotification(`Group Call - ${groupName}`, `${callerName} started a group call`);
  }
}
window.showIncomingGroupCallBanner = showIncomingGroupCallBanner;

async function acceptIncomingGroupCall(groupId, callId){
  const data = window._incomingGroupCall;
  if(!data) return;
  
  const banner = document.getElementById('incomingGroupCallBanner');
  if(banner) banner.classList.remove('show');
  if(typeof stopRingtone==='function') stopRingtone();
  
  try{
    const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({audio:true, video:false});
    groupCallState.localStream = stream;
    groupCallState.active = true;
    groupCallState.groupId = data.groupId;
    groupCallState.callId = data.callId;
    groupCallState.startedAt = Date.now();
    
    // Get group members
    let memberIds = [];
    let groupName = data.groupName;
    try{
      const snap = await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(groupId).get();
      if(snap.exists){
        const gData = snap.data();
        memberIds = gData.memberIds||[];
        groupName = gData.name||groupName;
        current = chats.find(c=>c.firestoreId===groupId) || current;
      }
    }catch(e){}
    
    // Build participants
    groupCallState.participants = memberIds.map(uid=>{
      const isSelf = uid===fbUser.uid;
      const chat = chats.find(c=>c.otherUid===uid);
      return {
        uid,
        name: isSelf ? (fbUserProfile?.displayName||'You') : (chat?.name||'Member'),
        photoURL: isSelf ? (fbUserProfile?.photoURL||'') : (chat?.photoURL||''),
        peerId: '',
        status: isSelf ? 'joined' : 'ringing',
        isSelf,
        stream: isSelf ? stream : null,
        analyser: null
      };
    });
    
    // Update Firebase that we joined
    await fbDb.collection('groupCalls').doc(groupId).update({
      participants: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.arrayUnion({
        uid: fbUser.uid,
        name: fbUserProfile?.displayName||'User',
        status: 'joined',
        peerId: window.KP_PEER_ID||localStorage.getItem('kpwa_peer_id')||'',
        joinedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
      })
    }).catch(async ()=>{
      // If arrayUnion doesn't work with object, do manual merge
      const doc = await fbDb.collection('groupCalls').doc(groupId).get();
      if(doc.exists){
        const existing = doc.data().participants||[];
        const filtered = existing.filter(p=>p.uid!==fbUser.uid);
        filtered.push({
          uid: fbUser.uid,
          name: fbUserProfile?.displayName||'User',
          status: 'joined',
          peerId: window.KP_PEER_ID||localStorage.getItem('kpwa_peer_id')||'',
          joinedAt: new Date()
        });
        await fbDb.collection('groupCalls').doc(groupId).update({participants: filtered});
      }
    });
    
    // Answer the incoming call if exists
    if(data.call){
      try{
        data.call.answer(stream);
        bindGroupCall(data.call, data.call.metadata?.uid||'initiator');
      }catch(e){ console.warn('Answer group call failed', e); }
    }
    
    // Show UI
    showGroupCallUI();
    startGroupCallTimer();
    startGroupCallSpeakingDetection();
    listenToGroupCallUpdates(groupId);
    
    // Also call other participants who are already joined (we need their peerIds)
    // This will be handled by listenToGroupCallUpdates when we get updated participant list
    
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Joined group call ✅');
    
  }catch(e){
    console.error('acceptIncomingGroupCall failed', e);
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Could not join: '+(e.message||'mic permission?'));
    cleanupGroupCall();
  }
}
window.acceptIncomingGroupCall = acceptIncomingGroupCall;

function rejectIncomingGroupCall(){
  const banner = document.getElementById('incomingGroupCallBanner');
  if(banner) banner.classList.remove('show');
  if(typeof stopRingtone==='function') stopRingtone();
  if(window._incomingGroupCall?.call){
    try{ window._incomingGroupCall.call.close(); }catch(e){}
  }
  window._incomingGroupCall = null;
  if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Group call declined');
}
window.rejectIncomingGroupCall = rejectIncomingGroupCall;

function toggleGroupMute(){
  if(!groupCallState.localStream) return;
  const track = groupCallState.localStream.getAudioTracks()[0];
  if(!track) return;
  track.enabled = !track.enabled;
  const btn = document.getElementById('groupMuteBtn');
  if(btn) btn.classList.toggle('muted', !track.enabled);
  const selfParticipant = groupCallState.participants.find(p=>p.isSelf);
  if(selfParticipant) selfParticipant.muted = !track.enabled;
  renderGroupCallGrid();
  if(typeof toast==='function') toast(track.enabled?'Mic on':'Mic muted');
}
window.toggleGroupMute = toggleGroupMute;

function startGroupCallSpeakingDetection(){
  if(window._groupSpeakingInterval) clearInterval(window._groupSpeakingInterval);
  window._groupSpeakingInterval = setInterval(()=>{
    groupCallState.participants.forEach(p=>{
      if(p.analyser){
        const data = new Uint8Array(p.analyser.frequencyBinCount);
        p.analyser.getByteFrequencyData(data);
        let sum=0;
        for(let i=4;i<data.length*0.6;i++) sum+=data[i];
        const vol = sum/(data.length*0.6);
        const wasSpeaking = p.speaking;
        p.speaking = vol>14;
        if(wasSpeaking!==p.speaking){
          renderGroupCallGrid();
        }
      } else if(p.isSelf && groupCallState.localStream){
        // Create analyser for self if not exists
        try{
          const ctx = window.kpAudioCtx || new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
          window.kpAudioCtx = ctx;
          if(ctx.state==='suspended') ctx.resume();
          const source = ctx.createMediaStreamSource(groupCallState.localStream);
          const analyser = ctx.createAnalyser();
          analyser.fftSize = 256;
          source.connect(analyser);
          p.analyser = analyser;
        }catch(e){}
      }
    });
  }, 120);
}

function stopGroupCallSpeakingDetection(){
  if(window._groupSpeakingInterval){
    clearInterval(window._groupSpeakingInterval);
    window._groupSpeakingInterval = null;
  }
}

async function endGroupCall(shouldDeleteDoc=true){
  stopGroupCallTimer();
  stopGroupCallSpeakingDetection();
  
  // Stop all peer calls
  for(const [uid, call] of groupCallState.peerCalls){
    try{ call.close(); }catch(e){}
    const audio = document.getElementById('groupAudio_'+uid);
    if(audio){ audio.srcObject=null; audio.remove(); }
  }
  groupCallState.peerCalls.clear();
  
  // Stop local stream
  if(groupCallState.localStream){
    groupCallState.localStream.getTracks().forEach(t=>t.stop());
    groupCallState.localStream = null;
  }
  
  // Unsubscribe
  if(groupCallState.fbUnsub){
    groupCallState.fbUnsub();
    groupCallState.fbUnsub = null;
  }
  
  // Delete Firebase doc if initiator
  if(shouldDeleteDoc && groupCallState.groupId){
    try{
      const doc = await fbDb.collection('groupCalls').doc(groupCallState.groupId).get();
      if(doc.exists && doc.data().initiator===fbUser.uid){
        await fbDb.collection('groupCalls').doc(groupCallState.groupId).delete();
      } else {
        // Just mark self as left
        const snap = await fbDb.collection('groupCalls').doc(groupCallState.groupId).get();
        if(snap.exists){
          const parts = (snap.data().participants||[]).filter(p=>p.uid!==fbUser.uid);
          await fbDb.collection('groupCalls').doc(groupCallState.groupId).update({participants: parts});
        }
      }
    }catch(e){ console.warn('Failed to clean group call doc', e); }
  }
  
  // Hide UI
  const overlay = document.getElementById('groupCallOverlay');
  if(overlay) overlay.classList.remove('show');
  
  // Reset state
  groupCallState.active = false;
  groupCallState.groupId = null;
  groupCallState.callId = null;
  groupCallState.participants = [];
  groupCallState.startedAt = null;
  
  if(typeof toast==='function' && shouldDeleteDoc) toast('Group call ended');
}
window.endGroupCall = endGroupCall;

function cleanupGroupCall(){
  endGroupCall(false);
}

// Override peer on call to handle both 1-1 and group
(function(){
  const origInitPeer = window.initPeer;
  window.initPeer = function(){
    if(origInitPeer) origInitPeer();
    // Wait for peer to be ready
    const check = setInterval(()=>{
      if(typeof peer!=='undefined' && peer){
        clearInterval(check);
        peer.off('call'); // remove old listeners
        peer.on('call', (call)=>{
          const meta = call.metadata||{};
          if(meta.type==='groupCall'){
            handleIncomingGroupCall(call);
          } else {
            // 1-1 call
            if(typeof handleIncomingCall==='function') handleIncomingCall(call);
          }
        });
      }
    }, 500);
  };
  
  // Also listen to Firebase groupCalls for ringing
  function listenForIncomingGroupCalls(){
    if(!fbUser || !fbDb) return;
    try{
      fbDb.collection('groupCalls').where('memberIds','array-contains', fbUser.uid).onSnapshot(snap=>{
        snap.docChanges().forEach(change=>{
          if(change.type==='added' || change.type==='modified'){
            const data = change.doc.data();
            if(data.initiator===fbUser.uid) return; // don't show for initiator
            if(data.status==='ringing' && !groupCallState.active){
              // Check if we are already in participants and not joined
              const isParticipant = (data.memberIds||[]).includes(fbUser.uid);
              if(isParticipant){
                const alreadyJoined = (data.participants||[]).some(p=>p.uid===fbUser.uid && p.status==='joined');
                if(!alreadyJoined){
                  showIncomingGroupCallBanner(data.groupId, data.callId, data.groupName||'Group', data.initiatorName||'Someone', null);
                }
              }
            }
          } else if(change.type==='removed'){
            // Call ended
            const banner = document.getElementById('incomingGroupCallBanner');
            if(banner) banner.classList.remove('show');
            if(groupCallState.active && groupCallState.groupId===change.doc.id){
              if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Group call ended by initiator');
              endGroupCall(false);
            }
          }
        });
      });
    }catch(e){ console.warn('listenForIncomingGroupCalls failed', e); }
  }
  
  // Start listening after auth
  setTimeout(listenForIncomingGroupCalls, 3000);
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', ()=>{ setTimeout(listenForIncomingGroupCalls, 4000); });
  
  // Re-listen on auth state change
  if(typeof fbAuth!=='undefined' && fbAuth){
    fbAuth.onAuthStateChanged(user=>{
      if(user) setTimeout(listenForIncomingGroupCalls, 2000);
    });
  }
})();

// Add group call button to group chat header
function addGroupCallButtonToHeader(){
  const headBtns = document.querySelector('.headBtns, .chatHead .headBtns');
  const chatHead = document.querySelector('.chatHead');
  if(!chatHead) return;
  
  // Check if current is group and button not exists
  if(current?.group && !document.getElementById('groupCallHeaderBtn')){
    let btnsContainer = chatHead.querySelector('.headBtns');
    if(!btnsContainer){
      btnsContainer = document.createElement('div');
      btnsContainer.className = 'headBtns';
      btnsContainer.style.display='flex';
      btnsContainer.style.gap='8px';
      chatHead.appendChild(btnsContainer);
    }
    
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.id = 'groupCallHeaderBtn';
    btn.className = 'groupCallBtn';
    btn.title = 'Group Call';
    btn.innerHTML = `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M16 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H6a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2"/><circle cx="9" cy="7" r="4"/><path d="M22 8v6"/><path d="M19 11h6"/><path d="M15 8a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v2a2 2 0 0 1-2 2"/><circle cx="17" cy="7" r="2"/></svg>`;
    btn.onclick = startGroupCall;
    btnsContainer.insertBefore(btn, btnsContainer.firstChild);
  }
}

// Call when chat opens
const _origOpenChatForGroupCall = window.openChat;
window.openChat = async function(id){
  if(_origOpenChatForGroupCall) await _origOpenChatForGroupCall(id);
  setTimeout(addGroupCallButtonToHeader, 500);
};

// Also add to group details page
const _origOpenGroupDetailsForCall = window.openGroupDetails;
window.openGroupDetails = async function(){
  if(_origOpenGroupDetailsForCall) {
    const result = _origOpenGroupDetailsForCall.apply(this, arguments);
    if(result instanceof Promise) await result;
  }
  setTimeout(()=>{
    const pageInner = document.getElementById('pageInner');
    if(pageInner && !document.getElementById('groupCallDetailsBtn')){
      const hero = pageInner.querySelector('.groupDetailHero, .pageCard');
      if(hero){
        const callBtn = document.createElement('button');
        callBtn.id = 'groupCallDetailsBtn';
        callBtn.className = 'primary';
        callBtn.style.marginTop='12px';
        callBtn.style.width='100%';
        callBtn.innerHTML = '📞 Start Group Call';
        callBtn.onclick = ()=>{ closePage(); setTimeout(startGroupCall, 300); };
        hero.appendChild(callBtn);
      }
    }
  }, 600);
};

console.log('Group Calling feature loaded - Start group call from group chat header');
</script>

  width:100% !important;
  height:100% !important;

<script id="perfect-match-js">
console.log('Perfect match fixes loading...');

// ===== 1. DP PERFECT ROUND - enforce everywhere =====
function enforceDPAlignment(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.avatar, .miniAvatar, .profileAvatarBig, .groupPickAvatar, .groupDetailAvatar, .pAvatar, .callAvatar').forEach(el=>{
    el.style.borderRadius = '50%';
    el.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    el.style.aspectRatio = '1/1';
    const img = el.querySelector('img');
    if(img){
      img.style.width='100%';
      img.style.height='100%';
      img.style.objectFit='cover';
      img.style.objectPosition='center';
      img.style.borderRadius='50%';
    }
  });
}
setInterval(enforceDPAlignment, 2000);
setTimeout(enforceDPAlignment, 500);

// ===== 2. MESSAGE BUBBLE - WHATSAPP EXACT =====
const _origRenderMessagesPerfect = window.renderMessages;
window.renderMessages = function(){
  if(_origRenderMessagesPerfect) _origRenderMessagesPerfect();
  // After render, fix meta alignment for short messages like "Hi"
  document.querySelectorAll('.msg.me, .msg.in').forEach(el=>{
    const txt = el.querySelector('.txt');
    const meta = el.querySelector('.meta');
    if(txt && meta){
      // If text is short (less than 30 chars), keep meta inline
      const textLen = txt.textContent.trim().length;
      if(textLen < 25){
        el.style.display = 'flex';
        el.style.flexWrap = 'wrap';
        el.style.alignItems = 'flex-end';
        el.style.gap = '4px';
        txt.style.display = 'inline';
        meta.style.display = 'inline-flex';
        meta.style.float = 'none';
        meta.style.marginLeft = '6px';
      }
    }
  });
};

// ===== 3. DELETED MESSAGE HANDLING =====
window.deleteMessage = async function(docId){
  if(!docId || !current?.firestoreId) return;
  if(!confirm('Delete this message?')) return;
  try{
    await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId).collection('messages').doc(docId).update({
      deleted: true,
      text: 'You deleted this message',
      type: 'deleted',
      deletedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
    });
    // Update local
    const msg = current.messages.find(m=>m[5]?.id===docId);
    if(msg){
      msg[0]='deleted';
      msg[1]='You deleted this message';
    }
    renderMessages();
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Message deleted');
  }catch(e){ console.error(e); }
};

// Override select to show delete for single
window.selectMessage = function(docId, idx){
  if(!docId) return;
  window.selectedMessageIds = window.selectedMessageIds||new Set();
  if(window.selectedMessageIds.has(docId)){
    window.selectedMessageIds.delete(docId);
  } else {
    window.selectedMessageIds.add(docId);
  }
  const bar = document.querySelector('.messageSelectBar');
  if(bar){
    if(window.selectedMessageIds.size>0){
      bar.classList.add('show');
      const countEl = bar.querySelector('.count');
      if(countEl) countEl.textContent = window.selectedMessageIds.size + ' selected';
      // If single, show delete icon directly on bubble
      if(window.selectedMessageIds.size===1){
        const el = document.querySelector(`.msg[data-message-id="${docId}"]`);
        if(el && !el.querySelector('.quickDelete')){
          const btn = document.createElement('button');
          btn.className='quickDelete';
          btn.innerHTML='🗑️';
          btn.style.cssText='position:absolute; top:-8px; right:-8px; width:28px; height:28px; border-radius:50%; background:#f15c6d; border:none; color:white; display:grid; place-items:center; cursor:pointer; z-index:5; box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.3)';
          btn.onclick = (e)=>{ e.stopPropagation(); window.deleteSelectedMessages(); };
          el.style.position='relative';
          el.appendChild(btn);
        }
      }
    } else {
      bar.classList.remove('show');
      document.querySelectorAll('.quickDelete').forEach(b=>b.remove());
    }
  }
  document.querySelectorAll('.msg[data-message-id]').forEach(el=>{
    const id = el.dataset.messageId;
    if(window.selectedMessageIds.has(id)){
      el.classList.add('msgLongSelected');
    } else {
      el.classList.remove('msgLongSelected');
      const qd = el.querySelector('.quickDelete');
      if(qd) qd.remove();
    }
  });
};

// ===== 4. CALL LOG BUBBLE - Image 3 =====
function createCallLogBubble(type, duration, time){
  const isVideo = type.includes('video');
  const icon = isVideo 
    ? `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M17 10.5V7c0-.55-.45-1-1-1H4c-.55 0-1 .45-1 1v10c0 .55.45 1 1 1h12c.55 0 1-.45 1-1v-3.5l4 4v-11l-4 4z"/></svg>`
    : `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M20.01 15.38c-1.23 0-2.42-.2-3.53-.56-.35-.12-.74-.03-1.01.24l-1.57 1.97c-2.83-1.35-5.48-3.9-6.89-6.83l1.95-1.66c.27-.28.35-.67.24-1.02-.37-1.11-.56-2.3-.56-3.53 0-.54-.45-.99-.99-.99H4.19C3.65 3 3 3.24 3 3.99 3 13.28 10.73 21 20.01 21c.71 0 .99-.63.99-1.18v-3.45c0-.54-.45-.99-.99-.99z"/></svg>`;
  
  const title = isVideo ? 'Video call' : 'Voice call';
  const sub = duration ? `${duration}` : (type.includes('missed') ? 'Missed' : 'Outgoing');
  
  return `
    <div class="callLogBubble">
      <div class="callIcon ${type.includes('missed')?'missed':''}">${icon}</div>
      <div class="callInfo">
        <b>${title}</b>
        <small>${sub}</small>
      </div>
      <div class="callTime">${time||new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})}</div>
    </div>
  `;
}

window.logCallInChat = async function(type, duration){
  if(!current?.firestoreId || !fbUser) return;
  const time = new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
  const bubble = createCallLogBubble(type, duration, time);
  try{
    await fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId).collection('messages').add({
      senderId: fbUser.uid,
      type: 'call',
      callType: type,
      duration: duration||'',
      text: bubble,
      createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
      readBy: [fbUser.uid],
      deliveredTo: current.memberIds||[]
    });
  }catch(e){ console.warn('logCallInChat failed', e); }
};

// Hook into endVoiceCall to log
const _origEndVoiceCallPerfect = window.endVoiceCall;
window.endVoiceCall = function(sendClose=true){
  const start = window.callStartedAt||0;
  const duration = start ? Math.floor((Date.now()-start)/1000) : 0;
  const durText = duration>0 ? `${Math.floor(duration/60)}:${String(duration%60).padStart(2,'0')}` : 'Missed';
  const type = duration>0 ? 'voice' : 'missed_voice';
  if(duration>0 && current){
    setTimeout(()=>{ window.logCallInChat(type, durText); }, 500);
  }
  if(_origEndVoiceCallPerfect) return _origEndVoiceCallPerfect(sendClose);
};

const _origEndGroupCallPerfect = window.endGroupCall;
window.endGroupCall = function(shouldDeleteDoc=true){
  const start = window.groupCallState?.startedAt||0;
  const duration = start ? Math.floor((Date.now()-start)/1000) : 0;
  const durText = duration>0 ? `${Math.floor(duration/60)} min, ${duration%60} sec` : '';
  if(duration>0 && current){
    setTimeout(()=>{ window.logCallInChat('group_video', durText); }, 500);
  }
  if(_origEndGroupCallPerfect) return _origEndGroupCallPerfect(shouldDeleteDoc);
};

// ===== 5. ADD PEOPLE PAGE - Image 1 =====
window.openAddPeoplePage = function(forGroupCall=false){
  const pv = document.getElementById('pageView');
  const pi = document.getElementById('pageInner');
  if(!pv||!pi) return;
  
  pv.style.display='';
  pv.classList.add('show');
  
  // Get contacts - from realUsers or chats
  const contacts = (window.realUsers||[]).slice(0,50).map(u=>({
    name: u.displayName||u.username||'User',
    status: u.bio||'Available',
    photo: u.photoURL||'',
    uid: u.uid,
    initial: (u.displayName||u.username||'U')[0].toUpperCase()
  }));
  
  // Add frequently contacted from chats
  const freq = chats.slice(0,6).filter(c=>!c.group).map(c=>({
    name: c.name,
    status: c.about||'Available',
    photo: c.photoURL||'',
    uid: c.otherUid,
    initial: c.initial
  }));
  
  const other = contacts.filter(c=>!freq.some(f=>f.uid===c.uid));
  
  pi.innerHTML = `
    <div class="addPeoplePage">
      <div class="addPeopleHeader">
        <button class="backBtn" onclick="closePage()"><svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="m15 18-6-6 6-6"/></svg></button>
        <div class="title"><b>Add people</b><small>${freq.length + other.length} contacts</small></div>
        <button class="searchBtn" onclick="if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Search')"><svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"/><path d="m21 21-4.3-4.3"/></svg></button>
      </div>
      
      <div class="addPeopleSection">
        <div class="sectionTitle">Frequently contacted</div>
        ${freq.map(c=>`
          <div class="addPeopleRow" onclick="addPeopleToCall('${c.uid}', '${c.name.replace(/'/g,"\'")}')">
            <div class="apAvatar">${c.photo ? `<img src="${c.photo}" alt="">` : c.initial}</div>
            <div class="apInfo"><b>${c.name}</b><small>${c.status}</small></div>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>
      
      <div class="addPeopleSection">
        <div class="sectionTitle">Other contacts</div>
        ${other.map(c=>`
          <div class="addPeopleRow" onclick="addPeopleToCall('${c.uid}', '${c.name.replace(/'/g,"\'")}')">
            <div class="apAvatar">${c.photo ? `<img src="${c.photo}" alt="">` : c.initial}</div>
            <div class="apInfo"><b>${c.name}</b><small>${c.status}</small></div>
          </div>
        `).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
};

window.addPeopleToCall = async function(uid, name){
  if(!uid){
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('User not available');
    return;
  }
  
  // If in group call, add to group call
  if(window.groupCallState?.active){
    // Add to participants
    const exists = groupCallState.participants.some(p=>p.uid===uid);
    if(exists){
      if(typeof toast==='function') toast(name+' already in call');
      return;
    }
    
    // Get peerId for this user if available
    let peerId = '';
    try{
      const userDoc = await fbDb.collection('users').doc(uid).get();
      if(userDoc.exists){
        const data = userDoc.data();
        peerId = data.peerId||data.lastPeerId||'';
      }
    }catch(e){}
    
    // Add to Firebase group call
    try{
      const groupId = groupCallState.groupId;
      const doc = await fbDb.collection('groupCalls').doc(groupId).get();
      if(doc.exists){
        const existing = doc.data().participants||[];
        existing.push({uid, name, status:'ringing', peerId});
        await fbDb.collection('groupCalls').doc(groupId).update({participants: existing});
        
        // Also try to call directly if peerId known
        if(peerId && groupCallState.localStream){
          callGroupParticipant(uid, peerId);
        }
        
        if(typeof toast==='function') toast(`Calling ${name}...`);
        closePage();
      }
    }catch(e){
      console.error('addPeopleToCall failed', e);
      if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Could not add '+name);
    }
  } else if(current?.group){
    // If in group chat but not in call, start group call with this person included
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast(`Adding ${name} to group call...`);
    closePage();
    setTimeout(()=>{ startGroupCall(); }, 300);
  } else {
    // 1-1 call - start call with this person
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast(`Calling ${name}...`);
    // Find chat for this uid and start call
    const chat = chats.find(c=>c.otherUid===uid);
    if(chat){
      current = chat;
      if(typeof openChat==='function') await openChat(chat.id);
      setTimeout(()=>{ if(typeof startVoiceCall==='function') startVoiceCall(); }, 500);
    }
    closePage();
  }
};

// Override group call add button to open add people page
document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
  const addBtn = e.target.closest('.callAddPeopleTop, .groupCallActions .gAction.add');
  if(addBtn && (window.groupCallState?.active || document.getElementById('groupCallOverlay')?.classList.contains('show'))){
    e.preventDefault();
    window.openAddPeoplePage(true);
  }
});

// Add plus button to calling screen (Image 2 top right)
function addPlusButtonToCallScreen(){
  const callCard = document.querySelector('.callCard.upgraded, .groupCallOverlay .groupCallHeader');
  if(!callCard) return;
  
  if(!document.querySelector('.callAddPeopleTop') && document.getElementById('callOverlay')?.classList.contains('show')){
    const btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.className='callAddPeopleTop';
    btn.innerHTML='<svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M16 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H6a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2"/><circle cx="9" cy="7" r="4"/><line x1="19" y1="8" x2="19" y2="14"/><line x1="22" y1="11" x2="16" y2="11"/></svg>';
    btn.onclick = ()=>{ window.openAddPeoplePage(true); };
    btn.title='Add people';
    document.getElementById('callOverlay')?.appendChild(btn);
  }
}
setInterval(addPlusButtonToCallScreen, 2000);

window.openGMapPage = function(){
  const pv = document.getElementById('pageView');
  const pi = document.getElementById('pageInner');
  if(!pv||!pi) return;
  
  pv.style.display='';
  pv.classList.add('show');
  
  pi.innerHTML = `
    <div class="gmapPage">
      <div class="gmapHeader">
        <button class="backBtn" onclick="closePage()" style="width:40px; height:40px; border-radius:50%; background:transparent; border:none; color:#e9edef; display:grid; place-items:center; cursor:pointer"><svg width="22" height="22" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="m15 18-6-6 6-6"/></svg></button>
        <div class="searchBox">
          <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"/><path d="m21 21-4.3-4.3"/></svg>
          <input id="gmapSearch" placeholder="Search G Map" onkeypress="if(event.key==='Enter'){ searchGMap(this.value); }">
        </div>
        <button onclick="if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Voice search')" style="width:40px; height:40px; border-radius:50%; background:transparent; border:none; color:#00a884; display:grid; place-items:center; cursor:pointer"><svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><path d="M12 1a3 3 0 0 0-3 3v8a3 3 0 0 0 6 0V4a3 3 0 0 0-3-3z"/><path d="M19 10v2a7 7 0 0 1-14 0v-2"/><line x1="12" y1="19" x2="12" y2="23"/><line x1="8" y1="23" x2="16" y2="23"/></svg></button>
      </div>
      
      <div class="gmapContainer">
        <div id="gmap" style="width:100%; height:100%; background:#0b141a; display:grid; place-items:center; color:#8696a0;">
          <div style="text-align:center; padding:20px">
            <div style="width:80px; height:80px; border-radius:50%; background:#202c33; display:grid; place-items:center; margin:0 auto 16px; font-size:32px">🗺️</div>
            <b style="color:#e9edef; display:block; font-size:16px">G Map</b>
            <small style="display:block; margin-top:4px">Share your live location, explore nearby</small>
            <button onclick="getCurrentLocationForGMap()" style="margin-top:16px; padding:10px 20px; background:#00a884; color:white; border:none; border-radius:20px; font-weight:500; cursor:pointer">📍 Use my location</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        <div class="gmapControls">
          <button onclick="if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Layers')" title="Layers"><svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><polygon points="12 2 2 7 12 12 22 7 12 2"/><polyline points="2 17 12 22 22 17"/><polyline points="2 12 12 17 22 12"/></svg></button>
          <button onclick="getCurrentLocationForGMap()" title="My location"><svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/></svg></button>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div class="gmapShareBar">
        <button class="settingsBtn" onclick="openGMapSettings()" title="Settings"><svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/><path d="M12 1v4M12 19v4M4.22 4.22l2.86 2.86M18.36 18.36l2.86 2.86M1 12h4M19 12h4M4.22 19.78l2.86-2.86M18.36 5.64l2.86-2.86"/></svg></button>
        <button class="shareBtn" onclick="shareLocationInChat()">📍 Share live location</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;
  
  // Try to load actual map with Leaflet if available, else keep placeholder
  setTimeout(()=>{
    const mapEl = document.getElementById('gmap');
    if(mapEl && typeof L==='undefined'){
      // Load Leaflet dynamically
      const link = document.createElement('link');
      link.rel = 'stylesheet';
      link.href = 'https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.css';
      document.head.appendChild(link);
      
      const script = document.createElement('script');
      script.src = 'https://unpkg.com/leaflet@1.9.4/dist/leaflet.js';
      script.onload = ()=>{
        initGMapLeaflet();
      };
      document.head.appendChild(script);
    } else if(typeof L!=='undefined'){
      initGMapLeaflet();
    }
  }, 500);
};

function initGMapLeaflet(){
  const mapEl = document.getElementById('gmap');
  if(!mapEl || mapEl._leaflet_id) return;
  
  mapEl.innerHTML = '';
  mapEl.style.background = '#0b141a';
  
  try{
    const map = L.map('gmap').setView([10.8505, 76.2711], 13); // Kerala
    L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
      attribution: '© OpenStreetMap'
    }).addTo(map);
    
    // Add marker for current location if available
    if(navigator.geolocation){
      navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(pos=>{
        const lat = pos.coords.latitude;
        const lng = pos.coords.longitude;
        L.marker([lat, lng]).addTo(map).bindPopup('You are here').openPopup();
        map.setView([lat, lng], 15);
        window._gmapCurrentPos = {lat, lng};
      });
    }
    
    window._gmapInstance = map;
  }catch(e){
    console.error('Leaflet init failed', e);
    mapEl.innerHTML = '<div style="padding:20px; text-align:center; color:#8696a0">Map loading failed, check internet<br><button onclick="initGMapLeaflet()" style="margin-top:10px; padding:8px 16px; background:#00a884; color:white; border:none; border-radius:16px">Retry</button></div>';
  }
}
window.initGMapLeaflet = initGMapLeaflet;

window.getCurrentLocationForGMap = function(){
  if(!navigator.geolocation){
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Geolocation not supported');
    return;
  }
  if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Getting location...');
  navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(pos=>{
    const lat = pos.coords.latitude;
    const lng = pos.coords.longitude;
    window._gmapCurrentPos = {lat, lng};
    if(window._gmapInstance){
      window._gmapInstance.setView([lat, lng], 16);
      L.marker([lat, lng]).addTo(window._gmapInstance).bindPopup('Your location').openPopup();
    }
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast(`Location: ${lat.toFixed(4)}, ${lng.toFixed(4)}`);
  }, err=>{
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Location error: '+err.message);
  });
};

window.shareLocationInChat = function(){
  const pos = window._gmapCurrentPos;
  if(!pos){
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Get location first');
    getCurrentLocationForGMap();
    return;
  }
  if(!current?.firestoreId){
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Open a chat first');
    return;
  }
  
  // Share as location message
  fbDb.collection('chats').doc(current.firestoreId).collection('messages').add({
    senderId: fbUser.uid,
    type: 'location',
    text: `📍 Location: ${pos.lat.toFixed(6)}, ${pos.lng.toFixed(6)}`,
    lat: pos.lat,
    lng: pos.lng,
    createdAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
    readBy: [fbUser.uid],
    deliveredTo: current.memberIds||[]
  }).then(()=>{
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Location shared ✅');
    closePage();
    if(typeof renderMessages==='function') renderMessages();
  }).catch(e=>{
    console.error(e);
    if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Share failed');
  });
};

window.openGMapSettings = function(){
  if(typeof toast==='function') toast('G Map Settings: Satellite, Traffic, Offline maps coming soon');
};

window.searchGMap = function(query){
  if(!query) return;
  if(typeof toast==='function') toast(`Searching "${query}" in G Map...`);
  // In real implementation, use Nominatim or Google Places API
  if(window._gmapInstance){
    // Simple search via Nominatim
    fetch(`https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search?format=json&q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=1`)
      .then(r=>r.json())
      .then(data=>{
        if(data[0]){
          const lat = parseFloat(data[0].lat);
          const lon = parseFloat(data[0].lon);
          window._gmapInstance.setView([lat, lon], 15);
          L.marker([lat, lon]).addTo(window._gmapInstance).bindPopup(data[0].display_name).openPopup();
        } else {
          if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Location not found');
        }
      })
      .catch(()=>{ if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Search failed, check internet'); });
  }
};

// Replace KPAI nav with G Map nav
function replaceAINavWithGMap(){
  const nav = document.querySelector('.nav');
  if(!nav) return;
  
  // Find KPAI button
  const kpaiBtn = nav.querySelector('.kpaiNav, [onclick*="openKPAI"], [onclick*="kpaiOpen"]');
  if(kpaiBtn){
    kpaiBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="kpaiIcon" style="background:#00a884">🗺️</span><span>G Map</span>';
    kpaiBtn.onclick = function(){
      document.querySelectorAll('.nav button').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
      this.classList.add('active');
      openGMapPage();
    };
    kpaiBtn.classList.remove('kpaiNav');
    kpaiBtn.classList.add('gmapNav');
  } else {
    // If no KPAI button, add G Map button
    if(!nav.querySelector('.gmapNav')){
      const btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.className='gmapNav';
      btn.innerHTML='<span class="kpaiIcon" style="background:#00a884">🗺️</span><span>G Map</span>';
      btn.onclick = function(){
        document.querySelectorAll('.nav button').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('active'));
        this.classList.add('active');
        openGMapPage();
      };
      nav.appendChild(btn);
    }
  }
}
setTimeout(replaceAINavWithGMap, 1000);
setTimeout(replaceAINavWithGMap, 3000);

console.log('Perfect match fixes loaded - DP round, message bubbles, call logs, add people, G Map');
})();

<script>
/* KPAI secure frontend integration. Gemini API key is NEVER stored in this HTML. */
function kpaiTimeNow(){return new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});}
function openKPAI(){
  const pv=document.getElementById('pageView'),pi=document.getElementById('pageInner');
  if(!pv||!pi)return;
  pv.classList.add('show');
  pi.innerHTML=`<div class="kpaiShell">
    <div class="kpaiTop">
      <button class="kpaiBack" onclick="closePage()" aria-label="Back"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m15 5-7 7 7 7"/></svg></button>
      <div class="kpaiOrb2"></div>
      <div class="kpaiHeadText"><b>KPAI</b><small><span class="kpaiOnlineDot"></span>AI assistant</small></div>
      <button class="kpaiClear" onclick="clearKPAIChat()" title="Clear chat"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 7h16M9 7V4h6v3m-8 0 1 13h8l1-13M10 11v6M14 11v6"/></svg></button>
    </div>
    <div class="kpaiWelcome"><div class="kpaiWelcomeOrb"></div><h2>How can KPAI help?</h2><p>Ask about KP WhatsApp, Firebase, groups, calls, profiles, status, GIFs, or your other KP projects.</p></div>
    <div class="kpaiSuggestions"><button class="kpaiSuggestion" onclick="kpaiPrompt('How do I create a group?')">Create a group</button><button class="kpaiSuggestion" onclick="kpaiPrompt('How do voice calls work?')">Voice calls</button><button class="kpaiSuggestion" onclick="kpaiPrompt('How does my profile photo sync?')">Profile & DP</button><button class="kpaiSuggestion" onclick="kpaiPrompt('How does Firebase work here?')">Firebase</button></div>
    <div class="kpaiBody2" id="kpaiBody"></div>
    <div class="kpaiTyping2" id="kpaiTyping">KPAI is thinking <span class="kpaiTypingDots"><i></i><i></i><i></i></span></div>
    <div class="kpaiComposer"><div class="kpaiInputWrap"><input id="kpaiInput" autocomplete="off" placeholder="Message KPAI" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'&&!event.shiftKey){event.preventDefault();sendKPAI()}"></div><button class="kpaiSend" onclick="sendKPAI()" aria-label="Send"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="m4 5 16 7-16 7 3-7zM7 12h13"/></svg></button></div>
  </div>`;
  const saved=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('kpai_ui_chat')||'[]');
  const body=document.getElementById('kpaiBody');
  if(saved.length) saved.forEach(m=>kpaiAppend(m.text,m.me)); else kpaiAppend('Hi! I’m KPAI. What would you like to know?',false);
}
function kpaiAppend(text,me){const body=document.getElementById('kpaiBody');if(!body)return;const d=document.createElement('div');d.className='kpaiBubble'+(me?' me':'');d.innerHTML='<button class="kpaiShare" onclick="shareKPAIMessage(this)" aria-label="Share message" title="Share"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="18" cy="5" r="2.5"/><circle cx="6" cy="12" r="2.5"/><circle cx="18" cy="19" r="2.5"/><path d="m8.2 10.8 7.5-4.3M8.2 13.2l7.5 4.3"/></svg></button><span class="kpaiText">'+escapeHtml(text)+'</span><span class="kpaiMeta">'+(me?'You':'KPAI')+' · '+kpaiTimeNow()+'</span>';d.dataset.shareText=text;body.appendChild(d);body.scrollTop=body.scrollHeight;}
async function shareKPAIMessage(btn){
  const bubble=btn?.closest('.kpaiBubble');
  const text=bubble?.dataset?.shareText||bubble?.querySelector('.kpaiText')?.textContent||'';
  if(!text)return;
  try{
    if(navigator.share){
      await navigator.share({title:'KPAI message',text});
    }else if(navigator.clipboard){
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Message copied to clipboard');
    }else{
      const ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.value=text;document.body.appendChild(ta);ta.select();document.execCommand('copy');ta.remove();
      if(typeof toast==='function') toast('Message copied to clipboard');
    }
  }catch(e){ if(e?.name!=='AbortError' && typeof toast==='function') toast('Share cancelled'); }
}
function kpaiPrompt(text){const i=document.getElementById('kpaiInput');if(i){i.value=text;i.focus();sendKPAI();}}
function clearKPAIChat(){sessionStorage.removeItem('kpai_ui_chat');openKPAI();}

// Local fallback replies when Cloud Function is not available
function kpaiLocalReply(t){
  const q=t.toLowerCase();
  if(q.includes('group')) return 'To create a group: Tap + → Create Group, choose a photo, add members, then create. Admins can add/remove members from Group Details.';
  if(q.includes('call') || q.includes('voice')) return 'Voice calls use PeerJS. Make sure you are online, then tap the call button in chat header. Both users must be online for call to connect.';
  if(q.includes('profile') || q.includes('dp') || q.includes('photo')) return 'Your profile photo syncs via Firebase. Go to Profile tab → Edit → Upload photo. It will update in all chats via real-time listeners.';
  if(q.includes('status')) return 'Status lets you post text or media for 24h. Tap Status tab → Post. Others can view and reply.';
  if(q.includes('firebase')) return 'KP WhatsApp uses Firebase Auth for login, Firestore for users/chats/messages, Realtime DB for presence/calls, and Cloud Functions for KPAI.';
  if(q.includes('username')) return 'Username is unique. You sign in only with username + password, no phone number needed.';
  if(q.includes('hi') || q.includes('hello') || q.includes('hey')) return 'Hi! I am KPAI. I can help with groups, calls, profile sync, status, Firebase setup, and your other KP projects. What do you want to know?';
  return 'I can help with KP WhatsApp features - groups, voice calls, profile/DP sync, status, Firebase, GIFs, and your KP game projects. Try asking: How do I create a group?';
}

async function sendKPAI(){
  const input=document.getElementById('kpaiInput'),typing=document.getElementById('kpaiTyping'),body=document.getElementById('kpaiBody');
  if(!input) return;
  const t=input.value.trim();
  if(!t) return;
  let saved=[];
  try{ saved=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('kpai_ui_chat')||'[]'); }catch(e){ saved=[]; }
  saved.push({text:t,me:true});
  sessionStorage.setItem('kpai_ui_chat',JSON.stringify(saved));
  if(typeof kpaiAppend==='function') kpaiAppend(t,true);
  else if(body) body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div class="kpaiBubble me"><span class="kpaiText">'+t+'</span></div>');
  input.value='';
  if(typing) typing.style.display='block';
  try{
    if(typeof fbAuth === 'undefined' || !fbAuth || !fbAuth.currentUser){
      throw new Error('LOGIN_REQUIRED');
    }
    const idToken=await fbAuth.currentUser.getIdToken();
    const KPAI_ENDPOINT='https://asia-south1-kp-whatsaappclone.cloudfunctions.net/kpaiChat';
    console.log('KPAI: calling', KPAI_ENDPOINT);
    const r=await fetch(KPAI_ENDPOINT,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+idToken},body:JSON.stringify({message:t})});
    let data={}; 
    try{ data=await r.json(); }catch(_){ data={}; }
    console.log('KPAI response status', r.status, data);
    if(!r.ok){
      if(data.error==='RATE_LIMIT') throw new Error('RATE_LIMIT');
      if(data.error==='AUTH_REQUIRED') throw new Error('LOGIN_REQUIRED');
      throw new Error('AI_UNAVAILABLE_'+r.status);
    }
    const answer=String(data.reply||'No response received.');
    let s2=[];
    try{ s2=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('kpai_ui_chat')||'[]'); }catch(e){ s2=[]; }
    s2.push({text:answer,me:false});
    sessionStorage.setItem('kpai_ui_chat',JSON.stringify(s2));
    if(typeof kpaiAppend==='function') kpaiAppend(answer,false);
  }catch(e){
    console.warn('KPAI cloud failed, using local fallback', e);
    let answer='';
    let isLocalFallback = false;
    if(e.message==='RATE_LIMIT') answer='You have reached the KPAI request limit. Please wait a minute and try again.';
    else if(e.message==='LOGIN_REQUIRED' || e.message.startsWith('LOGIN')) answer='Please sign in to KP WhatsApp before using KPAI. If you are signed in, your login session may have expired - please logout and login again.';
    else {
      // Use local fallback instead of showing unavailable
      answer = kpaiLocalReply(t);
      isLocalFallback = true;
    }
    let s3=[];
    try{ s3=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('kpai_ui_chat')||'[]'); }catch(err){ s3=[]; }
    s3.push({text:answer,me:false});
    sessionStorage.setItem('kpai_ui_chat',JSON.stringify(s3));
    if(typeof kpaiAppend==='function') kpaiAppend(answer,false);
    
    if(isLocalFallback){
      // Show small hint about cloud function after local answer
      setTimeout(()=>{
        const hint = 'Note: Cloud AI is offline (function not deployed or API key missing). Showing local help. Check Firebase Console > Functions > Logs for kpaiChat.';
        if(typeof kpaiAppend==='function') kpaiAppend(hint,false);
      }, 800);
    } else {
      if(typeof toast==='function' && !isLocalFallback) toast(answer);
    }
  }finally{
    if(typing) typing.style.display='none';
  }
}

</script>
