  // ===== KPAI API SETTINGS & AGENT FUNCTIONS =====
  function openKPAISettings(){
    const panel=document.getElementById('kpaiSettingsPanel');
    if(!panel) return;
    panel.classList.toggle('show');
    // load saved
    try{
      const key=localStorage.getItem('kpai_api_key')||'';
      const mode=localStorage.getItem('kpai_agent_mode')||'full';
      const imgModel=localStorage.getItem('kpai_image_model')||'pollinations';
      const kInput=document.getElementById('kpaiApiKeyInput');
      const mInput=document.getElementById('kpaiAgentMode');
      const iInput=document.getElementById('kpaiImageModel');
      if(kInput) kInput.value=key;
      if(mInput) mInput.value=mode;
      if(iInput) iInput.value=imgModel;
    }catch(e){}
  }
  window.openKPAISettings=openKPAISettings;
  
  async function saveKPAISettings(){
    try{
      const key=document.getElementById('kpaiApiKeyInput')?.value.trim()||'';
      const mode=document.getElementById('kpaiAgentMode')?.value||'full';
      const imgModel=document.getElementById('kpaiImageModel')?.value||'pollinations';
      localStorage.setItem('kpai_api_key', key);
      localStorage.setItem('kpai_agent_mode', mode);
      localStorage.setItem('kpai_image_model', imgModel);
      // also save to Firestore user doc if logged in
      if(typeof fbDb!=='undefined' && typeof fbUser!=='undefined' && fbUser?.uid){
        try{
          await fbDb.collection('users').doc(fbUser.uid).set({
            kpaiSettings:{agentMode:mode, imageModel:imgModel, hasApiKey:!!key, updatedAt: firebase.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp()}
          }, {merge:true});
          if(key){
            // Do NOT save raw key to Firestore in plain text for security - only flag
            // If you really want, uncomment below (not recommended)
            // await fbDb.collection('users').doc(fbUser.uid).collection('private').doc('apiKeys').set({gemini:key});
          }
        }catch(err){ console.warn('Could not save to Firestore', err); }
      }
      const panel=document.getElementById('kpaiSettingsPanel');
      if(panel) panel.classList.remove('show');
      if(typeof toast==='function') toast('KPAI settings saved! Mode: '+mode);
      if(typeof kpaiAppend==='function'){
        kpaiAppend('Settings saved. Agent mode: '+mode+'. '+(key?'API key added - image generation enabled.':'Using free image mode.'), false);
      }
    }catch(e){
      console.error('saveKPAISettings', e);
      alert('Could not save: '+e.message);
    }
  }
  window.saveKPAISettings=saveKPAISettings;

  async function generateKPAIImage(prompt){
    const imgModel = localStorage.getItem('kpai_image_model')||'pollinations';
    const apiKey = localStorage.getItem('kpai_api_key')||'';
    const body=document.getElementById('kpaiBody');
    if(!prompt) return;
    if(typeof kpaiAppend==='function') kpaiAppend('Generating image: "'+prompt+'"...', false);
    try{
      let imageUrl='';
      if(imgModel==='pollinations'){
        // Free image generation - Pollinations
        const encoded = encodeURIComponent(prompt);
        imageUrl = `https://image.pollinations.ai/prompt/${encoded}?width=1024&height=1024&nologo=true&model=flux`;
        // Test load
        const bubbleId = 'img_'+Date.now();
        const html = `<div class="kpaiBubble" id="${bubbleId}"><b>KPAI Image</b><br><span class="kpaiText">${prompt}</span><br><img src="${imageUrl}" style="width:100%;border-radius:12px;margin-top:8px;max-width:400px" onload="this.parentElement.scrollIntoView()" onerror="this.parentElement.innerHTML='Image failed to load. Try again.'"><br><small style="color:#6d7a7e">Generated via Pollinations - Free</small><div style="margin-top:6px"><button class="kpaiSuggestion" onclick="window.open('${imageUrl}', '_blank')">Open Full</button> <button class="kpaiSuggestion" onclick="if(typeof shareKPAIMessage==='function'){ const b=document.getElementById('${bubbleId}'); if(b) shareKPAIMessage(b.querySelector('.kpaiShare')||b); }">Share</button></div></div>`;
        if(body) { body.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html); body.scrollTop=body.scrollHeight; }
      } else if(imgModel==='gemini' && apiKey){
        // Gemini image generation via direct API call
        // Note: This uses client-side key (only for personal use)
        const resp = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash-exp-image-generation:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`, {
          method:'POST',
          headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
          body: JSON.stringify({contents:[{parts:[{text: "Generate image: "+prompt}]}]})
        });
        const data = await resp.json();
        console.log('Gemini image resp', data);
        // For now fallback to pollinations if Gemini image not available in your region
        if(!resp.ok) throw new Error('Gemini image error: '+(data.error?.message||resp.status));
        if(typeof kpaiAppend==='function') kpaiAppend('Gemini image API responded, but direct image display needs extra handling. Using Pollinations as fallback for now. Prompt: '+prompt, false);
        // fallback
        generateKPAIImage(prompt); // will use pollinations if fails? avoid loop - directly call pollinations
      } else {
        if(typeof kpaiAppend==='function') kpaiAppend('Please add API key in settings or select Pollinations (free) mode.', false);
      }
    }catch(err){
      console.error('generateKPAIImage', err);
      if(typeof kpaiAppend==='function') kpaiAppend('Image generation failed: '+(err.message||err)+'. Try Pollinations mode (free, no key needed).', false);
    }
  }
  window.generateKPAIImage=generateKPAIImage;

  // Override kpaiPrompt to handle image commands
  const originalKpaiPrompt = window.kpaiPrompt;
  window.kpaiPrompt = function(text){
    if(text && (text.toLowerCase().startsWith('/image') || text.toLowerCase().startsWith('/img') || text.toLowerCase().includes('generate image') || text.toLowerCase().includes('photo உருவாக்கு') || text.toLowerCase().includes('image bana'))){
      let prompt = text.replace('/image','').replace('/img','').replace('generate image','').trim();
      if(!prompt) prompt = text;
      generateKPAIImage(prompt);
      const input=document.getElementById('kpaiInput');
      if(input) input.value='';
      return;
    }
    if(typeof originalKpaiPrompt==='function') originalKpaiPrompt(text);
    else {
      const i=document.getElementById('kpaiInput');
      if(i){ i.value=text; i.focus(); if(typeof sendKPAI==='function') sendKPAI(); }
    }
  };



  function bindKpaiRows(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.kpaiChatRow, .kpaiNav').forEach(row=>{
      if(row.dataset.kpaiBound==='1') return;
      row.dataset.kpaiBound='1';
      // remove inline onclick to avoid double call confusion, we will handle via listener
      row.addEventListener('click',function(e){
        if(e.target.closest('button.kpaiSuggestion, .kpaiComposer button, .kpaiTop button')) return;
        // allow if it's the row itself
        e.preventDefault(); 
        e.stopPropagation(); 
        kpaiOpenFixed();
      },false);
    });
  }
  function boot(){
    bindKpaiRows();
    const list=document.getElementById('chatList');
    if(list && !list.dataset.kpaiObserver){
      list.dataset.kpaiObserver='1';
      new MutationObserver(bindKpaiRows).observe(list,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    }
    const nav=document.querySelector('.nav');
    if(nav && !nav.dataset.kpaiObserver){
      nav.dataset.kpaiObserver='1';
      new MutationObserver(bindKpaiRows).observe(nav,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    }
    // Also fix any existing inline onclick that might be broken due to earlier overwritten function
    document.addEventListener('click', function(e){
      const row = e.target.closest('.kpaiChatRow, .kpaiNav');
      if(row){
        // if already handled by bound listener, this is backup
        if(!row.dataset.kpaiBound){
          kpaiOpenFixed();
        }
      }
    }, true);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot); else boot();
  // also run boot after a short delay to catch late renderChats
  setTimeout(boot, 500);
  setTimeout(boot, 1500);
})();</script>
